Moreover, VAP shows stronger reasoning abilities than previous methods and effectively selects keyframes relevant to questions. These findings highlight the potential of leveraging active perception to improve the frame effectiveness and efficiency of long-form video QA.

</details>


### [73] [Deep neural networks with Fisher vector encoding for medical image classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01667)
*Lucas O. Lyra,Antonio E. Fabris,Joao B. Florindo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将无序编码方法（Fisher Vectors）引入混合CNN+ViT架构，通过限制GMM估计成本的方法，使模型同时适用于小规模和大规模数据集，并在MedMNIST等医学图像分类任务上取得了超越基准的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合CNN+ViT模型已超越纯CNN方法，但尚未充分探索与更精细特征表示（如无序编码方法）的集成；同时，Fisher Vectors在大数据集上的GMM估计计算成本高昂，限制了其应用。

Method: 将Fisher Vectors（一种基于GMM估计的无序编码方法）引入混合CNN+ViT架构；并提出一种限制GMM估计成本增长的方法，以适应大规模数据集。

Result: 在MedMNIST (v2)所有数据集上超越基准结果；在Clean-CC-CCII和ISIC2018上取得与文献相当的结果。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效结合了无序编码与混合CNN+ViT的优势，适用于不同规模的数据集，并在医学图像分类中展现出竞争力。

Abstract: Orderless encoding methods have shown to improve Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) for image classification in the context of limited availability of data. Additionally, hybrid CNN + Vision Transformers (ViT) models have been recently proposed to address CNN locality bias issues. These models outperformed CNN-only approaches. Despite that, the integration of such hybrid models with more elaborated feature representation can be highly beneficial and remains large unexplored in the literature. In this context, we propose the introduction of an orderless encoding method, Fisher Vectors, to hybrid CNN + ViT architectures, aiming at achieving a model suitable for both small and large datasets. Such enconding method relies on estimating a Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM) on image features. In large datasets, computational costs of the GMM estimation is a limiting factor for the application of Fisher Vectors. Thus, we propose a method to limit the growth of GMM estimation costs as we increase the size of the dataset. We explore the feasibility of our method in the context of medical image classification by appling it to MedMNIST (v2), Clean-CC-CCII and ISIC2018. This collection of datasets contains a wide variety of data scales and modalities. We outperform benchmark results in all MedMNIST (v2) datasets and obtain literature-competitive results in Clean-CC-CCII and ISIC2018.

</details>


### [74] [IMPACT-Scribe: Interactive Temporal Action Segmentation with Boundary Scribbles and Query Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01668)
*Qian Yin,Di Wen,Kunyu Peng,David Schneider,Zeyun Zhong,Alexander Jaus,Zdravko Marinov,Jiale Wei,Ruiping Liu,Junwei Zheng,Yufan Chen,Chen Zhang,Lei Qi,Rainer Stiefelhagen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: IMPACT-Scribe是一个利用每一次人工修正来改进未来人机协作的密集标注框架，通过不确定性感知的边界标注监督、局部提议建模、成本感知查询规划、结构化传播和修正驱动适应等模块，在降低人工标注成本的同时提升标注质量和边界精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序性活动视频的密集时间标注对动作理解和具身智能至关重要，但现有标注工具反应式且效率低下——每次修正都被视为孤立的编辑操作，无法复用标注人员的不确定性和模型可靠性信息，导致标注过程劳动密集且成本高昂。

Method: 提出IMPACT-Scribe修正驱动框架，包含五个核心组件：(1) 不确定性感知的边界标注监督；(2) 局部提议建模；(3) 成本感知的查询规划；(4) 结构化传播；(5) 修正驱动自适应。形成从初始标注到人工修正再到模型更新的闭环。

Result: 实验和人工研究表明，该闭环设计在相同标注努力下提升了标注质量，提高了边界精度，并随着时间推移改善了人机交互效果。

Conclusion: 基于修正驱动的闭环标注范式能够有效复用标注过程中的不确定性信息，在降低人工成本的同时提升密集标注的质量和效率，为程序性活动视频标注提供了一种更智能的人机协作方案。

Abstract: Dense temporal annotation of procedural activity videos is vital for action understanding and embodied intelligence but remains labor-intensive due to reactive tools. Each correction is treated as an isolated edit, limiting reuse of information on annotator uncertainty and model reliability. We introduce IMPACT-Scribe, a correction-driven framework for dense labeling that uses each correction to improve future human-machine collaboration. IMPACT-Scribe combines uncertainty-aware boundary scribble supervision, local proposal modeling, cost-aware query planning, structured propagation, and correction-driven adaptation. Experiments and a human study show that this closed-loop design improves labeling quality per effort, enhances boundary accuracy, and fosters better human-machine interaction over time. The code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/BanzQians/IMPACT_AS.

</details>


### [75] [TrajRAG: Retrieving Geometric-Semantic Experience for Zero-Shot Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01700)
*Yiyao Wang,Sixian Zhang,Keming Zhang,Xinhang Song,Songjie Du,Shuqiang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TrajRAG框架，通过检索增强生成技术积累和利用过往导航轨迹的几何-语义经验，提升零样本物体目标导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本ObjectNav方法依赖互联网文本常识而非具身3D经验，且丢弃导航中的情节观测，无法积累终身经验。

Method: 提出拓扑-极坐标轨迹表示紧凑编码空间布局与语义上下文；设计层次化分块结构实现由粗到细的检索；在导航中利用候选前沿生成多轨迹假设查询TrajRAG，指导大模型进行航点选择。

Result: 在MP3D、HM3D-v1和HM3D-v2数据集上，TrajRAG有效检索相关几何-语义经验，提升了零样本ObjectNav性能。

Conclusion: TrajRAG通过检索增强生成将过往导航经验结构化积累并用于指导当前决策，实现了终身导航经验的持续累积，有效改进了零样本物体目标导航。

Abstract: Existing zero-shot Object Goal Navigation (ObjectNav) methods often exploit commonsense knowledge from large language or vision-language models to guide navigation. However, such knowledge arises from internet-scale text rather than embodied 3D experience, and episodic observations collected during navigation are typically discarded, preventing the accumulation of lifelong experience. To this end, we propose Trajectory RAG (TrajRAG), a retrieval-augmented generation framework that enhances large-model reasoning by retrieving geometric-semantic experiences. TrajRAG incrementally accumulates episodic observations from past navigation episodes. To structure these observations, we propose a topological-polar (topo-polar) trajectory representation that compactly encodes spatial layouts and semantic contexts, effectively removing redundancies in raw episodic observations. A hierarchical chunking structure further organizes similar topo-polar trajectories into unified summaries, enabling coarse-to-fine retrieval. During navigation, candidate frontiers generate multiple trajectory hypotheses that query TrajRAG for similar past trajectories, guiding large-model reasoning for waypoint selection. New experiences are continually consolidated into TrajRAG, enabling the accumulation of lifelong navigation experience. Experiments on MP3D, HM3D-v1, and HM3D-v2 show that TrajRAG effectively retrieves relevant geometric-semantic experiences and improves zero-shot ObjectNav performance.

</details>


### [76] [Exploring Entropy-based Active Learning for Fair Brain Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01706)
*Ghazal Danaee,Mélanie Gaillochet,Christian Desrosiers,Herve Lombaert,Sylvain Bouix*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种公平感知的主动学习框架，通过加权熵选择策略在医学图像分割中减少不同群体间的性能差异，在合成脑MRI数据上相比标准方法减少了75%-86%的群体间性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准基于不确定性的主动学习方法在医学图像分割中只关注最大化整体性能指标，忽视了不同敏感属性群体间的性能差异或公平性问题。尽管公平主动学习已在分类任务中有所探索，但在医学图像分割领域仍是空白。

Method: 提出一个公平感知的主动学习框架，包含：1）加权熵选择策略，基于当前标记集上各组别的性能估计来调节不确定性；2）掩码缩放熵，将熵计算限制在感兴趣区域内以解耦真实认知不确定性与解剖体积变异。在合成T1加权脑MRI上评估，使用3D U-Net分割左尾状核。

Result: 该方法相比随机采样和标准不确定性采样显著降低了群体间的性能差距。在强偏置和弱偏置设置下，与标准熵相比，最终预算时分别减少了75%和86%的差异度量，并持续实现了最高的公平缩放性能。

Conclusion: 本研究是首批将公平主动学习引入医学图像分割的工作之一，提供了一种在资源受限环境下训练更公平模型的高效策略。

Abstract: Active learning (AL) has emerged as a crucial strategy for reducing the prohibitive costs associated with medical image segmentation. However, standard uncertainty-based AL methods typically focus on maximizing performance metrics, ignoring performance disparities or fairness across groups with sensitive attributes. While fair active learning has been explored in classification tasks, its intersection with medical image segmentation remains unaddressed. In this work, we introduced a fairness-aware active learning framework with a Weighted Entropy selection strategy that modulates uncertainty based on current group-specific performance estimates on the labeled set. To decouple true epistemic uncertainty from anatomical volume variances, we further utilized a masked, scaled entropy restricted to the region of interest. The framework was evaluated on synthetic T1-weighted brain MRIs with controlled left caudate bias in both strong and weak bias settings. A 3D U-Net was trained to segment the left caudate under several AL strategies, starting from both demographically balanced and strongly imbalanced initial labeled sets. Experiments demonstrated that our method markedly reduces performance disparities between groups compared to random sampling and standard uncertainty sampling. By prioritizing poorly segmented subgroups during the AL cycles, our method consistently achieved the highest equity-scaled performance and reduced the disparity metric by 75% (strong bias) and 86% (weak bias) relative to standard entropy at the final budget. Overall, this work is among the first studies on fair AL for medical image segmentation, offering an efficient strategy to train more equitable models in resource-constrained environments.

</details>


### [77] [Dual-branch Robust Unlearnable Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01718)
*Xianlong Wang,Hangtao Zhang,Wenbo Pan,Ziqi Zhou,Changsong Jiang,Li Zeng,Xiaohua Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为DUNE的双分支不可学习集成扰动优化方法，分别在空间和颜色域优化扰动，结合集成策略增强不可学习性，在CIFAR-10和ImageNet上比12种SOTA方案更鲁棒，平均测试准确率降低14.95%-50.82%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不可学习样本方案由于启发式设计或扰动域范围狭窄，对先进防御手段的鲁棒性有限。

Method: DUNE采用双分支结构分别在空间域和颜色域优化扰动，建立扰动与偏移标签的映射；提出不可学习性增强集成策略，聚合多种预训练模型进行双分支优化。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和ImageNet数据集上，DUNE在7种主流防御下优于12种SOTA方案，平均测试准确率低14.95%-50.82%。

Conclusion: DUNE通过扩展扰动域和集成学习有效提升了不可学习样本的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Unlearnable examples (UEs) aim to compromise model training by injecting imperceptible perturbations to clean samples. However, existing UE schemes exhibit limited robustness against advanced defenses due to their heuristic design or narrowly scoped domain perturbations. To address this, we propose \texttt{DUNE}, a \underline{\textbf{D}}ual-branch \underline{\textbf{UN}}learnable \underline{\textbf{E}}nsemble perturbation optimization approach. Specifically, \texttt{DUNE} separately optimizes perturbations in the spatial and color domains to establish the mapping between perturbations and shift-induced labels. This design extends the perturbation domain to increase noise intensity for improving robustness and drives the models to learn perturbation-oriented features with degraded generalization, thereby achieving unlearnability. To strengthen \texttt{DUNE}'s performance, we further propose an unlearnability-enhancing ensemble strategy that aggregates diverse pre-trained models during the dual-branch optimization. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets CIFAR-10 and ImageNet verify that \texttt{DUNE}'s robustness outperforms 12 SOTA UE schemes under 7 mainstream defenses, yielding a lower average test accuracy of 14.95\% to 50.82\%.

</details>


### [78] [SignVerse-2M: A Two-Million-Clip Pose-Native Universe of 25+ Sign Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01720)
*Sen Fang,Hongbin Zhong,Yanxin Zhang,Dimitris N. Metaxas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SignVerse-2M是一个大规模多语言手语姿态数据集，包含约200万个片段，覆盖超过25种手语。它将原始视频通过DWPose统一预处理为2D姿态序列，支持开放世界的手语识别、翻译和姿态驱动视频生成。论文同时提供了数据构建流程、任务定义和SignDW Transformer基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模手语资源仅提供原始视频-文本对齐级别的监督，且多在实验室环境制作，无法直接支持：（1）开放世界场景下鲁棒的RGB识别；（2）现代姿态驱动的手语视频生成框架。手语领域缺乏一个能与姿态原生范式直接接口、同时面向真实开放场景的数据资源。

Method: 从公开的多语言手语视频资源出发，使用DWPose统一预处理流水线将原始视频转换为2D姿态序列，构建约200万片段、覆盖25+种手语的语料库。保留真实世界视频的录制条件和说话人多样性，同时通过统一姿态表示减少外观变化。提出SignDW Transformer作为基线模型。

Result: 构建了SignVerse-2M数据集（约200万片段，25+种手语），提供了数据构建流水线、任务定义和SignDW Transformer基线，展示了其多语言姿态空间建模的可行性和与现代姿态驱动流水线的兼容性。

Conclusion: SignVerse-2M填补了手语领域缺少可直接接口现代姿态原生范式、面向真实开放场景的数据资源的空白，为开放世界手语识别、翻译和姿态驱动生成提供了统一框架。同时讨论了该资源支持的评估主张和当前局限性。

Abstract: Existing large-scale sign language resources typically provide supervision only at the level of raw video-text alignment and are often produced in laboratory settings. While such resources are important for semantic understanding, they do not directly provide a unified interface for open-world recognition and translation, or for modern pose-driven sign language video generation frameworks: 1. RGB-based pretrained recognition models depend heavily on fixed backgrounds or clothing conditions during recording, and are less robust in open-world settings than style-agnostic pose-processing models. 2. Recent pose-guided image/video generation models mostly use a unified keypoint representation such as DWPose as their control interface. At present, the sign language field still lacks a data resource that can directly interface with this modern pose-native paradigm while also targeting real-world open scenarios. We present SignVerse-2M, a large-scale multilingual pose-native dataset for sign language pose modeling and evaluation. Built from publicly available multilingual sign language video resources, it applies DWPose in a unified preprocessing pipeline to convert raw videos into 2D pose sequences that can be used directly for modeling, resulting in a consolidated corpus of about two million clips covering more than 25 sign languages. Unlike many laboratory datasets, this resource preserves the recording conditions and speaker diversity of real-world videos while reducing appearance variation through a unified pose representation. Toward this goal, we further provide the data construction pipeline, task definitions, and a simple SignDW Transformer baseline, demonstrating the feasibility of this resource for multilingual pose-space modeling and its compatibility with modern pose-driven pipelines, while discussing the evaluation claims it can support as well as its current limitations.

</details>


### [79] [GEASS: Training-Free Caption Steering for Hallucination Mitigation in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01733)
*Zeshang Li,Shuoyang Zhang,Jiashen Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文发现直接嵌入自生成字幕会导致视觉语言模型产生目标幻觉并降低性能，原因是字幕的结构性属性（锚定效应和不对称错误）。为此提出GEASS模块，通过门控机制、熵加权和证据提升策略，在每个查询上动态决定模型应消费多少字幕信息，仅需两次额外前向传播即可稳定提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作将自生成字幕视为统一的正面资源，但作者发现直接嵌入字幕反而会降低模型性能（如Qwen2.5-VL-3B在HallusionBench上下降近10个点），且字幕的有用性是逐查询而非逐语料库的属性，因此需要一种能够动态决定字幕使用程度的机制。

Method: 提出GEASS（Gated Evidence-Aware Selective Steering），一个免训练模块。具体包含三个核心策略：1）通过干净路径的置信度对字幕进行门控；2）根据字幕产生的熵降低程度进行加权；3）当两条路径产生分歧时提高证据门槛。仅需两次额外前向传播即可实现。

Result: 在POPE和HallusionBench两个基准上，对四个不同的VLM进行实验，GEASS相比普通推理和对比解码方法均取得一致性的性能提升。

Conclusion: 字幕的有用性取决于具体查询，需要动态决定字幕的使用程度；GEASS方法通过轻量级的门控和加权机制有效缓解了直接嵌入字幕带来的负面影响。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) excel at grounded reasoning but remain prone to object hallucination. Recent work treats self-generated captions as a uniformly positive resource, yet we find that naively embedding one can degrade rather than help--dropping Qwen2.5-VL-3B accuracy on HallusionBench by nearly 10 points. Two structural properties explain this. First, captions anchor not only the model's final answer but also its reasoning trajectory and lexical choices. Second, caption errors are asymmetric: omissions vastly outnumber fabrications, yet each fabrication carries a much larger per-instance impact. A caption's usefulness is therefore a per-query property, not a per-corpus one. We propose GEASS (Gated Evidence-Aware Selective Steering), a training-free module that decides on each query how much of the caption the model consumes: it gates the caption by the clean path's confidence, weights it by the entropy reduction it produces, and raises the evidence bar when the two pathways disagree. Experiments on POPE and HallusionBench across four VLMs show that GEASS consistently improves over vanilla inference and contrastive decoding, with only two extra forward passes per query.

</details>


### [80] [Multi-Scale Gaussian-Language Map for Zero-shot Embodied Navigation and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01736)
*Sixian Zhang,Yiyao Wang,Xinhang Song,Keming Zhang,Zijian Xu,Shuqiang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多尺度高斯-语言地图（GLMap），通过显式几何、多尺度语义（实例+区域）和双模态接口（自然语言描述+3D高斯表示），实现无需梯度优化的高效增量建图，在ObjectNav、InstNav和SQA任务上零样本提升目标导航与上下文推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义建图方法在显式几何与多尺度语义之间存在权衡，且缺乏与大模型的天然接口，需要额外训练特征投影进行语义对齐。

Method: 提出GLMap，包含三种关键设计：(1) 显式几何；(2) 覆盖实例和区域概念的多尺度语义；(3) 每个语义单元联合存储自然语言描述和3D高斯表示的双模态接口。同时提出高斯估计器，从稠密点云解析推导高斯参数，无需基于梯度的优化。

Result: 在ObjectNav、InstNav和SQA任务上，GLMap有效增强了目标导航和上下文推理，并能以零样本方式与基于大模型的方法兼容。

Conclusion: GLMap通过结合3D高斯表示与自然语言描述，实现了显式几何与多尺度语义的统一建图，为大模型提供了原生接口，提升了具身导航与推理的性能。

Abstract: Understanding the geometric and semantic structure of environments is essential for embodied navigation and reasoning. Existing semantic mapping methods trade off between explicit geometry and multi-scale semantics, and lack a native interface for large models, thus requiring additional training of feature projection for semantic alignment. To this end, we propose the multi-scale Gaussian-Language Map (GLMap), which introduces three key designs: (1) explicit geometry, (2) multi-scale semantics covering both instance and region concepts, and (3) a dual-modality interface where each semantic unit jointly stores a natural language description and a 3D Gaussian representation. The 3D Gaussians enable compact storage and fast rendering of task-relevant images via Gaussian splatting. To enable efficient incremental construction, we further propose a Gaussian Estimator that analytically derives Gaussian parameters from dense point clouds without gradient-based optimization. Experiments on ObjectNav, InstNav, and SQA tasks show that GLMap effectively enhances target navigation and contextual reasoning, while remaining compatible with large-model-based methods in a zero-shot manner. The code is available at https://github.com/sx-zhang/GLMap.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [81] [LiDAR for Rehabilitation: A Comprehensive Survey of Applications, AI Techniques, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00879)
*Soumia Siyoucef,Najmeddine Dhieb,Hakim Ghazzai,Eleonora Guanziroli,Franco Molteni,Gianluca Setti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了2019-2025年间LiDAR在康复、伤后护理和医院环境中的应用，涵盖3D体表扫描、步态分析、机器人辅助康复、实时监测与安全导航、活动识别等方面，并分析了基于学习的处理技术，是首篇专注于LiDAR康复应用的全面综述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统康复监测方法存在隐私问题（摄像头）或佩戴不适、易出错（可穿戴传感器）等缺陷，LiDAR作为一种非接触式传感器具有独特优势，但目前缺乏针对LiDAR在康复领域应用的系统性综述，需要填补这一空白。

Method: 对2019-2025年间发表的研究进行系统文献综述，从多个应用维度（3D体表扫描与步态分析、机器人系统集成、实时监测与环境扫描、活动与位置识别）分类梳理，并结合统计分析和学习-based处理方法讨论。

Result: 识别了LiDAR在康复中的主要应用方向、当前趋势、研究空白和未来机遇，并总结了基于AI的处理技术现状。

Conclusion: 这是首篇专门针对LiDAR在康复应用中的全面综述，提供了当前方法、AI处理技术和开放挑战的概览，为后续研究提供了系统参考。

Abstract: Rehabilitation aims to help patients with limited mobility regain their physical abilities through targeted movements, exercises, stimulation, and other therapeutic methods. Recent advances in technology have introduced sensor-based systems into rehabilitation and clinical practices, enabling real-time monitoring and providing accurate feedback on movement accuracy. Among these sensors, LiDAR has demonstrated strong potential, offering key advantages over conventional techniques such as camera-based systems, which raise privacy concerns, and wearable sensors, which can be uncomfortable and prone to errors. In this work, we review the applications of LiDAR in rehabilitation, post-injury care, and hospital environments, focusing on studies published between 2019 and 2025. Studies across several areas have been explored: 3D body scanning and gait analysis with standalone LiDAR, LiDAR mounted on robotic systems for rehabilitation, real-time monitoring and environment scanning for safe navigation, and activity and position recognition. We also analyze processing techniques, particularly learning-based approaches, and support the discussion with statistical analysis, highlighting trends, gaps, and future research opportunities. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first comprehensive survey dedicated to LiDAR for rehabilitation applications, providing an overview of current methods, AI-based processing techniques, and open challenges.

</details>


### [82] [ARIS: Agentic and Relationship Intelligence System for Social Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00943)
*Stavya Datta,Fucai Ke,Leimin Tian,Hamid Rezatofighi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ARIS，一个融合多模态推理、基于图谱的社会世界模型和检索增强生成的模块化智能体框架，用于社交机器人。在Pepper机器人上的用户研究（N=23）表明，ARIS在感知智能、生命力、拟人化和可爱度上显著优于大语言模型基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型虽推动了社交机器人的发展，但在多轮对话、社会关系推理和上下文接地方面仍存在不足，缺乏能够长期维持社交互动的系统。

Method: ARIS框架包含三大核心组件：(1) 社会世界模型——通过知识图谱显式建模和更新用户间的社交关系，支持跨轮次的社会推理和用户重识别；(2) 基于RAG的对话流水线——在对话历史扩展至数千轮时仍能保持低延迟和响应相关性；(3) 模块化智能体架构——通过结构化API协调语音、视觉和物理动作。

Result: 在Pepper机器人上进行的中介对话用户研究（N=23）显示，ARIS在感知智能、生命力、拟人化和可爱度四个维度上均显著优于大语言模型基线。

Conclusion: ARIS通过融合社会世界模型、RAG流水线和模块化智能体架构，有效提升了社交机器人在多轮对话中的社会推理能力和用户感知体验，系统将开源发布。

Abstract: Foundational models have advanced social robotics, enabling richer perception and communicative interaction with users. However, current systems still struggle with multi-turn engagement, social-relationship reasoning, and contextually grounded dialogue at scale. We present ARIS (Agentic and Relationship Intelligence System), an agentic AI framework that unifies multimodal reasoning, a graph-based Social World Model, and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) within a single modular architecture for social robots. We evaluate ARIS with the Pepper robot in a robot-mediated dyadic conversational setting, comparing it against a large language model baseline. A user study (N=23) shows that ARIS yields significantly higher perceived intelligence, animacy, anthropomorphism, and likeability. Our contributions are threefold: (1)~a Social World Model that explicitly maps and updates social relationships between users through a knowledge graph, enabling social reasoning and re-identification across encounters; (2)~an efficient RAG-based conversational pipeline that maintains bounded latency as dialogue histories grow to thousands of exchanges while preserving response relevance; and (3)~system integration and empirical validation of these components within a modular agentic architecture that coordinates speech, vision, and physical action through structured APIs. The implementation of ARIS will be released as open source upon publication.

</details>


### [83] [Ablation Study of Multimodal Perception, Language Grounding, and Control for Human-Robot Interaction in an Object Detection and Grasping Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00963)
*Zi Tian,Guanting Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过控制消融实验，系统评估了多模态人机交互系统中三个关键模块（大语言模型、感知系统、控制器）对端到端性能的影响，明确了各组件对执行时间和成功率的贡献差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在隔离分析多模态人机交互系统中三个关键模块（LLM、感知系统、控制器）的独立贡献，而非重新设计整个流水线，从而厘清各组件对端到端性能的影响。

Method: 采用两阶段实验设计：第一阶段分别比较3种语言模型、5种感知配置和3种控制器；第二阶段对最优候选进行析因实验，评估最佳组合的端到端性能。

Result: 明确了不同模块选择主要影响执行时间还是成功率，并指出了未来系统改进中最具工程收益的方向。

Conclusion: 通过系统消融实验揭示了各模块的性能瓶颈，为后续多模态交互系统的工程优化提供了量化依据和优先级参考。

Abstract: This manuscript extends our previous multimodal human-robot interaction system by introducing a controlled ablation study of the three modules that most strongly influence end-to-end performance: the large language model used for action extraction, the perception system used for visual grounding, and the controller used for motion execution. The goal is not to redesign the full pipeline, but to isolate the contribution of each component under a common experimental protocol and then evaluate the best combinations end-to-end. We therefore compare three language models, five perception configurations, and three controllers, followed by a second-stage factorial study over the best candidates. The resulting analysis is intended to clarify which choices primarily affect execution time, which primarily affect success rate, and where the largest engineering gains are likely to come from in future revisions of the system.

</details>


### [84] [Value Functions for Temporal Logic: Optimal Policies and Safety Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01051)
*Oswin So,William Sharpless,Sylvia Herbert,Chuchu Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了无限时域无折扣设置下，针对时序逻辑规范（尤其是嵌套Until、Globally和Globally-Until）的值函数最优性与策略最优性之间的微妙关系。发现贪心最大化Q函数可能导致策略无限推迟任务完成（路径问题），为此提出基于状态历史的非马尔可夫策略来避免该问题，并证明了其在定量鲁棒性评分下的最优性。同时展示了Q函数可作为复杂时序逻辑规范的安全滤波器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无限时域无折扣环境中，针对更复杂的时序逻辑任务（如嵌套Until、Globally等），标准Bellman方程的值最优性并不保证策略最优性——贪心最大化Q函数可能导致策略无限推迟任务完成，现有方法未能解决这一病理现象。

Method: 基于将时序逻辑值函数分解为若干组成值函数的图结构这一近期成果，构建基于状态历史的非马尔可夫策略，并证明其在定量鲁棒性评分下的最优性；同时扩展Q函数作为安全滤波器的应用范围。

Result: 非马尔可夫策略成功避免了贪心策略导致的无限推迟任务完成的病理问题，并证明了其对嵌套Until、Globally和Globally-Until规范的最优性；Q函数安全滤波器从简单的Avoid/Reach-Avoid任务推广到了更复杂的时序逻辑规范。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了无限时域无折扣设置下值函数最优与策略最优的不一致性，并提供了理论保证的解决方案，为复杂时序逻辑任务下的最优策略合成提供了新思路。

Abstract: While Bellman equations for basic reach, avoid, and reach-avoid problems are well studied, the relationship between value optimality and policy optimality becomes subtle in the undiscounted infinite-horizon setting, particularly for more complicated tasks. Greedily maximizing the Q-function can produce policies that indefinitely defer task completion for reach-avoid problems, or equivalently, Until specifications, even when the value function is optimal. Building upon recent results decomposing the value function for temporal logic (TL) into a graph of constituent value functions, we construct non-Markovian policies based on state history that avoid this pathology and prove their optimality with respect to the quantitative robustness score for nested Until, Globally, and Globally-Until specifications. We further show how the Q function can serve as a safety filter for complex TL specifications, extending prior results beyond simple avoid or reach-avoid tasks.

</details>


### [85] [Online Safety Filter for Deformable Object Manipulation with Horizon Agnostic Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01069)
*Jiaxing Li,Hanjiang Hu,Zhuoyuan Wang,Yorie Nakahira,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种约束驱动的在线安全滤波器，用于可变形物体（如流体）的机器人操作。该方法结合了水平无关的神经算子（学习偏微分方程的边界输入输出映射）和边界控制障碍函数（通过轻量级二次规划实时认证安全性），实现了对任意标称控制策略的最小干预式安全约束。在FluidLab流体操作任务中，该方法相比基于奖励塑形的基线策略，安全轨迹率提升最高22%，且到达安全集的步数更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的可变形物体（流体、布料、软体）操作控制方法通过奖励塑形间接编码安全性，无法保证部署时的约束满足，存在安全隐患。

Method: 提出约束驱动的在线安全滤波器，包含两个核心组件：1) 水平无关的神经算子，学习PDE动力学的边界输入输出映射，可跨可变展开长度泛化；2) 边界控制障碍函数，通过轻量级二次规划在任务相关输出层面认证安全性，约束形式为边界输入速率的仿射关系，支持实时在线滤波。

Result: 在FluidLab流体操作任务中，安全滤波器将安全轨迹率提升最高22%，同时减少到达安全集所需的步数。

Conclusion: 约束驱动的安全执行比奖励塑形方法更可靠、更高效，为可变形物体操作提供了一种实时的、有保障的任务级安全约束满足方案。

Abstract: Safety critical control of robotic manipulation tasks involving deformable media such as fluids, cloth, and soft objects remains challenging because existing learning based approaches encode safety indirectly through reward shaping, which provides no guarantee of constraint satisfaction at deployment. We present a constraint driven online safety filter for deformable object manipulation that enforces explicit task level safety constraints in real time by minimally modifying any nominal control policy. Our approach combines two key components: a horizon agnostic neural operator that learns the boundary input output mapping of the underlying PDE dynamics and generalizes across variable rollout lengths without retraining, and a boundary control barrier function that certifies safety at the task relevant output level via a lightweight quadratic program. The resulting safety constraint is affine in the boundary input rate, enabling real time online filtering. We evaluate the proposed method on fluid manipulation tasks in FluidLab, where the filter improves safe trajectory rates by up to 22% over unfiltered base policies while also reducing the number of steps required to reach the safe set, demonstrating that constraint driven safety enforcement is both more reliable and more efficient than reward shaping approaches.

</details>


### [86] [Sentinel-VLA: A Metacognitive VLA Model with Active Status Monitoring for Dynamic Reasoning and Error Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01191)
*Wenhao Li,Xiu Su,Yichao Cao,Hongyan Xu,Xiaobo Xia,Shan You,Yi Chen,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Sentinel-VLA，一种带有主动监控模块的元认知VLA模型，通过按需推理和自进化持续学习算法，在44个任务、260万条轨迹上训练，相比SOTA模型PI0任务成功率提升超30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型存在推理能力有限、缺乏状态监控、难以自我纠正等关键挑战，亟需一种能够主动监控执行状态并按需进行动态推理和错误恢复的模型。

Method: 提出Sentinel-VLA模型：1) 设计主动"哨兵"模块实时监控执行状态，仅在初始规划或检测到错误时触发动态推理；2) 提出自进化持续学习(SECL)算法，使模型自主识别能力边界并自动收集扩展数据；3) 提出正交持续适配器(OC-Adapter)，将参数更新约束在正交空间以防止灾难性遗忘；4) 设计全自动数据生成和标注流水线，覆盖44个任务、260万条轨迹。

Result: 真实世界实验表明，Sentinel-VLA相比当前最佳模型PI0，任务成功率提升超过30%。

Conclusion: Sentinel-VLA通过元认知架构和持续学习机制，有效解决了VLA模型的推理、监控和自我纠正问题，并将在代码、权重和数据生成流程上开源。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have advanced the field of embodied manipulation by harnessing broad world knowledge and strong generalization. However, current VLA models still face several key challenges, including limited reasoning capability, lack of status monitoring, and difficulty in self-correction. In this paper, we introduce \textbf{Sentinel-VLA}, a metacognitive VLA model equipped with an active ``sentinel'' module to monitor real-time execution status. Only when necessary, such as during initial planning or upon detecting an error, the model triggers a dynamic reasoning or formulate error recovery solutions. This on-demand reasoning mechanism ensures robust decision-making while minimizing computational overhead. Notably, all training data (spanning 44 tasks and over 2.6 million transitions) is automatically generated and annotated through our designed pipeline. We also propose the Self-Evolving Continual Learning (SECL) algorithm, which allows Sentinel-VLA to identify its capability boundaries and automatically collect data for expansion, paired with Orthogonal Continual Adapter (OC-Adapter) to constrain parameter updates to an orthogonal space, thereby preventing catastrophic forgetting. Real-world experiments demonstrate that Sentinel-VLA boosts the task success rate by over 30\% compared to the SOTA model, PI0. We will open-source all the code, weights, and data generation pipeline.

</details>


### [87] [VLA-ATTC: Adaptive Test-Time Compute for VLA Models with Relative Action Critic Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01194)
*Wenhao Li,Xiu Su,Dan Niu,Yichao Cao,Hongyan Xu,Zhe Qu,Lei Fan,Shan You,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA-ATTC框架通过不确定性驱动的“认知离合器”和相对动作评判器（RAC），为VLA模型赋予自适应测试时计算能力，在LIBERO-LONG上使PI0.5失败率降低50%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型依赖快速直觉决策，在复杂/模糊场景下缺乏深思熟虑，容易产生次优甚至灾难性动作。

Method: 提出VLA-ATTC框架：1）基于不确定性的“认知离合器”动态切换反射执行与TTC深思阶段；2）相对动作评判器（RAC）通过成对比较从候选动作中识别最优动作，避免不稳定的绝对值估计；3）高效采样策略分摊计算成本；4）自动化数据管道无需人工标注即可构建偏好对。

Result: 在LIBERO-LONG基准上，将SOTA模型PI0.5的失败率降低超过50%。

Conclusion: VLA-ATTC通过自适应测试时计算有效提升了VLA模型在复杂场景下的决策质量，开源代码和权重将促进该方向研究。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated remarkable capabilities and generalization in embodied manipulation. However, their decision-making relies on a fast, instinctive process that lacks deliberation. This strategy often leads to suboptimal or catastrophic actions when facing complex or ambiguous scenarios that require greater consideration. In this paper, we introduce \textbf{VLA-ATTC}, a framework that endows VLA models with adaptive test-time compute (TTC). VLA-ATTC employs an uncertainty-based ``cognitive clutch'' to dynamically transition from reflexive execution to a TTC deliberation phase when necessary. During TTC phase, a novel \textbf{Relative Action Critic} (RAC) model identifies the optimal action from generated candidates via pairwise comparisons. This relative mechanism replaces unstable absolute value estimation, significantly simplifying the learning objective. Furthermore, we introduce an efficient sampling strategy to amortize computational costs and an automated data pipeline that curates preference pairs without manual annotation. On the LIBERO-LONG benchmark, VLA-ATTC reduces the failure rate of the SOTA model PI0.5 by over 50\%. We will open-source all the code and weights.

</details>


### [88] [TAIL-Safe: Task-Agnostic Safety Monitoring for Imitation Learning Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01195)
*Riad Ahmed,Momotaz Begum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TAIL-Safe 提出了一种基于 Lipschitz 连续 Q 值函数的框架，用于识别模仿学习策略的安全集，并通过 Nagumo 定理的恢复机制将策略引导回安全区域，实验证明该方法能显著提升流匹配策略在扰动下的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模仿学习算法（如流匹配和扩散策略）在执行复杂操作任务时表现出色，但由于对初始条件的极端敏感性和不可约的近似误差，即使在训练分布内也常失败，导致部署时存在安全隐患。因此需要让策略能够判断自身是否能够安全执行任务，并具备从危险状态恢复的能力。

Method: 提出 Lipschitz 连续的 Q 值函数，基于三种短期任务无关准则（可见性、可识别性、可抓取性）将状态-动作对映射为长期安全得分；该函数的零上水平集刻画了经验控制不变集；利用 Nagumo 定理的梯度上升机制在策略动作超出安全集时进行恢复；通过高斯泼溅构建高保真数字孪生系统收集失败数据，避免物理硬件风险。

Result: 在 Franka Emika 机器人上的实验表明，流匹配策略在运行时扰动下原本会失败，但在 TAIL-Safe 引导下能够实现一致的任务成功。

Conclusion: TAIL-Safe 为模仿学习策略的安全部署提供了一种理论严谨且实践有效的方法，通过安全集识别与恢复机制显著提升了策略在扰动环境下的鲁棒性和安全性。

Abstract: Recent imitation learning (IL) algorithms such as flow-matching and diffusion policies demonstrate remarkable performance in learning complex manipulation tasks. However, these policies often fail even when operating within their training distribution due to extreme sensitivity to initial conditions and irreducible approximation errors that lead to compounding drift. This makes it unsafe to deploy IL policies in the field where out-of-distribution scenarios are prevalent. A prerequisite for safe deployment is enabling the policy to determine whether it can execute a task the way it was learned from demonstrations. This paper presents TAIL-Safe, a principled approach to identify, for a trained IL policy, a safe set from where the policy empirically succeeds in completing the learned task. We propose a Lipschitz-continuous Q-value function that maps state-action pairs to a long-term safety score based on three short-term task-agnostic criteria: visibility, recognizability, and graspability. The zero-superlevel set of this function characterizes an empirical control invariant set over state-action pairs. When the nominal policy proposes an action outside this set, we apply a recovery mechanism inspired by Nagumo's theorem that uses gradient ascent to the Q-function to steer the policy back to safety. To learn this Q-function, we construct a high-fidelity digital twin using Gaussian Splatting that enables systematic collection of failure data without risk to physical hardware. Experiments with a Franka Emika robot demonstrate that flow-matching policies, which fail under run-time perturbations, achieve consistent task success when guided by the proposed TAIL-Safe.

</details>


### [89] [To Do or Not to Do: Ensuring the Safety of Visuomotor Policies Learned from Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01201)
*Riad Ahmed,Moniruzzaman Akash,Momotaz Begum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"执行保证"的策略无关安全度量方法，利用视图合成和Nagumo次切线条件，为IL策略在状态空间特定区域内提供最大任务成功保证，并通过Franka机器人实验验证了该方法在安全性与性能之间的平衡效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模仿学习研究以任务成功率作为主要性能指标，忽视了现场机器人应用中安全性的至关重要性。安全与性能之间的权衡在经典控制领域已有深入研究，但在IL领域却严重缺乏探索，且缺乏统一的安全定义，现有理论工作难以扩展到IL驱动的现场机器人。

Method: 提出"执行保证"这一策略无关的安全度量方法；利用视图合成技术识别IL策略在状态空间中的安全区域；基于Nagumo次切线条件（集合不变性理论）证明并操作化该安全区域内的执行保证。

Result: 在Franka机器人上的仿真和真实实验表明，所提出的安全分析方法能使多种IL策略在保证安全的前提下实现最大任务成功；此外，作为安全分析副产物的恢复策略能够提升策略性能，从而缓解安全-性能权衡问题。

Conclusion: 本文为IL策略提供了可证明的安全保证框架，有效弥合了现场机器人部署中安全性与任务性能之间的鸿沟，并展示了如何通过恢复策略进一步优化两者之间的平衡。

Abstract: Task success has historically been the primary measure of policy performance in imitation learning (IL) research. This characteristics strictly limits the ubiquitous applications of IL algorithms in field robotics where safety assurance, in addition to task-success, is of paramount importance. It is often desirable for an IL-powered robot in the field not to roll out a policy, and hence score a poor performance, if the safety is not guaranteed. Although this trade-off between safety and performance is well investigated in classical control literature, policy safety is a heavily underexplored domain in IL research. There is no universal definition of safety in IL. To make things worst, many existing theoretical works on safety is notoriously difficult to extend to IL-powered robots in the field. This paper offers important insights on the safety and performance of IL policies. We propose execution guarantee, a policy-agnostic safety measure that guarantees the maximum task success for a visuomotor IL policy, despite minor run-time changes, from within a specific region in the state space. We leverage recent advances in view synthesis to identify such regions in the state space for an IL policy and explore a fundamental result on set invariance - namely, Nagumo's sub-tangentiality condition - to prove and operationalize execution guarantee from inside that region. Experiments with a Franka robot, both in simulation and real world, demonstrate how the proposed safety analysis allows various IL policies to achieve maximum task success with guarantee. We also demonstrate some interesting results on how a recovery policy - a by-product of the proposed safety analysis - can help to increase the policy performance and thereby mitigating the safety-performance tradeoff in IL.

</details>


### [90] [Dynamics Aware Quadrupedal Locomotion via Intrinsic Dynamics Head](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01227)
*Aman Arora,Nalini Ratha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新颖的四足机器人训练框架，通过并行训练内在动力学（ID）头来学习状态到扭矩的动力学，并利用动力学奖励引导控制策略向更可预测的动态行为收敛，在仿真和真实机器人上均实现了更高效率和平滑性的运动性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人的敏捷、 versatile运动依赖于对底层物理动力学的理解与估计，现有方法缺乏让控制策略显式学习和推理动力学机制的手段。

Method: 提出并行训练框架：在强化学习训练控制策略的同时，训练一个内在动力学（ID）头学习状态到扭矩的映射；基于ID头定义动力学奖励（dynamics reward），激励策略产生更可预测的动态行为；通过调节ID头的训练系数来调控策略中学到的动力学特性。

Result: 仿真实验表明该方法驱动策略收敛到更优解，产生更高效、更平滑的运动；真实机器人实验验证了sim-to-real迁移，扭矩效率提升16.8%，动作频率降低18.6%，机械功率降低12.8%，安全扭矩占用率提升6.4%。

Conclusion: 显式学习内在动力学并利用动力学奖励引导策略训练，是提升四足机器人运动控制效率和稳定性的有效范式，且具有良好的sim-to-real迁移能力。

Abstract: Quadrupedal locomotion plays a critical role in enabling agile, versatile movement across complex terrains. Understanding and estimating the underlying physical dynamics are essential for achieving efficient and stable quadrupedal locomotion. We propose a novel training framework for quadrupedal locomotion that enables the Control Policy to understand and reason about physical dynamics. In simulation, we concurrently train an Intrinsic Dynamics (ID) Head that learns state-to-torque dynamics alongside the Control Policy, and we define a dynamics reward enabled by the ID Head that encourages the Policy toward more predictable dynamical behavior. We also provide a mechanism to tune the learned dynamics in the resulting Policy by controlling the training coefficients of the ID Head. Our simulation experiments show that this mechanism drives convergence to better optima across a wide range of standard quadrupedal locomotion rewards, yielding more efficient and smoother policies. Our real-robot experiments demonstrate sim-to-real transfer of these improvements, with significant gains in torque efficiency (16.8%), action rate (18.6%), and mechanical power (12.8%), while improving safe torque occupancy by 6.4%.

</details>


### [91] [A Principled Approach for Creating High-fidelity Synthetic Demonstrations for Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01232)
*Moniruzzaman Akash,Momotaz Begum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)的演示合成框架，通过将专家轨迹建模为动态运动基元(DMP)并重定向到新目标，在保持专家运动结构的同时实现多样化的任务演示生成，并引入障碍物感知DMP直接在3DGS密度场上进行碰撞避免，相比现有方法在轨迹偏差、碰撞率和下游策略成功率上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS合成管线使用基于采样或轨迹优化的方法生成新运动，这些方法往往偏离专家演示路径，丢弃了对接触密集和形状敏感操作至关重要的精细时空结构，导致演示多样性增加反而损害下游策略学习。

Method: 将专家轨迹建模为DMP并重定向到新目标、物体配置和视点；提出障碍物感知DMP公式，直接在3DGS连续密度场上进行碰撞避免，最小程度扰动专家标称运动。

Result: 在Spot移动机械臂的三个操作任务上，相比基于规划器和优化的合成方法，本方法产生更低偏差和碰撞率的轨迹，并在训练扩散视觉运动策略时获得更高任务成功率。

Conclusion: 演示合成应将专家轨迹作为强先验，通过DMP保留专家运动结构并实现安全多样化合成，统一了逼真渲染和几何推理，无需额外场景表示。

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have enabled visually realistic demonstration generation from a single expert trajectory and a short multi-view scan. However, existing 3DGS-based synthesis pipelines typically generate new motions using sampling-based planners or trajectory optimization, which often deviate substantially from the expert's demonstrated path. While such deviations may be acceptable for tasks insensitive to motion shape, they discard subtle spatial and temporal structure that is critical for contact-rich and shape-sensitive manipulation, causing increased demonstration diversity to harm downstream policy learning. We argue that demonstration synthesis should treat the expert trajectory as a strong prior. Building on this principle, we propose a framework that synthesizes diverse task demonstrations while explicitly preserving expert motion structure. We model the expert trajectory using Dynamic Movement Primitives (DMPs) and retarget it to new goals, object configurations, and viewpoints within a reconstructed 3DGS scene, yielding phase-consistent, shape-preserving motion by construction. To safely realize this expert-preserving diversity in cluttered scenes, we introduce an analytic obstacle-aware DMP formulation that operates directly on the continuous density field induced by the 3DGS representation. This enables collision avoidance while minimally perturbing the nominal expert motion, unifying photorealistic rendering and geometric reasoning without additional scene representations. We evaluate our approach on a Spot mobile manipulator across three manipulation tasks with increasing sensitivity to trajectory fidelity. Compared to planner- and optimization-based synthesis, our method produces trajectories with lower deviation and collision rates and yields higher task success when training diffusion-based visuomotor policies.

</details>


### [92] [Bi-Level Reinforcement Learning Control for an Underactuated Blimp via Center-of-Mass Reconfiguration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01289)
*Xiaorui Wang,Hongwu Wang,Yue Fan,Hao Cheng,Feitian Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双层强化学习框架，用于解决带有重心重构的欠驱动飞艇的目标跟踪控制问题。外层策略负责任务级重心规划，内层策略负责连续推力控制，通过两阶段学习策略实现稳定训练。在仿真和真实实验中，该方法在27个目标的评估集上优于固定重心基线和PID控制器，展现出更高的跟踪精度、鲁棒性和仿真到现实的迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统飞艇采用过驱动设计虽然控制简单，但能效和载荷能力有限。本文旨在探索一种由两个推进器和一个移动内部滑块组成的紧凑架构，以提升能效和载荷能力，同时克服由此带来的欠驱动和非线性耦合挑战。

Method: 提出双层强化学习框架：外层策略在飞行前根据目标确定重心配置，内层策略生成推力命令以跟踪直线参考。采用两阶段学习策略确保稳定训练，并提供了双层过程的收敛性分析。

Result: 在27个目标的评估集上，所提方法在跟踪精度、鲁棒性和仿真到现实迁移方面均优于固定重心基线和PID控制器。

Conclusion: 双层强化学习方法有效解决了欠驱动飞艇的重心重构与跟踪控制耦合问题，实现了更高效、更鲁棒的自主飞行控制。

Abstract: This paper investigates goal-directed tracking control of underactuated blimps with center-of-mass (CoM) reconfiguration. Unlike conventional overactuated blimp designs that rely on redundant actuation for simplified control, this paper focuses on a compact architecture consisting of two thrusters and a movable internal slider, aiming to improve energy efficiency and payload capacity. This hardware-efficient configuration introduces significant underactuation and strong nonlinear coupling between CoM dynamics and vehicle motion. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a bi-level reinforcement learning framework that explicitly decouples task-level CoM planning from continuous thrust control. The outer policy determines a target-dependent CoM configuration prior to flight, while the inner policy generates thrust commands to track straight-line references. To ensure stable learning, this paper introduces a two-stage learning strategy, supported by a convergence analysis of the resulting bi-level process. Extensive simulations and real-world experiments on a 27-goal evaluation set demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms fixed-CoM baselines and PID-based controllers, achieving higher tracking accuracy, enhanced robustness, and reliable sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [93] [Terrain Perception for Agricultural UAVs in Complex Farmland via Rotating mmWave Radar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01340)
*Zhihao Zhan,Le Tao,Shaobin Li,Chenxin Fang,Xingrui Yang,Liang Li,Rui Fan,Yuhang Ming*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于旋转毫米波雷达的低成本地形感知框架，用于农业无人机在复杂农田环境中的地形跟随飞行。通过机械旋转感知设计扩大空间覆盖范围，并设计了适用于稀疏、噪声、部分可观测雷达数据的一致姿态地形重建管线。实地实验表明，该方法在地面分割F1分数达94.42，优于对比方法（90.48），实现了更鲁棒的地形跟随飞行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业无人机在复杂农田环境中进行地形跟随飞行时，现有方法受限于遮挡、复杂地形和环境影响；而现有的无人机载雷达系统依赖固定视场和针对密集LiDAR数据设计的地形提取方法，导致地形估计不完整且不可靠。

Method: 采用机械旋转毫米波雷达感知设计以扩大空间覆盖范围并提升地形可观测性；在此基础上设计了一致姿态地形重建管线，专门处理稀疏、噪声、部分可观测的雷达数据，实现可靠的地面提取和连续地形表面估计。

Result: 实地实验表明，该方法在地面分割任务上F1分数达到94.42，而最接近的对比方法仅为90.48，有效提升了地形覆盖率和估计精度，实现了更鲁棒的地形跟随飞行。

Conclusion: 所提出的旋转毫米波雷达地形感知框架能够在复杂农田环境中实现更完整、可靠的地形估计，显著优于现有方法，为农业无人机地形跟随飞行提供了一种低成本、高鲁棒性的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate terrain perception is essential for terrain-following flight of agricultural unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), yet remains challenging in real-world farmland due to occlusions, complex terrain geometry, and environmental disturbances. Millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar is a promising sensing modality for this task due to its robustness to adverse conditions; however, existing UAV-mounted radar systems rely on fixed field of view (FoV) and terrain extraction methods designed for dense LiDAR data, leading to incomplete and unreliable terrain estimation. To address these limitations, we present a low-cost rotating mmWave radar-enabled terrain perception framework for agricultural UAVs operating in complex farmland environments. Specifically, a mechanically rotating sensing design is introduced to enlarge spatial coverage and improve terrain observability beyond the limitations of fixed-view radar under dynamic low-altitude flight. Building upon this sensing capability, we further design a pose-consistent terrain reconstruction pipeline tailored for sparse, noisy, and partially observable radar data, enabling reliable ground extraction and continuous terrain surface estimation in challenging agricultural scenarios. The complete system is deployed on a real agricultural UAV platform and comprehensively evaluated through extensive field experiments. Experimental results demonstrate improved terrain coverage and estimation accuracy, achieving an F1 score of 94.42 for ground segmentation, while the closest rival only achieves 90.48. Thus, leading to more robust terrain following flight.

</details>


### [94] [Assistance Without Interruption: A Benchmark and LLM-based Framework for Non-Intrusive Human-Robot Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01368)
*Yuedi Zhang,Shuanghao Bai,Wanqi Zhou,Haoran Zhang,Qi Zhang,Zhirong Luan,Badong Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文形式化了非侵入式辅助作为人机交互的独立范式，并建立了仿真基准NIABench，提出了一种结合LLM与评分模型的混合架构，在减少人类操作的同时保持任务有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HRI任务依赖直接命令、显式协商或基于用户习惯的主动干预，缺乏对不中断人类正在进行的多步骤活动的辅助形式化研究。

Method: 提出混合架构：LLM结合评分模型，评分模型先通过语义检索剪枝候选动作集，再由排序器评估人-步与机器人-动作对，实现时序和跨步依赖的推理。

Result: 在NIABench和真实场景实验中，该方法实现了主动且非侵入式的辅助，在减少人类操作的同时保持了任务有效性。

Conclusion: 非侵入式辅助可作为HRI的独立范式，所提方法能有效平衡主动辅助与不干扰人类计划之间的关系。

Abstract: Human-robot interaction (HRI) has long studied how agents and people coordinate to achieve shared goals. In this work, we formalize and benchmark the non-intrusive assistance as an independent paradigm of HRI, where a robot proactively supports a human's ongoing multi-step activities while strictly avoiding interruptions. Unlike conventional HRI tasks that rely on direct commands, explicit negotiation, or proactive interventions based on user habits and history, our task treats the human's plan as the primary process and formulates assistance as a joint decision over when to act and what to do. To systematically evaluate this problem, we establish a simulation benchmark, NIABench, along with new metrics tailored to the non-intrusive assistance task. We further propose a hybrid architecture that integrates an LLM with a scoring model. The scoring model first applies semantic retrieval to prune large candidate action sets, and then a ranker evaluates human-step and robot-action pairs, enabling reasoning over timing and cross-step dependencies. Comprehensive experiments on both NIABench and real-world scenarios demonstrate that our method achieves proactive, non-intrusive assistance that reduces human effort while preserving task effectiveness.

</details>


### [95] [ESARBench: A Benchmark for Agentic UAV Embodied Search and Rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01371)
*Daoxuan Zhang,Ping Chen,Jianyi Zhou,Shuo Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出面向多模态大模型驱动的无人机搜救任务ESAR及首个综合基准ESARBench，基于UE5和AirSim构建高保真仿真环境，包含600个真实救援案例建模的任务和动态变量评估指标，实验揭示了现有方法在空间记忆、空中适应及搜索效率与飞行安全权衡方面的关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机搜救研究主要依赖传统视觉和路径规划方法，缺乏面向具身智能体的统一综合基准，无法有效评估多模态大模型在无人机搜救中的能力。

Method: 1) 提出具身搜救任务ESAR，要求无人机自主探索、识别线索并推理受害者位置；2) 构建ESARBench基准，基于UE5和AirSim从真实GIS数据映射四个高保真开放环境，引入天气、时间、线索随机放置等动态变量；3) 创建600个真实救援案例建模的任务数据集，设计多维度评估指标。

Result: 评估了从传统启发式到先进MLLM基线的多种方法，实验揭示了ESAR任务的关键挑战：空间记忆瓶颈、空中域适应困难、搜索效率与飞行安全之间的权衡。

Conclusion: ESARBench可作为推动具身搜救领域研究的重要资源，帮助识别当前方法的不足并指引未来改进方向。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) has empowered Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) with exceptional capabilities in spatial reasoning, semantic understanding, and complex decision-making, making them inherently suited for UAV Search and Rescue (SAR). However, existing UAV SAR research is dominated by traditional vision and path-planning methods and lacks a comprehensive and unified benchmark for embodied agents. To bridge this gap, we first propose the novel task of \textbf{Embodied Search and Rescue (ESAR)}, which requires aerial agents to autonomously explore complex environments, identify rescue clues, and reason about victim locations to execute informed decision-making. Additionally, we present \textbf{ESARBench}, the first comprehensive benchmark designed to evaluate MLLM-driven UAV agents in highly realistic SAR scenarios. Leveraging Unreal Engine 5 and AirSim, we construct four high-fidelity, large-scale open environments mapped directly from real-world Geographic Information System (GIS) data to ensure photorealistic landscapes. To rigorously simulate actual rescue operations, our benchmark incorporates dynamic variables including weather conditions, time of day, and stochastic clue placement. Furthermore, we create a dataset of 600 tasks modeled after real-world rescue cases and propose a robust set of evaluation metrics. We evaluate diverse baselines, ranging from traditional heuristics to advanced ground and aerial MLLM-based ObjectNav agents. Experimental results highlight the challenges in ESAR, revealing critical bottlenecks in spatial memory, aerial adaptation, and the trade-off between search efficiency and flight safety. We hope ESARBench serves as a valuable resource to advance research on Embodied Search and Rescue domain. Source code and project page: https://4amgodvzx.github.io/ESAR.github.io.

</details>


### [96] [SixthSense: Task-Agnostic Proprioception-Only Whole-Body Wrench Estimation for Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01427)
*Xingzhou Chen,Xiayan Xu,Yan Ning,Jiyu Yu,Yizheng Zhang,Siyi Qian,Lingzhu Xiang,Jiahao Chen,Yuquan Wang,Haodong Zhang,Ling Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SixthSense，一种仅利用本体感知和IMU数据推断人形机器人全身接触时序、位置和 wrench 的任务无关方法，通过条件流匹配实现接触事件流的稀疏估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形机器人缺乏可靠的力交互能力，现有分析框架需要理想化假设和难以获取的测量值，无法在实际场景中有效估计外部wrench。

Method: 采用条件流匹配（conditional flow matching）对本体感知历史进行 tokenize，估计时空稀疏的接触事件流，仅依赖本体感知和IMU数据。

Result: 在站立、行走和全身运动跟踪策略上展现了前所未有的性能，支持碰撞检测、物理人机交互和力反馈遥操作等应用。

Conclusion: SixthSense可作为即插即用的感知模块，有效弥补人形机器人力交互能力的不足。

Abstract: Humanoid robots are entering our physical world at scale, yet as oversized toys--good at singing and dancing, but short on force-interaction capabilities for practical tasks. Bridging this gap necessitates prioritizing reliable contact perception as a fundamental requirement. Estimating external wrenches in humanoids is complicated by floating-base dynamics and indeterminate contact locations. Existing analytical frameworks require idealistic assumptions and hard-to-obtain measurements, which are often unavailable in practice. To bridge this gap, we propose SixthSense, a task-agnostic approach that infers whole-body contact timing, location, and wrenches from proprioception and IMU data alone. To capture the multi-modal dynamics between unstructured contact inputs and the uncertain motion outputs, we employ conditional flow matching to tokenize proprioceptive histories and estimate a spatiotemporally sparse contact-event flow. SixthSense serves as a plug-and-play perception module for applications including collision detection, physical human-robot interaction, and force-feedback teleoperation. Experiments across standing, walking, and whole-body motion-tracking policies showcased unprecedented performance in diverse behaviors.

</details>


### [97] [Evidence-Based Landing Site Selection and Vison-Based Landing for UAVs in Unstructured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01432)
*Sina Sajjadi,Jacopo Panerati,Sina Soleymanpour,Varunkumar Mehta,Farrokh Janabi-Sharifi,Iraj Mantegh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于证据的概率框架，用于无人机在杂乱或非结构化环境中自主着陆。该框架将不确定条件下的决策与视觉伺服执行分离，通过递归累积帧级视觉似然来推断着陆安全性，并结合硬几何约束进行着陆点选择，最终通过ORB特征跟踪和IBVS实现精确着陆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在杂乱或非结构化环境中的自主着陆是安全关键挑战，尤其在传感器不确定性和平台振动等噪声感知条件下，现有方法缺乏对不确定性的显式建模和稳健决策机制。

Method: 构建了基于证据的概率框架：1) 将着陆安全性建模为隐变量，通过递归累积平坦度、坡度、障碍物等帧级视觉似然，生成时间一致的信度图；2) 施加基于最小着陆半径的硬几何约束，拒绝尺寸不足区域；3) 采用约束最大后验估计选择最终着陆点；4) 锁定目标后通过ORB特征跟踪和图像视觉伺服(IBVS)执行精确对齐和下降。

Result: 通过在真实实验室实验和Nvidia Isaac Sim高保真仿真中的验证，该方法在不同域中均展现出一致、谨慎且稳定的着陆行为。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效分离了不确定条件下的决策与执行环节，通过概率推理和几何约束相结合的方式，实现了对瞬态感知误差的鲁棒性，为无人机在复杂环境中的安全自主着陆提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Autonomous landing in cluttered or unstructured environments remains a safety-critical challenge for unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), particularly under noisy perception caused by sensor uncertainty and platform-induced disturbances such as vibration. This paper presents an evidence-based probabilistic framework for autonomous UAV landing that explicitly separates decision-making under uncertainty from execution via visual servoing. Landing safety is modeled as a latent variable and inferred through recursive accumulation of frame-wise visual likelihoods derived from flatness, slope, and obstacle cues, yielding a temporally consistent belief map that is robust to transient perception errors. Physical feasibility is enforced through a hard geometric constraint based on the minimum required landing radius of the UAV, ensuring that undersized but visually appealing regions are rejected. The final landing site is selected using constrained maximum a posteriori estimation. Once selected, the UAV locks onto the target region using ORB feature tracking and performs precise alignment and descent via image-based visual servoing (IBVS). The proposed approach is validated through both real-world laboratory experiments and high-fidelity simulations in Nvidia Isaac Sim, demonstrating consistent, cautious, and stable landing behavior across domains.

</details>


### [98] [High-Speed, Scalable Sensor Readout for Dexterous Robotic Hands via Shift-Register Multiplexing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01434)
*Jaehoon Kim,Lazaros Christoforidis,Michalis Papadakis,Victor Kartsch,Robert K. Katzschmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于移位寄存器原理的模拟传感器串行读取架构，仅需三根信号线即可扩展，在欠驱动机械手上实现了20个传感器通道1 kHz全扫描率，关节传感器斜率绝对百分比误差最大0.446%，触觉传感器力估计RMSE为0.125 N，五类接触位置分类准确率93.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧手传感器读出架构在传感器数量、布线复杂度和采样带宽之间存在权衡，缺乏可扩展的高速多模态传感方案。

Method: 采用串行输入并行输出（SIPO）移位寄存器原理，设计可扩展的模拟传感器读出架构，仅需三条信号线连接传感器模块，支持异构模拟输出传感器的集成与可配置采样。

Result: 在腱驱动机器人手上集成16个关节传感器模块和1个四通道触觉传感器模块，实现20个通道1 kHz全扫描（稳定至1.5 kHz）；关节传感器最大斜率APE 0.446%，角度估计误差低于1度；LSTM触觉模型力估计RMSE 0.125 N，五类接触分类准确率93.4%，支持1 kHz实时推理。

Conclusion: 该架构为全传感器化灵巧手提供了可行的可扩展路径，在交互中关节传感器比基于电机的估计更准确，触觉传感器能实现响应式力估计。

Abstract: Dexterous robotic hands require high-speed multimodal sensing across many degrees of freedom, yet existing readout architectures often impose trade-offs between sensor count, wiring complexity, and sampling bandwidth. This paper presents a scalable analog sensor readout architecture based on a serial-in parallel-out (SIPO) shift-register principle. The proposed architecture supports versatile integration of heterogeneous analog-output sensors, scalable expansion using only three signal lines between sensor modules, and fast, configurable sampling. We validate the approach on a tendon-driven robotic hand integrating 16 joint sensor modules and one four-channel tactile sensor module, enabling acquisition of 20 sensor channels at a full-scan rate of 1 kHz, with stable operation up to 1.5 kHz. Joint sensor characterization showed a maximum slope absolute percentage error (APE) of 0.446% and sub-degree estimation error, indicating that the proposed readout system does not significantly degrade sensing performance. For tactile sensing, LSTM-based models achieved an RMSE of 0.125 N for force estimation and 93.4% accuracy for five-class contact-location classification, and were deployed for real-time inference at 1 kHz. System-level experiments showed that the joint sensors provide more accurate feedback than motor-based estimation during interaction, while the tactile sensor enables responsive force estimation in contact. The proposed architecture offers a practical path toward fully sensorized robotic hands for dexterous manipulation.

</details>


### [99] [Decompose and Recompose: Reasoning New Skills from Existing Abilities for Cross-Task Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01448)
*Xitie Zhang,Aming Wu,Yahong Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Decompose and Recompose框架，通过原子技能-动作对作为中间表示，将 demonstrations 分解为可解释的技能-动作对齐，再通过组合推理重组技能以解决未见任务，实现零样本跨任务泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于上下文学习的方法仅提供低级连续动作序列作为上下文，无法捕获可组合的技能知识，导致模型退化为浅层轨迹模仿，缺乏真正的跨任务泛化能力。

Method: 1) 将演示分解为原子技能-动作对作为中间表示；2) 通过视觉语义检索与规划智能体的技能序列构建任务自适应动态演示库；3) 结合覆盖感知静态库补充缺失技能模式；4) 通过组合推理实现技能组合与执行顺序的显式推理。

Result: 在AGNOSTOS基准测试和真实环境中验证了零样本跨任务泛化能力。

Conclusion: 技能-动作对作为中间表示能够有效提取可迁移的技能知识，通过组合推理框架实现跨任务泛化，优于仅依赖低级动作序列的方法。

Abstract: Cross-task generalization is a core challenge in open-world robotic manipulation, and the key lies in extracting transferable manipulation knowledge from seen tasks. Recent in-context learning approaches leverage seen task demonstrations to generate actions for unseen tasks without parameter updates. However, existing methods provide only low-level continuous action sequences as context, failing to capture composable skill knowledge and causing models to degenerate into superficial trajectory imitation. We propose Decompose and Recompose, a skill reasoning framework using atomic skill-action pairs as intermediate representations. Our approach decomposes seen demonstrations into interpretable skill--action alignments, enabling the model to recompose these skills for unseen tasks through compositional reasoning. Specifically, we construct a task-adaptive dynamic demonstration library via visual-semantic retrieval combined with skill sequences from a planning agent, complemented by a coverage-aware static library to fill missing skill patterns. Together, these yield skill-comprehensive demonstrations that explicitly elicit compositional reasoning for skill composition and execution ordering. Experiments on the AGNOSTOS benchmark and real-world environments validate our method's zero-shot cross-task generalization capability.

</details>


### [100] [LLM-Foraging: Large Language Models for Decentralized Swarm Robot Foraging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01461)
*Peihan Li,Joanna Gutierrez,Fabian Hernandez,Qi Lu,Lifeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LLM-Foraging，一种用大语言模型作为战术决策模块的分布式群集觅食控制器，无需训练即可跨不同配置迁移，在多种团队规模、场地大小和资源分布下均优于GA调优的CPFA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统群集觅食算法（如CPFA）依赖遗传算法或强化学习离线调参，所得策略与特定配置（团队规模、场地大小、资源分布）紧密耦合，部署条件变化时性能下降且重训练成本高。

Method: 在CPFA状态机中嵌入LLM作为战术决策器，在三个决策点（存放后、到达中心区、搜索饥饿）由LLM基于本地可观测状态输出行动指令；每个机器人独立运行LLM客户端，底层运动与感知模块保持不变。

Result: 在Gazebo仿真中，使用TurtleBot3在36种配置（团队规模4-10、场地6x6至10x10米、三种资源分布）下评估，LLM-Foraging资源采集量全面优于GA调优的CPFA基线，且一致性更强。

Conclusion: LLM作为通用决策策略，无需训练即可部署并跨配置迁移，解决了传统方法单配置调参无法泛化的问题。

Abstract: Swarm foraging algorithms, such as the central-place foraging algorithm (CPFA), typically rely on offline parameter optimization using genetic algorithms (GA) or reinforcement learning, yielding policies tightly coupled to a specific combination of team size, arena size, and resource distribution. When deployment conditions change, performance degrades, and retraining is computationally expensive. We propose LLM-Foraging, a decentralized swarm controller that augments the CPFA state machine with a large language model (LLM) tactical decision-maker at three structured decision points, namely post-deposit, central-zone arrival, and search starvation. Each robot runs its own LLM client and queries it using only locally observable state, while the existing CPFA motion and sensing stack executes the selected action. Because the LLM serves as a general decision policy rather than parameters fitted to a single configuration, the controller is training-free at deployment and transfers across configurations without re-optimization. We evaluate LLM-Foraging in Gazebo with TurtleBot3 robots across 36 configurations spanning team sizes of 4 to 10 robots, arena sizes from 6x6 to 10x10 meters, and three resource distributions (clustered, powerlaw, random). LLM-Foraging collects more resources than the GA-tuned CPFA baseline across the evaluated configurations and is more consistent, a property that the GA's single-configuration tuning does not transfer.

</details>


### [101] [Action Agent: Agentic Video Generation Meets Flow-Constrained Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01477)
*Jeffrin Sam,Nguyen Khang,Yara Mahmoud,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Action Agent，一个两阶段框架：第一阶段由LLM协调生成第一人称导航视频，第二阶段通过FlowDiT（流约束扩散Transformer）将视频和语言指令转换为连续速度命令，实现多实体（人形机器人、无人机、轮式机器人）的语言引导导航。在真实Unitree G1上达到64.7%任务完成率，运行频率40-47Hz。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航方法在不同形态机器人（人形、无人机、轮式）上缺乏统一框架，且第一人称导航视频生成成功率低（单次仅35%），需要一种能够解耦轨迹想象（视频生成）与运动执行（速度控制）的可扩展范式。

Method: 两阶段框架：阶段I - LLM协调模块，迭代选择扩散模型、优化提示词、积累跨任务记忆，将语言和图像输入合成为第一人称导航视频（成功率从35%提升至86%）；阶段II - FlowDiT，使用流约束扩散Transformer，融合DINOv2视觉特征、学习光流表征自运动、CLIP语言嵌入实现语义停止，在动作空间进行去噪扩散生成连续速度命令。预训练于RECON户外数据集，并在Isaac Sim中采集的203个Unitree G1人形机器人episode上微调。

Result: 模拟导航成功率73.2%，真实Unitree G1在未知室内环境开环执行任务完成率64.7%，运行频率40-47Hz。单43M参数checkpoint即可工作。在三类实体（人形机器人真机、无人机、轮式机器人模拟）上均验证有效。

Conclusion: 解耦轨迹想象与运动执行是一种可扩展且具身感知的语言引导导航范式，能够统一多形态机器人导航，且小模型（43M参数）即可实现高效实时的导航控制。

Abstract: We present Action Agent, a two-stage framework that unifies agentic navigation video generation with flow-constrained diffusion control for multi-embodiment robot navigation. In Stage I, a large language model (LLM) acts as an orchestration module that selects video diffusion models, refines prompts through iterative validation, and accumulates cross-task memory to synthesize physically plausible first-person navigation videos from language and image inputs. This increases video generation success from 35% (single-shot) to 86% across 50 navigation tasks. In Stage II, we introduce FlowDiT, a Flow-Constrained Diffusion Transformer that converts optimized goal videos and language instructions into continuous velocity commands using action-space denoising diffusion. FlowDiT integrates DINOv2 visual features, learned optical flow for ego-motion representation, and CLIP language embeddings for semantic stopping. We pretrain on the RECON outdoor navigation dataset and fine-tune on 203 Unitree G1 humanoid episodes collected in Isaac Sim to calibrate velocity dynamics. A single 43M-parameter checkpoint achieves 73.2% navigation success in simulation and 64.7% task completion on a real Unitree G1 in unseen indoor environments under open-loop execution, while operating at 40--47 Hz. We evaluate Action Agent across three embodiments: a Unitree G1 humanoid (real hardware), a drone, and a wheeled mobile robot (Isaac Sim), demonstrating that decoupling trajectory imagination from execution yields a scalable and embodiment-aware paradigm for language-guided navigation.

</details>


### [102] [Cut-In Gap Acceptance Toward Autonomous vs. Human-Driven Vehicles: Evidence from the Waymo Open Motion Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01485)
*Abdulaziz Alhuraish,Yuhang Wang,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 人类驾驶员在切入自动驾驶车辆（AV）前方时，会接受比切入人类驾驶车辆（HDV）前方更短的纵向间隙（中位数7.58米 vs 9.57米），且切入速度更高（51.7 vs 37.7 km/h），揭示了人类对AV的特殊驾驶行为偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证人类驾驶员是否因AV遵循保守、基于规则的策略，而倾向于在AV前方接受更短的纵向切入间隙。

Method: 使用Waymo开放运动数据集（WOMD），提取25,906个真实高速场景中706个HDV-to-AV和3,172个HDV-to-HDV切入事件，基于八准则换道检测算法，通过中位数间隙比较、速度匹配重采样和卡方检验进行统计分析。

Result: AV目标的中位数接受间隙为7.58米，比HDV的9.57米短1.99米（p=5.76×10⁻⁸, d=-0.224）；切入速度高37%（51.7 vs 37.7 km/h, d=0.502）；68.0%的AV目标切入发生在10米间隙以下，而HDV为51.8%（χ²=60.5, p<10⁻¹³）。

Conclusion: 人类对AV存在系统性且与安全相关的间隙接受行为不对称，AV的运动规划安全包络和交通仿真模型需要针对此特性进行专门校准。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles (AVs) are widely known to follow conservative, rule-based motion policies that surrounding drivers can learn to anticipate. A direct consequence is that human drivers may accept shorter longitudinal gaps when cutting in front of an AV than when targeting another human-driven vehicle (HDV). We test this hypothesis using the Waymo Open Motion Dataset (WOMD), which provides 25,906 real-world highway scenarios at 10 hertz. An eight-criterion lane-change detector extracts 706 HDV-to-AV and 3,172 HDV-to-HDV cut-in events from the same traffic environment. The median accepted gap in front of the Waymo AV is 7.58 meters versus 9.57 meters for HDV targets, a 1.99 meter reduction that is statistically significant (p equals 5.76 times 10 to the negative eighth power, d equals negative 0.224) and persists under speed-matched resampling. Cut-in speeds toward the AV are 37 percent higher (51.7 versus 37.7 kilometers per hour, d equals 0.502), and 68.0 percent of AV-targeted cut-ins occur below the 10 meter gap boundary versus 51.8 percent of HDV-targeted events (chi-squared equals 60.5, p is less than 10 to the negative thirteenth power). These results reveal a systematic and safety-relevant asymmetry in human gap-acceptance behavior that warrants AV-specific calibration of both motion-planning safety envelopes and traffic simulation models.

</details>


### [103] [Distributed Algorithm with Emergent Area Partitioning and Base Station's Situation Awareness for Multi-Robot Patrolling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01501)
*Kazuho Kobayashi,Shohei Kobayashi,Seiya Ueno,Takehiro Higuchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为LR-PT的多机器人巡逻算法，通过局部信息决策和自主区域划分，实现了更高的巡逻效率和基站态势感知能力，并在仿真中验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人巡逻中需要提高巡逻效率并增强基站操作员的态势感知能力，使其能够预测机器人行为、支持识别与决策以及执行紧急干预。

Method: LR-PT（Local Reactive and Partition）算法：机器人基于局部可用信息独立选择巡逻目标，将巡逻需求与向基站报告任务进度的紧迫性整合为统一效用函数；算法自主涌现区域划分，避免陷入局部最优。

Result: 仿真结果显示LR-PT在确保所有感兴趣位置的高频巡逻、增强基站态势感知方面优于现有方法，并且对通信限制和机器人故障具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LR-PT融合了群体机器人学的优势，有效应对实际作战挑战，实现了对全任务区域的综合巡逻。

Abstract: Patrolling with multiple robots offers efficient surveillance to detect and manage undesired situations. This necessitates improved patrol efficiency and operator situation awareness at base stations. Enhanced situation awareness enables operators to predict robots' behaviors, support recognition and decision-making, and execute emergency interventions. This study presents the Local Reactive and Partition (LR-PT) algorithm, a novel multi-robot patrolling approach. In simulations, LR-PT outperformed existing methods by ensuring frequent patrols of all locations of interest and enhancing the situation awareness of the base station. Robots independently select patrol targets based on locally available information, integrating patrol needs and the urgency of reporting mission progress to the base station into a unified utility function. This locality also contributes to robustness against communication constraints and robot failures, as demonstrated in this research. The algorithm further autonomously emerged the area partition, which can avoid falling into local optima and realize the comprehensive patrol over the whole mission area. The simulation results demonstrated the superior performance of LR-PT for multi-robot patrolling, utilizing the advantages of swarm robotics and addressing real-world operational challenges.

</details>


### [104] [Dynamics Distillation for Efficient and Transferable Control Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01516)
*Xunjiang Gu,Kashyap Chitta,Mahsa Golchoubian,Vladimir Suplin,Igor Gilitschenski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Sim2Sim2Sim框架，通过将高保真模拟器动力学蒸馏为可高度并行化的学习动力学模型，在蒸馏环境中训练控制策略并部署回原模拟器，实现高效策略优化与可靠迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶模拟器要么物理真实（高保真）但计算扩展性差，要么计算可扩展但物理真实性不足，无法同时满足鲁棒控制策略学习所需的两个关键属性。

Method: 将高保真模拟器的动力学模型蒸馏到一个高度并行化的可学习动力学模型中，在此蒸馏环境中训练强化学习控制策略，再部署回原高保真模拟器进行验证。

Result: 在蒸馏环境中训练的策略能够高效优化并可靠迁移到高保真模拟器；同时发现预测精度本身不能完全表征学习动力学模型作为训练环境的适用性。

Conclusion: 学习动力学模型作为RL训练环境的评估不应仅看预测准确性，还应考察其能否催生高质量策略；Sim2Sim2Sim框架有效弥合了高保真模拟与可扩展RL之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Robust control policy learning for autonomous driving requires training environments to be both physically realistic and computationally scalable, properties that existing simulators provide only in isolation. We introduce Sim2Sim2Sim, a framework that bridges high-fidelity vehicle simulation and scalable reinforcement learning by distilling simulator dynamics into a highly parallelizable learned dynamics model. By training control policies purely within this distilled environment and deploying them back into the high-fidelity source simulator, we demonstrate more efficient policy optimization and reliable transfer under challenging dynamics. We further show that predictive accuracy alone does not fully characterize a learned dynamics model's suitability as a reinforcement learning training environment, which should also be assessed by the quality of the policies it enables.

</details>


### [105] [VOFA: Visual Object Goal Pushing with Force-Adaptive Control for Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01518)
*Zichao Hu,Zifan Xu,Dongsik Chang,He Yin,Linh Tran,Roberto Martín-Martín,Peter Stone,Jingyu Qiao,Joydeep Biswas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VOFA是一个面向人形机器人的视觉目标条件推搡操作系统，通过双层分层架构（高层视觉运动策略+低层力自适应全身控制器），在无需先验物体物理属性信息的情况下，实现对未知质量与摩擦系数的重型物体（最高17kg，超过机器人自重一半）的任意目标位置推搡，在仿真和真实实验中分别达到90%和80%以上的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人需要具备利用机载自我中心感知、以目标导向方式推动大物体的能力，以完成仓库物料搬运等复杂任务。现有方案要么依赖特权物体状态信息且缺乏机载感知，要么对目标配置变化和物体物理属性变化的鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出VOFA系统，采用双层分层架构：高层视觉运动策略处理噪声机载观测，生成目标条件指令，在多种物体-目标配置下闭环运行；低层力自适应全身控制器提供对物体物理属性变化的鲁棒性。

Result: 在Booster T1人形机器人上进行了仿真和真实实验，仿真成功率超过90%，真实实验成功率超过80%；成功推动最高17kg的物体，超过Booster T1自重的一半。

Conclusion: VOFA系统通过视觉目标条件的分层架构，实现了人形机器人对未知物理属性物体的鲁棒推搡操作，验证了其在仿真和真实环境中的高效性。

Abstract: The ability to push large objects in a goal-directed manner using onboard egocentric perception is an essential skill for humanoid robots to perform complex tasks such as material handling in warehouses. To robustly manipulate heavy objects to arbitrary goal configurations, the robot must cope with unknown object mass and ground friction, noisy onboard perception, and actuation errors; all in a real-time feedback loop. Existing solutions either rely on privileged object-state information without onboard perception or lack robustness to variations in goal configurations and object physical properties. In this work, we present VOFA, a visual goal-conditioned humanoid loco-manipulation system capable of pushing objects with unknown physical properties to arbitrary goal positions. VOFA consists of a two-level hierarchical architecture with a high-level visuomotor policy and a low-level force-adaptive whole-body controller. The high-level policy processes noisy onboard observations and generates goal-conditioned commands to operate in closed loop across diverse object-goal configurations, while the low-level whole-body controller provides robustness to variations in object physical properties. VOFA is extensively evaluated in both simulation and real-world experiments on the Booster T1 humanoid robot. Our results demonstrate strong performance, achieving over 90% success in simulation and over 80% success in real-world trials. Moreover, VOFA successfully pushes objects weighing up to 17kg, exceeding half of the Booster T1's body weight.

</details>


### [106] [Good in Bad (GiB): Sifting Through End-user Demonstrations for Learning a Better Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01529)
*Noushad Sojib,Momotaz Begum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GiB算法，能从混合质量的人类演示中自动识别并丢弃错误子任务，保留高质量子任务，从而提升策略学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数模仿学习算法假设可获取高质量演示，但非专家用户的数据常包含无意错误。直接学习会导致不安全策略行为，而丢弃整条演示又浪费宝贵数据。

Method: 先训练自监督模型学习潜在特征并为每条演示分配二元权重（好/坏）；然后建模高质量片段的潜在特征分布，利用马氏距离检测和评估低质量子任务。

Result: 在Franka机器人的仿真和真实多步任务中验证，从混合质量人类演示学习时，策略性能得到提升。

Conclusion: GiB能有效过滤低质量子任务，使任何策略学习算法都能从过滤后的数据中训练出更鲁棒的策略。

Abstract: Imitation learning offers a promising framework for enabling robots to acquire diverse skills from human users. However, most imitation learning algorithms assume access to high-quality demonstrations an unrealistic expectation when collecting data from non-expert users, whose demonstrations often contain inadvertent errors. Naively learning from such demonstrations can result in unsafe policy behavior, while discarding entire demonstrations due to occasional mistakes wastes valuable data, especially in low-data settings. In this work, we introduce GiB (Good-in-Bad), an algorithm that automatically identifies and discards erroneous subtasks within demonstrations while preserving high-quality subtasks. The filtered data can then be used by any policy learning algorithm to train more robust policies. GiB first trains a self-supervised model to learn latent features and assigns binary weights to label each demonstration as good or bad. It then models the latent feature distribution of high-quality segments and uses the Mahalanobis distance to detect and evaluate poor-quality subtasks. We validate GiB on the Franka robot in both simulated and real-world multi-step tasks, demonstrating improved policy performance when learning from mixed-quality human demonstrations.

</details>


### [107] [An Efficient Metric for Data Quality Measurement in Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01544)
*Noushad Sojib,Momotaz Begum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于功率谱密度(PSD)的演示轨迹排名指标，用于自动筛选高质量演示数据，以提升模仿学习策略在新环境中的微调效果，无需策略学习、环境交互或专家标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习机器人在实际部署中面临分布外场景的挑战，而通过终端用户演示进行微调是有效策略，但终端用户演示质量低下（包含过多修正动作、振荡和突然调整），现有自动化数据筛选方法需要策略回滚，计算成本高且不实用。

Method: 提出基于功率谱密度(PSD)的演示轨迹排名指标，PSD值越低表示轨迹越平滑、质量越高，越高表示轨迹存在伪影和异常。该方法无需策略学习、环境交互或专家标注。

Result: 在专家和非专家演示数据集上评估，并在养老院做用户研究（使用π0.5模型微调日常生活任务），PSD筛选的数据相比未筛选基线和两种竞争方法，任务成功率更高、执行轨迹更平滑。

Conclusion: PSD指标是一种快速、高效、全自动的演示数据筛选方法，适用于大规模实地数据整理，能有效提升模仿学习策略性能。

Abstract: Imitation learning (IL) has seen remarkable progress, yet field deployment of IL-powered robots remains hindered by the challenge of out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios. Fine-tuning pre-trained policies with end-user demonstrations collected in deployment environments is a promising strategy to address this challenge. However, end-user demonstrations are frequently of poor quality, characterized by excessive corrective motions, oscillations, and abrupt adjustments that degrade both learned and fine-tuned policy performance. Existing automated approaches for curating demonstration data require policy rollouts in the environment, making them computationally expensive and impractical for real-world deployment. In this paper, we propose a fast, efficient, and fully automated demonstration ranking metric based on the power spectral density (PSD) of demonstration trajectories. The PSD metric requires no policy learning, environment interaction, or expert labeling, making it well-suited for scalable, in-the-field data curation. Lower PSD values correspond to smoother, higher-quality demonstrations, while higher PSD values indicate erratic, artifact-laden trajectories. We evaluate the proposed metric on two benchmark imitation learning datasets comprising expert and lay-user demonstrations, and through a user study with older adults at a retirement facility, where collected demonstrations are used to fine-tune $\pi0.5$ \cite{intelligence2025pi_} for a daily living task. Results demonstrate that PSD-curated data yields policies with higher task success rates and smoother execution trajectories compared to uncurated baselines and two competitive data-ranking methods.

</details>


### [108] [Hydra-DP3: Frequency-Aware Right-Sizing of 3D Diffusion Policies for Visuomotor Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01581)
*Jinhao Zhang,Zhexuan Zhou,Huizhe Li,Yichen Lai,Wenlong Xia,Haoming Song,Youmin Gong,Jie Mei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文从频域视角重新审视扩散策略，发现机器人动作轨迹能量集中在低频DCT模式，最优去噪器误差由低频子空间维度和残差高频能量决定，两步去噪即可饱和。据此提出Hydra-DP3，采用轻量级Diffusion Mixer解码器支持两步DDIM推理，参数少于先前方法的1%，在多个基准和真实任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散视觉运动策略继承了图像生成风格的多步采样解码器，推理成本高。作者从频域视角发现机器人动作轨迹具有高度平滑性，能量集中在低频DCT模式，表明动作去噪比图像生成简单得多，从而启发设计更轻量的去噪模型。

Method: 提出Hydra-DP3，采用pocket-scale的3D扩散策略，核心是轻量级Diffusion Mixer解码器，支持两步DDIM反向采样。通过频域理论分析证明了最优去噪器的误差界由低频子空间维度和残差高频能量决定，验证两步去噪的充分性。

Result: 在RoboTwin2.0、Adroit、MetaWorld和真实世界任务中达到SOTA性能，参数少于先前3D扩散策略方法的1%，显著降低推理延迟。

Conclusion: 从频域角度揭示了机器人动作去噪与图像生成的本质差异，证明两步去噪足以满足动作生成需求，为设计高效轻量的机器人扩散策略提供了理论依据和实用方案。

Abstract: Diffusion-based visuomotor policies perform well in robotic manipulation, yet current methods still inherit image-generation-style decoders and multi-step sampling. We revisit this design from a frequency-domain perspective. Robot action trajectories are highly smooth, with most energy concentrated in a few low-frequency discrete cosine transform modes. Under this structure, we show that the error of the optimal denoiser is bounded by the low-frequency subspace dimension and residual high-frequency energy, implying that denoising error saturates after very few reverse steps. This further suggests that action denoising requires a much simpler denoising model than image generation. Motivated by this insight, we propose Hydra-DP3(HDP3), a pocket-scale 3D diffusion policy with a lightweight Diffusion Mixer decoder that supports two-step DDIM inference. Our synthetic experiments validate the theory and support the sufficiency of two-step denoising. Futhermore, across RoboTwin2.0, Adroit, MetaWorld, and real-world tasks, HDP3 achieves state-of-the-art performance with fewer than 1% of the parameters of prior 3D diffusion-based policies and substantially lower inference latency.

</details>


### [109] [Lateral String Stability for Vehicle Platoons: Formulation, Definition, and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01731)
*Sixu Li,Swaroop Darbha,Yang Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于弧长（欧拉）视角的侧向串行稳定性框架，分析了仅依赖车载感知与V2V通信两种控制策略对路径跟踪误差的衰减能力，证明了车载感知无法保证误差衰减而V2V通信可实现真正的误差衰减，并指出了保证稳定性所需的控制器结构条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向串行稳定性已被广泛研究，但侧向串行稳定性——控制路径跟踪误差沿车队的传播——尚待探索。随着自动驾驶车辆依赖车载感知和无地图导航，传感器遮挡和密集编队加剧了安全风险，亟需一个聚焦于路径相对跟踪误差的侧向串行稳定性分析框架。

Method: 采用弧长（欧拉）视角替代传统分析视角，定义L2侧向串行稳定性；提出两种控制策略：基于车载感知的反馈-前馈策略，以及基于V2V通信的"向 predecessor 学习"策略；针对跟踪误差向量和侧向（横摆）误差两个指标进行稳定性分析。

Result: 1) 仅依靠车载感知无法保证路径跟踪误差的衰减，存在根本性的安全限制；2) V2V通信能够实现真正的误差衰减；3) 确定了结构性控制器要求：对特定测量量的非零反馈是保证稳定性的必要条件。

Conclusion: 建立了面向安全关键的侧向串行稳定性分析框架，揭示了车载感知的固有局限性与V2V通信的必要性，为编队控制器的结构设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Platooning of connected and automated vehicles provides significant benefits in terms of energy efficiency, traffic throughput, and, most critically, safety.
