These safety benefits depend on string stability, which dictates how disturbances propagate along a vehicle string. Although longitudinal string stability has been extensively examined, lateral string stability, which governs the propagation of path-tracking errors that can lead to unsafe deviations from the desired path, remains underexplored. Its importance is growing as autonomous vehicles increasingly depend on onboard sensing and map-free navigation, where sensor occlusions and tight formations amplify safety risks. This paper presents a framework for lateral string stability that focuses directly on safety-critical, path-relative tracking errors and enables consistent comparison across vehicles that follow the same planned path. The key element of the framework is an arc-length (Eulerian) viewpoint, a departure from traditional analyses, that clarifies how tracking errors at a given point on the path propagate from one vehicle to the next. Building on this foundation, we propose the definition of L2 lateral string stability along with two control strategies: a feedback-feedforward strategy that relies solely on onboard sensing, and a novel learn-from-predecessor strategy that makes use of vehicle-to-vehicle communication. Both strategies are analyzed for lateral string stability with respect to two error measures: tracking error vector and lateral (cross-track) error. Our results show that onboard sensing alone cannot guarantee attenuation of path-tracking errors, imposing a fundamental safety limitation, while V2V communication enables true error attenuation. The analysis further identifies structural controller requirements, showing that nonzero feedback on specific measurements is essential for guaranteeing stability.

</details>


### [110] [Anticipation-VLA: Solving Long-Horizon Embodied Tasks via Anticipation-based Subgoal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01772)
*Zhilong Zhang,Wenyu Luo,Haonan Wang,Yifei Sheng,Yidi Wang,Hanyuan Guo,Haoxiang Ren,Xinghao Du,Yuhan Che,Tongtong Cao,Lei Yuan,Yang Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Anticipation-VLA模型，通过自适应递归生成子目标来解决VLA模型在长时域任务中的复合错误问题，在仿真和真实机器人任务中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在长时域任务中因复合错误表现不佳，先前方法使用固定粒度的子任务分解无法适应执行状态的变化复杂性，限制了鲁棒性。

Method: 提出Anticipation Model自适应递归生成未来子目标，随任务展开持续调整；基于此构建Anticipation-VLA层次化模型，微调统一多模态模型（UMM）进行高层子目标生成，并采用目标条件化VLA策略执行低层动作。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人任务中验证了Anticipation-VLA的有效性，表明自适应递归子目标生成对鲁棒策略执行的重要性。

Conclusion: Anticipation-VLA通过自适应递归子目标生成机制，有效提升了VLA模型在长时域任务中的表现，为层次化具身智能提供了新思路。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a powerful paradigm for embodied intelligence, enabling robots to perform tasks based on natural language instructions and current visual input. However, existing VLA models struggle with long-horizon tasks due to compounding errors. Prior methods decompose tasks into subtasks of fixed granularity, which cannot adapt to the varying complexity of execution states, limiting their robustness in long-horizon tasks. To overcome this, we introduce Anticipation Model, which adaptively and recursively generates future subgoals. This model continuously adapts as the task unfolds, adjusting future subgoals in response to evolving dynamics, facilitating more reliable planning paths. Building on this concept, we propose Anticipation-VLA, a hierarchical VLA model that leverages the anticipation model to generate actionable subgoals that guide VLA policy execution. We implement Anticipation-VLA with finetuning a Unified Multimodal Model (UMM) for high-level subgoal generation and a goal-conditioned VLA policy for low-level action execution. Experiments in both simulated and real-world robotic tasks demonstrate the effectiveness of Anticipation-VLA, highlighting the importance of adaptive and recursive subgoal generation for robust policy execution.

</details>


### [111] [On the Characterization and Limits of 4D Radar for Aided Inertial Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01773)
*Morten Nissov,Kostas Alexis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对FMCW雷达在辅助惯性导航中的复杂噪声问题，从基本原理推导噪声模型，设计基于因子图的估计器，并通过仿真和实际实验验证了其在鲁棒性和精度上的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FMCW雷达虽在挑战性环境中优于LiDAR和视觉传感器，但其复杂的噪声特性阻碍了可靠估计与广泛应用，亟需从原理上理解并解决该问题。

Method: 从雷达信号处理的第一性原理出发推导噪声模型，基于因子图设计估计器，采用一阶近似传播测量噪声，并通过仿真和实地实验评估不同噪声源、近似有效性和状态依赖协方差。

Result: 仿真验证了噪声源重要性、一阶近似的有效性和协方差的状态依赖性；实验表明所提方法在多种环境和飞行剖面下（包括超出雷达标准工作范围）具有优越的鲁棒性和精度。

Conclusion: 通过原理性噪声建模和因子图估计器设计，有效解决了FMCW雷达辅助惯性导航中的复杂噪声问题，显著提升了估计鲁棒性和准确性。

Abstract: Frequency Modulated Continuous Wave (FMCW) radar is a promising sensor for aided inertial navigation, due to its robustness in environments that challenge traditional alternatives, such as LiDAR and vision. However, its widespread adoption is hindered by complex, noisy measurements, which make reliable estimation difficult. This manuscript addresses these challenges by analyzing the fundamental measurement relations of FMCW radar sensing and developing a reliable estimator. Noise models are derived by applying first principles to the underlying signal processing of a typical radar sensor. These models guide the design of a factor graph-based estimator, utilizing a first-order approximation for the measurement noise propagation. The approach is first examined through simulation, evaluating the significance of different noise sources, the validity of the first-order approximation, and the state-dependent nature of the covariance expressions. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superior robustness and accuracy of the proposed method across diverse field environments and flight profiles, including beyond the radar's standard operating range. Furthermore, the experiments confirm the insights from the simulation regarding the behavior and performance of different estimator configurations relative to their operating conditions. The evaluation data and estimator implementation are made available at https://github.com/ntnu-arl/rig.

</details>


### [112] [Optimizing Trajectory-Trees in Belief Space: An Application from Model Predictive Control to Task and Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01860)
*Camille Phiquepal,Marc Toussaint*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在部分可观测机器人规划问题中，使用轨迹树（trajectory-trees）替代传统的顺序轨迹的好处。论文分别针对模型预测控制（MPC）和任务与运动规划（TAMP）两个场景，提出了PO-MPC和PO-LGP方法，并通过分布式增广拉格朗日算法（D-AuLa）实现实时优化，实验证明了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测环境中，机器人的最优行动依赖于观测信息，而传统顺序轨迹无法自然捕获这种依赖关系。轨迹树通过在信念空间中进行分支优化，能够考虑多个可能的观测情景，从而更有效地处理不确定性。

Method: 1) PO-MPC：将控制问题建模为单分支轨迹树的优化，提出分布式增广拉格朗日（D-AuLa）算法，利用问题的可分解性进行并行加速。2) PO-LGP：在任务层面推理决策树，在运动规划层面使用轨迹树，扩展了逻辑几何规划（LGP）框架以处理部分可观测问题。

Result: 在自动驾驶场景的线性和非线性MPC实验中，PO-MPC相比顺序轨迹方法降低了控制成本；PO-LGP在小规模信念状态问题上表现良好，并通过优化探索性策略（宏观动作）扩展到更大规模问题。

Conclusion: 轨迹树在部分可观测规划中优于顺序轨迹，能够通过分支结构更好地处理观测依赖性和不确定性，且所提出的并行优化算法满足实时性要求。

Abstract: This paper explores the benefits of computing arborescent trajectories (trajectory-trees) instead of commonly used sequential trajectories for partially observable robotic planning problems. In such environments, a robot infers knowledge from observations, and the optimal course of action depends on these observations. \revise{Trajectory-trees, optimized in belief space, naturally capture this dependency by branching where the belief state is expected to evolve into multiple distinct scenarios, such as upon receiving an observation. Unlike sequential trajectories, which model a single forward evolution of the system, trajectory-trees capture multiple possible contingencies.}
  First, we focus on Model Predictive Control (MPC) and demonstrate the benefits of planning tree-like trajectories. We formulate the control problem as the optimization of a tree with a single branching (PO-MPC). This improves performance by reducing control costs through more informed planning. To satisfy the real-time constraints of MPC, we develop an optimization algorithm called Distributed Augmented Lagrangian (D-AuLa), which leverages the decomposability of the PO-MPC formulation to parallelize and accelerate the optimization. We apply the method to both linear and non-linear MPC problems using autonomous driving examples.
  Second, we address Task And Motion Planning (TAMP), and introduce a planner (PO-LGP) reasoning on decision trees at task level, and trajectory-trees at motion-planning level. This approach builds upon the Logic-Geometric-Programming Framework (LGP) and extends it to partially observable problems. The experiments show the method's applicability to problems with a small belief state size, and scales to larger problems by optimizing explorative policies, which are used as macro-actions in an overarching task plan.

</details>


### [113] [Phone2Act: A Low-Cost, Hardware-Agnostic Teleoperation System for Scalable VLA Data Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01948)
*Om Mandhane,Bipin Yadav,Sangeetha Prasanna Ram,Gopalakrishnan Narayanan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Phone2Act是一个低成本、硬件无关的遥操作框架，利用智能手机和Google ARCore实现6-DoF机器人控制，基于模块化ROS 2架构，支持多种机器人平台，无需代码修改即可录制多相机RGB流和机器人状态数据，并直接导出为LeRobot格式用于VLA模型微调。在130个采集回合上微调GR00T-N1.5，在真实世界的多阶段拾取放置任务中达到90%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型训练所需的高质量操作数据采集成本高昂，依赖专用硬件或紧耦合特定机器人平台，限制了研究群体的可及性。

Method: 提出Phone2Act框架，利用智能手机和Google ARCore实现6-DoF遥操作；采用模块化ROS 2架构，通过可互换桥接节点解耦控制逻辑与硬件；Universal Recorder同步多相机RGB流与机器人状态反馈，原生导出LeRobot格式数据集。

Result: 在130个采集回合上微调GR00T-N1.5，在多阶段拾取放置任务中达到90%成功率，部署在物理Dobot CR5机器人上。

Conclusion: Phone2Act验证了低成本、硬件无关的遥操作框架能够有效支持VLA模型的数据采集与微调，显著降低数据获取门槛。

Abstract: Collecting diverse, high-quality manipulation data for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model training remains prohibitively expensive for many research groups, as existing teleoperation frameworks rely on specialized hardware or are tightly coupled to specific robot platforms. We present Phone2Act, a low-cost, hardware-agnostic teleoperation framework that transforms a commodity smartphone into a 6-DoF robot controller via Google ARCore. Built on a modular ROS 2 architecture, Phone2Act decouples control logic from hardware specifics through interchangeable bridge nodes, supporting platforms from industrial cobots to low-cost bimanual arms without code modification. A Universal Recorder synchronizes multi-camera RGB streams with robot state feedback and exports demonstrations natively in the LeRobot dataset format, eliminating post-processing and enabling immediate VLA fine-tuning. We validate the framework by fine-tuning GR00T-N1.5 on 130 collected episodes, achieving a 90% success rate on a real-world multi-stage pick-and-place task deployed on a physical Dobot CR5.

</details>


### [114] [Sonar-GPS Fusion for Seabed Mapping in Turbid Shallow Waters with an Autonomous Surface Vehicle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01949)
*Yisheng Zhang,Michael Xu,Alan Williams,Matthew Gray,Nare Karapetyan,Miao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种抗漂移的海底地图构建框架，结合傅里叶-梅林变换的局部声呐帧对齐和基于扩展卡尔曼滤波的全局轨迹优化，在牡蛎养殖场实验中相比纯FMT基线将RMSE漂移降低了9.5%，实现了亚米级重建精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统光学方法在浑浊浅水区（如贝类养殖场）效果有限，现有声呐系统因定位测量精度低和长轨迹漂移累积难以实现高分辨率地图构建。

Method: 提出抗漂移框架：(1) 局部FLS帧对齐采用傅里叶-梅林变换；(2) 全局轨迹优化基于扩展卡尔曼滤波，融合GPS、IMU和罗盘数据；(3) 使用基于方差的图像融合策略减少重叠区域视觉伪影。

Result: 在结构化牡蛎养殖场实地试验中，相比纯FMT基线，RMSE漂移降低了9.5%，实现亚米级重建精度并保留高分辨率纹理，可用于牡蛎库存估计。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架有效解决了长轨迹下的漂移问题，能够在浑浊浅水环境中实现高精度海底地图构建，支持栖息地监测和基础设施检查等实际应用。

Abstract: Accurate seabed mapping is essential for habitat monitoring and infrastructure inspection. In turbid, shallow coastal waters, such as shellfish aquaculture farms, the effectiveness of traditional optical methods is limited. Autonomous surface vehicles (ASVs) equipped with forward-looking sonar (FLS) offer a promising alternative. However, existing sonar-based systems face challenges in achieving fine resolution mapping over long trajectories due to low-resolution positioning measurements and accumulated drift over long trajectories. In this paper, we present a drift-resilient seabed mapping framework that integrates local FLS frame alignment using the Fourier-Mellin transform (FMT) with global trajectory optimization based on an extended Kalman filter (EKF) that fuses global positioning system (GPS), inertial measurement unit (IMU), and compass data. A variance-based image blending strategy is used to further reduce visual artifacts in overlapping regions. Field trials on a structured oyster farm site show that our framework helps reduce drift in RMSE by 9.5% relative to the FMT-only baseline. This framework also enables sub-meter reconstruction accuracy and preservation of high-resolution textures needed for oyster inventory estimation within the mapped areas.

</details>


### [115] [Optimized and kinematically feasible multi-agent motion planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01996)
*Anja Hellander,Kristoffer Bergman,Daniel Axehill*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段多智能体运动规划方法：先通过CBS/PBS等算法获得初始可行解，再通过多相最优控制问题对解进行优化改进。针对拖拉机-拖车系统进行了评估，并扩展了SIPP-IP算法以支持更通用的成本函数和更大尺寸的智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体运动规划是自主系统的关键问题，现有方法难以同时保证解的优化性和运动学可行性，需要一种既能找到可行解又能进一步优化解质量的系统化方法。

Method: 提出两阶段方法：第一阶段使用CBS或PBS等现有方法生成初始可行解；第二阶段将初始解作为热启动，求解多相最优控制问题(OCP)来优化解。同时提出在时长均为同一采样时间整数倍的约束下生成优化运动基元的方法。扩展了SIPP-IP算法以支持更通用的成本函数和更大尺寸的智能体。

Result: 在拖拉机-拖车系统上评估：对于该特定系统，基于晶格(lattice)的规划器由于碰撞检查不那么保守而优于SIPP-IP；CBS比PBS成功率更高、平均运行时间更短，但两者经过改进步骤后解的质量相似。

Conclusion: 两阶段方法能有效优化多智能体运动规划解，不同初始规划器（CBS/PBS）在该系统上表现有差异但优化后质量趋同，且规划器的选择需根据系统特性（如SIPP-IP与lattice-based规划器的对比）来决定。

Abstract: Multi-agent motion planning (MAMP) is an important problem for autonomous systems with multiple agents. In this work we propose a two-step method for finding optimized and kinematically feasible solutions to MAMP problems. The first step finds an initial feasible solution using state-of-the-art methods such as conflict-based search (CBS) or priority-based search (PBS), and the second step is an improvement step which improves the solution by solving a multi-phase optimal control problem (OCP) where the initial solution is used to warm-start the solver. We also propose a method for generating motion primitives in an optimized way under the constraint that the primitive durations are all multiples of the same sample time.
  We evaluate our proposed framework on a MAMP problem for tractor-trailer systems. We extend the safe interval path planning with interval projections (SIPP-IP) algorithm so it can handle more general cost functions and larger agents, but our results show that for the tractor-trailer system a simple lattice-based planner performs better due to less conservative collision checks. Our experiments also indicate that CBS performs better than PBS for this system as it achieves a higher success rate in environments with obstacles and had a lower average runtime, although both planners achieve solutions of similar quality after the improvement step.

</details>


### [116] [Neural Backward Reach-Avoid Tubes with MPC Supervision for High-Dimensional Systems: An Application to Safe Spacecraft Docking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02021)
*Santiago Thorup,Luca Castelletto,Zeyuan Feng,Somil Bansal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于学习的后向可达避碰管（BRAT）框架，通过将Hamilton-Jacobi分析与MPC监督相结合，解决了自主航天器对接中高维耦合动力学下的可达-避碰问题，在6D和13D系统中均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主航天器对接需要在耦合的高维平移-旋转动力学下同时保证碰撞规避和目标可达性。HJ可达性分析能提供形式化的可达-避碰保证，但经典求解器仅限于低维系统；学习方法虽开始扩展HJ分析，但在可达-避碰设置中（尤其当目标集与失效集紧密耦合时）表现不佳。

Method: 提出学习型BRAT框架：离线阶段使用基于PDE的损失结合课程式MPC监督训练HJ值函数的神经近似，其中MPC监督提供信息丰富的值目标并稳定训练；在线阶段通过两种实时控制器部署学习到的值函数：(i)值梯度驱动控制器，(ii)值函数增强的终端MPC，显式在时域末端强制执行可达性。

Result: 在6D平面对接问题上与网格法基真值对比验证，然后扩展到完整13D系统。在两个设置中，该方法在成功率和计算效率上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 将HJ结构分析与MPC监督紧密结合的学习框架能有效扩展HJ可达性分析至高维耦合系统，为自主航天器对接提供了兼具形式化保证与计算效率的控制策略。

Abstract: Autonomous spacecraft docking requires control policies that simultaneously ensure collision avoidance and target reachability under coupled, high-dimensional translational-rotational dynamics. Hamilton-Jacobi (HJ) reachability provides formal reach-avoid guarantees, but classical solvers are limited to low-dimensional systems. Learning-based approaches have begun to scale HJ analysis, yet they struggle in reach-avoid settings, especially where goal and failure sets are tightly coupled, as in docking. We propose a learning-based Backward Reach-Avoid Tube (BRAT) framework that addresses this challenge by tightly integrating HJ structure with MPC-based supervision. In the offline phase, we train a neural approximation of the HJ value function using PDE-based losses augmented with curriculum-driven MPC supervision, which provides informative value targets and stabilizes training in regions where purely PDE-based methods fail. In the online phase, the learned value function is deployed through two real-time controllers: (i) a value gradient-driven controller, and (ii) a value-function-augmented terminal MPC that explicitly enforces reachability at the horizon. We evaluate the proposed method on a 6D planar docking problem against grid-based ground truth and then scale to the full 13D system. Across both settings, our approach outperforms existing methods in success rate and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [117] [VILAS: A VLA-Integrated Low-cost Architecture with Soft Grasping for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02037)
*Zijian An,Hadi Khezam,Bill Cai,Ran Yang,Shijie Geng,Yiming Feng,Yue,Zheng,Lifeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VILAS是一个基于低成本模块化硬件（Fairino FR5机械臂+Jodell夹爪+双摄像头）的端到端VLA策略学习与部署平台，采用ZMQ通信架构统一遥操作、数据采集和策略部署，并设计了基于kirigami的柔性夹爪扩展件用于安全操作脆弱物体。在葡萄抓取任务上微调并评估了pi_0、pi_0.5和GR00T N1.6三个VLA模型，验证了低成本硬件上训练和部署可行操作策略的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略学习与部署通常依赖昂贵或专用的硬件平台，缺乏低成本、模块化且支持完整工作流（遥操作、数据采集、策略部署）的通用平台解决方案。

Method: 1) 集成Fairino FR5协作臂、Jodell RG52-50电动夹爪和双摄像头感知模块的硬件平台；2) 基于ZMQ的通信架构统一协调遥操作、数据采集和策略部署；3) 设计基于kirigami结构的柔性夹爪扩展件，利用可预测形变实现对脆弱物体的安全操作，无需显式力传感；4) 基于公开预训练检查点微调pi_0、pi_0.5和GR00T N1.6三个VLA模型。

Result: 在葡萄抓取任务上，三个VLA模型均成功训练并部署在低成本模块化硬件上，验证了系统有效性，并提供了当前VLA模型在真实场景中部署特性的实践洞察。

Conclusion: 低成本的模块化硬件平台足以支撑端到端VLA策略的学习与部署，剪裁式柔性夹爪扩展件能够在不依赖力传感器的情况下实现对脆弱物体的安全操作。

Abstract: We present VILAS, a fully low-cost, modular robotic manipulation platform designed to support end-to-end vision-language-action (VLA) policy learning and deployment on accessible hardware. The system integrates a Fairino FR5 collaborative arm, a Jodell RG52-50 electric gripper, and a dual-camera perception module, unified through a ZMQ-based communication architecture that seamlessly coordinates teleoperation, data collection, and policy deployment within a single framework. To enable safe manipulation of fragile objects without relying on explicit force sensing, we design a kirigami-based soft compliant gripper extension that induces predictable deformation under compressive loading, providing gentle and repeatable contact with delicate targets. We deploy and evaluate three state-of-the-art VLA models on the VILAS platform: pi_0, pi_0.5, and GR00T N1.6. All models are fine-tuned from publicly released pretrained checkpoints using an identical demonstration dataset collected via our teleoperation pipeline. Experiments on a grape grasping task validate the effectiveness of the proposed system, confirming that capable manipulation policies can be successfully trained and deployed on low-cost modular hardware. Our results further provide practical insights into the deployment characteristics of current VLA models in real-world settings.

</details>


### [118] [Robotic Desk Organization: A Multi-Primitive Approach to Manipulating Heterogeneous Objects via Environmental Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02135)
*Yi Dong. Yangjun Liu,Jinjun Duan,Yang Li,Zhendong Dai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向任务的服务机器人桌面整理框架，通过环境辅助操作原语（接触抓取、边缘推抓、杠杆抓取）和任务规划器，实现对平面刚性和可变形物体的多目标整理，实验验证了其有效性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务机器人在桌面整理任务中面临异构物体类型（刚性与可变形）和多样化操作目标（如收集、堆叠）带来的挑战，现有方法缺乏统一的框架来同时处理这些复杂需求。

Method: 设计感知流水线（数据增强+几何位姿/关键点估计+环境约束检测）；提出三种环境辅助操作原语（接触抓取小物体、边缘推抓刚性物体、杠杆抓取可变形物体）；集成任务规划器实现多物体整理。

Result: 充分的实际机器人实验表明所提框架在桌面整理任务中具有有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究为平面刚性和可变形物体提供了实用的操作原语，强调了环境和物体间约束在复杂多物体操作任务中的关键作用。

Abstract: Desktop organization remains challenging for service robots because of heterogeneous objects and diverse manipulation objectives, such as collection and stacking. In this article, a task-oriented framework is presented for organizing planar rigid and deformable objects on desks. A perception pipeline was developed that augments existing datasets with uncommon desktop items and makes geometry-based pose and keypoint estimation possible, along with the detection of environmental constraints, such as table edges. To handle diverse manipulation requirements, environment-assisted primitives are used, including contact-based grasping for small objects, edge-based push-grasping for planar rigid objects, and levering-based grasping for planar deformable objects. These primitives leverage environmental and interobject constraints to improve robustness. A task planner was designed to integrate these primitives into multiobject organization. Sufficient real-world experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed framework. This research provides practical manipulation primitives for planar rigid and deformable objects, highlighting the role of environmental and interobject constraints in complex multiobject manipulation tasks. Code and video are available online.

</details>


### [119] [Sampling-Based Control via Entropy-Regularized Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02147)
*Vincent Pacelli,Akash Ratheesh,Evangelos A. Theodorou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出OT-MPC算法，通过熵正则化最优传输理论解决采样型模型预测控制中的模式平均问题，无需梯度计算即可实现高性能实时控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MPPI和CEM等信息论方法忽略控制问题的几何结构，在复杂代价景观下出现模式平均(pathological mode-averaging)等病态行为。

Method: 基于熵正则化最优传输公式，通过Sinkhorn算法计算候选控制序列与低代价提议之间的最优耦合，实现无梯度闭式更新，在精细化候选的同时协调整体保持解空间覆盖。

Result: 在导航、操作和 locomotion 任务中，OT-MPC的成功率优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 最优传输框架有效解决了采样型MPC的几何忽视问题，在不依赖梯度的情况下提升了非线性系统控制的鲁棒性和性能。

Abstract: Sampling-based model predictive control methods like MPPI and CEM are essential for real-time control of nonlinear robotic systems, particularly where discontinuous dynamics preclude gradient-based optimization. However, these methods derive from information-theoretic objectives that are agnostic to the geometry of the control problem, leading to pathological behaviors such as mode-averaging when the cost landscape is complex. We present OT-MPC, a sampling-based algorithm that overcomes these limitations through an entropy-regularized optimal transport formulation. By computing an optimal coupling between candidate control sequences and low-cost proposals, OT-MPC refines candidates toward nearby promising samples while coordinating updates across the ensemble to maintain coverage of the solution space. We derive closed-form, gradient-free updates via the Sinkhorn algorithm, enabling real-time performance. Experiments on navigation, manipulation, and locomotion tasks demonstrate improved success rates over existing methods.

</details>


### [120] [Do We Really Need Immediate Resets? Rethinking Collision Handling for Efficient Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02192)
*Shanze Wang,Xinming Zhang,Siwei Cheng,Xianghui Wang,Hailong Huang,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出多碰撞重置预算(MCB)框架，允许智能体在同一回合中多次尝试碰撞后的导航，打破传统单次碰撞即全局重置的惯例，显著提升早期探索效率和导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统深度强化学习导航框架中，每次碰撞立即触发全局环境重置并视为完全任务失败，这阻碍了智能体在训练早期探索具有挑战性的障碍物配置，减缓了学习进度。

Method: 提出MCB（Multi-Collision reset Budget）框架，将局部碰撞终止与全局环境重置解耦，允许智能体在同一回合内对困难配置进行多次重试。

Result: 在多个模拟和真实机器人平台上的实验表明，该框架加速了早期探索，相比传统单次碰撞重置基线，同时提升了成功率和导航效率，且较小的碰撞预算即可产生最大的性能提升。

Conclusion: 挑战了导航任务中"一次碰撞即终止"的惯例，证明了允许有限次碰撞重试能够有效促进训练早期的探索，并提升整体导航性能。

Abstract: Should a single collision necessarily terminate an entire navigation episode? In most deep reinforcement learning (DRL) frameworks for robot navigation, this remains the standard practice: every collision immediately triggers a global environment reset and is penalized as a complete task failure. While a collision during deployment naturally indicates task failure, applying the same treatment during training prevents the agent from exploring challenging obstacle configurations, which slows learning progress in the early training phase. In this work, we challenge this convention and propose a Multi-Collision reset Budget (MCB) framework that decouples local collision termination from global environment resets, allowing the agent to retry difficult configurations within the same episode. Experiments on multiple simulated and real-world robotic platforms show that the framework accelerates early-stage exploration and improves both success rate and navigation efficiency over conventional single-collision reset baselines, with a small collision budget producing the largest gains.

</details>


### [121] [Change-Robust Online Spatial-Semantic Topological Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02227)
*Jiaming Wang,Jizhuo Chen,Diwen Liu,Atharva Ghotavadekar,Jiaxuan Da,Linh Kästner,Harold Soh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为CROSS的在线空间-语义表示方法，用姿态感知的拓扑图替代传统全局度量地图，通过连续SE(3)上的序贯假设检验处理感知模糊性，在光照变化和家具重排等环境变化下比SLAM基线和拓扑基线更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将语义附着在SLAM构建的度量地图上，但在外观变化和场景动态下，数据关联和重定位性能会退化，需要一种对环境变化鲁棒的空间-语义推理方法。

Method: 提出CROSS表示：用在线、姿态感知的RGB-D关键帧拓扑图替代全局一致性度量地图；采用连续SE(3)上的序贯假设检验显式推理感知模糊性；通过边界高斯混合信念维护姿态估计，以理论上处理回环检测和绑架机器人问题。

Result: 在严重外观变化（光照变化、家具重排）的真实机器人目标导航实验中，CROSS相比SLAM基线和拓扑基线表现出更强的鲁棒性，且在感知混淆下仍保持安全性。

Conclusion: 抛弃全局度量一致性、采用拓扑图+序贯假设检验的姿态推理方式，可以在环境变化下实现更鲁棒的空间-语义推理。

Abstract: Autonomous robots require change-robust spatial-semantic reasoning: using spatial and semantic knowledge to decide where to go, how to get there, and where the robot is despite environmental change. Existing approaches typically attach semantics to SLAM-built metric maps, but these pipelines are brittle under appearance shifts and scene dynamics, where data association and relocalization degrade. We propose a Change-Robust Online Spatial-Semantic (CROSS) representation that replaces a globally consistent metric substrate with an online, pose-aware topological graph of RGB-D keyframes. The system explicitly reasons over perceptual ambiguity using sequential hypothesis testing in continuous SE(3). Our estimator maintains a bounded Gaussian-mixture belief over poses, enabling principled handling of loop closures and kidnapped-robot events. Experiments under severe appearance change, including real-robot object-goal navigation with lighting shifts and furniture rearrangement, demonstrate improved robustness over SLAM-based and topological baselines while remaining safe under perceptual aliasing.

</details>


### [122] [Exact Higher-Order Derivatives for SE(3) via Analytical/AD Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02252)
*Frank O. Kuehnel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合解析/自动微分（AD）方法，用于SE(3)负对数似然函数的高阶导数计算。通过将解析/AD分界置于点作用接口y=Tx处，利用闭合形式的Lie群雅可比矩阵，实现了从一阶梯度到精确Hessian矩阵和高阶导数张量的高效计算。在基准测试中，该方法比有限差分法快约5倍，仅增加约70行解析雅可比代码，并解决了SO(3)/SE(3)标量基的奇异点问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Lie群框架（GTSAM、manif、Sophus等）主要针对一阶计算优化，从一阶原语到精确Hessian矩阵、观测信息矩阵和高阶导数张量之间存在空白，而这些是精确牛顿步、观测信息协方差估计和协方差校正所需的量。快速原型开发新的SE(3)估计目标在实际中仍然不便。

Method: 采用混合解析/AD方法：在点作用接口y=Tx处设置分界，接口之前使用闭合形式的Lie群雅可比矩阵（解析），接口之后使用自动微分。同一源码通过不同标量类型实例化：普通浮点数用于梯度，向量种子对偶数用于单次前向模式精确Hessian，嵌套对偶数用于高阶导数张量。同时识别并修复了SO(3)/SE(3)标量基在原点处因种子AD产生NaN的可移除奇点。

Result: 在6自由度、5地标的SE(3)负对数似然基准上，所提出的种子Hessian路径比有限差分AD梯度快约5倍，且与嵌套AD基准在机器精度内匹配。实现仅在AD基线基础上增加约70行解析雅可比代码。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一条从快速编写的SE(3)目标到精确高阶导数的实用路径，具有可预测的运行时且无需调优有限差分参数。

Abstract: Fast prototyping of new SE(3) estimation objectives remains awkward in practice. Modern Lie-group frameworks -- GTSAM, manif, Sophus, SymForce, Ceres -- target first-order workloads through different code-generation and automatic-differentiation strategies, each optimized for a particular seam between hand-derived geometry and generic differentiation. The remaining gap is a compact, AD-safe path from these first-order primitives to exact Hessians, observed-information matrices, and higher-order derivative tensors: the quantities needed for exact Newton steps, observed-information covariance estimates, and covariance correction.
  This paper presents a hybrid analytical/AD recipe for SE(3) negative log-likelihoods. The practitioner writes the NLL gradient once, generic over a scalar type, and places the analytical/AD seam at the point-action interface y = Tx. Closed-form Lie-group Jacobians are used up to this interface; AD is applied only beyond it. The same source is then instantiated with ordinary floating-point scalars for gradients, vector-seeded dual numbers for exact Hessians in a single forward-mode pass, and nested dual numbers for higher-order derivative tensors. On a representative 6-DoF, 5-landmark SE(3) NLL, the advocated seeded-Hessian path is approximately 5x faster than finite-differencing the AD gradient on this benchmark while matching a nested-AD oracle to machine precision. The implementation adds roughly 70 lines of analytical-Jacobian code over an AD-only baseline. We also identify and fix a removable singularity in the standard SO(3)/SE(3) scalar basis that would otherwise produce NaNs at the origin under seeded AD, and we audit which Lie-group derivative tensors require this stabilized basis. The result is a practical path from rapidly written SE(3) objectives to exact higher-order derivatives, with predictable runtime and no finite-difference tuning.

</details>


### [123] [SAGA: A Robust Self-Attention and Goal-Aware Anchor-based Planner for Safe UAV Autonomous Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02301)
*Junhao Wei,Yanxiao Li,Dexing Yao,Yifu Zhao,Haochen Li,Qibin He,Baili Lu,Xiaofan Zou,Dingcheng Yang,Sio-Kei Im,Yapeng Wang,Xu Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAGA是一种基于自注意力和目标感知锚点的规划器，用于无人机在杂乱环境中的安全自主导航，通过将局部规划建模为联合回归与排序问题，利用自注意力进行跨锚点全局推理，并引入极坐标位置编码和目标感知调制模块，在最高4.0m/s速度下实现100%成功率，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机导航规划方法在计算效率和结构表达能力之间难以兼顾，在杂乱环境中无法有效推理多个可行运动，导致高速度下成功率大幅下降。

Method: 将局部规划建模为固定运动锚点晶格上的单阶段联合回归与排序问题；利用自注意力将锚点对齐特征转换为几何感知token进行跨锚点全局推理；引入基于锚点偏航和俯仰的极坐标位置编码（PPE）保持token空间的方向结构；设计目标感知调制模块注入速度、加速度和目标信息以优化最终分数预测。

Result: 在2.0、3.0、4.0 m/s最大速度下均实现100%成功率；4.0 m/s下YOPO降至62.50%，Ego-planner降至52.63%，Fast-planner降至38.46%；平均安全距离从1.9843m提升至2.3888m，最小安全距离从0.4390m提升至0.7576m，总飞行时间从40.4631s降至27.4901s。

Conclusion: SAGA在计算效率和安全性上全面超越现有方法，极坐标位置编码对跨锚点推理和狭窄通道安全选择至关重要，验证了自注意力机制在无人机局部规划中的有效性。

Abstract: Agile unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) navigation in cluttered environments demands a planning architecture that is both computationally efficient and structurally expressive enough to reason over multiple feasible motions. This paper presents SAGA, a robust self-attention and goal-aware anchor-based planner for safe UAV autonomous navigation. SAGA formulates local planning as a one-stage joint regression-and-ranking problem over a fixed lattice of motion anchors. Given a depth image and a body-frame motion state, the planner predicts refined terminal states and planning scores for all anchors in a single forward pass, after which the best candidate is decoded into a dynamically feasible trajectory. The key idea of SAGA is to transform anchor-aligned features into geometry-aware tokens and perform cross-anchor global reasoning with self-attention. To preserve directional structure in the token space, we further introduce a polar positional encoding derived from anchor yaw and pitch. In addition, a goal-aware modulation module injects velocity, acceleration, and target information into the token representation before final score prediction. Experiments in cluttered pillar-map environments under maximum speed settings of 2.0, 3.0, and 4.0~m/s show that SAGA consistently achieves a 100\% success rate, while YOPO drops from 90.91\% to 62.50\%, Ego-planner from 71.43\% to 52.63\%, and Fast-planner from 52.63\% to 38.46\%. Under the 4.0~m/s maximum speed setting, SAGA also improves average safety from 1.9843~m to 2.3888~m and minimum safety from 0.4390~m to 0.7576~m over YOPO, while reducing total flight time from 40.4631~s to 27.4901~s. The comparison with SAGA w/o PPE further shows that explicit polar positional encoding is critical for stable cross-anchor reasoning and safe passage selection in cluttered scenes.

</details>


### [124] [Natural Gradient Bayesian Filtering: Geometry-Aware Filter for Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02306)
*Chang Liu,Wenhan Cao,Zeju Sun,Tianyi Zhang,Jiayu Yuan,Yi Zeng,Ting Yuan,Yao Lyu,Wei Wu,Stephen Shing-Toung Yau,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文从信息几何视角重新审视高斯滤波，提出了一种基于自然梯度下降的NANO（Natural Gradient Gaussian Approximation）滤波器，该滤波器在保持协方差矩阵正定性的同时迭代优化后验均值和协方差，并在线性高斯情形下退化为经典卡尔曼滤波。通过在卫星姿态估计、SLAM及四足/人形机器人状态估计等非线性问题中的验证，展示了该方法的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯滤波是非线性系统状态估计的核心，但精确解仅存在于线性高斯模型。现有高斯滤波方法（如EKF、UKF）虽广泛应用，但未充分利用高斯分布族的几何结构，导致近似精度有限。本文旨在通过引入信息几何视角，提出一种尊重高斯族内在几何结构的滤波方法，以提升非线性状态估计的性能。

Method: 将预测和测量更新步骤视为对状态分布的推断过程，在高斯分布统计流形上引入自然梯度下降（Natural Gradient Descent）来迭代优化后验均值和协方差，形成NANO滤波器。该方法通过信息几何中的Fisher信息度规自然保持协方差的正定性，且理论上证明单个自然梯度步长在线性高斯情形下等价于经典卡尔曼滤波的测量更新。

Result: 在卫星姿态估计、SLAM、四足机器人及人形机器人状态估计等代表性非线性问题上验证了NANO滤波器的有效性。理论分析表明NANO与经典卡尔曼滤波存在深刻联系，线性高斯情形下单步自然梯度精确恢复卡尔曼更新。

Conclusion: 信息几何视角为高斯滤波提供了新的理论框架，NANO滤波器在保持计算效率的同时，通过几何感知的迭代优化提升了非线性状态估计的精度，并在多个机器人及航空航天系统中展现出实际应用价值。

Abstract: Bayesian filtering is a cornerstone of state estimation in complex systems such as aerospace systems, yet exact solutions are available only for linear Gaussian models. In practice,nonlinear systems are handled through tractable approximations,with Gaussian filters such as the extended and unscented Kalman filters being among the most widely used methods. This tutorial revisits Gaussian filtering from an information-geometric perspective, viewing the prediction and measurement update steps as inference procedures over state distributions. Within this framework, we introduce a geometry-aware Gaussian filtering approach that leverages natural gradient descent on the statistical manifold of Gaussian distributions. The resulting Natural Gradient Gaussian Approximation (NANO) filter iteratively refines the posterior mean and covariance while respecting the intrinsic geometry of the Gaussian family and preserving the positive definiteness of the covariance matrix. We further highlight fundamental connections to the classical Kalman filtering, showing that a single natural-gradient step exactly recovers the Kalman measurement update in the linear-Gaussian case. The practical implications of the proposed framework are illustrated through case studies in representative nonlinear estimation problems,including satellite attitude estimation, simultaneous localization and mapping, and state estimation for robotic systems including quadruped and humanoid robots.

</details>


### [125] [ShapeGrasp: Simultaneous Visuo-Haptic Shape Completion and Grasping for Improved Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02347)
*Lukas Rustler,Matej Hoffmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ShapeGrasp是一种迭代抓取与形状补全管线，通过融合初始视觉估计与抓取过程中获得的触觉和 proprioceptive 反馈，实现了对未知物体的鲁棒抓取和3D形状重建。在真实世界实验中，三指和两指机械手的抓取成功率分别达到84%和91%，同时提升了形状重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类通过视觉初始估计与触觉/本体感觉反馈结合来抓取陌生物体，现有机器人方法缺乏在抓取过程中利用实际接触信息更新形状表示的能力。本文旨在填补这一空白，实现首次在真实世界抓取后更新形状表示的机器人系统。

Method: 提出ShapeGrasp迭代抓取-补全管线：(1) 从单张RGB-D图像推断完整形状（点云或三角网格）；(2) 通过刚体仿真生成候选抓取并执行最佳可行抓取；(3) 每次抓取尝试后，融合触觉表面接触和夹爪占用空间等几何约束来更新物体形状；(4) 失败时利用优化后的形状进行位姿重估计和重新抓取。核心是隐式表面视觉-触觉形状补全与基于物理的抓取规划相耦合。

Result: 在真实世界中用两种不同机器人和夹爪进行评估：三指夹爪抓取成功率84%，两指夹爪91%，均优于基线方法；在所有评估指标上均提升了3D形状重建质量。这是首个在真实世界抓取后更新形状表示的方法。

Conclusion: 通过将触觉和本体感觉反馈融入形状补全过程，ShapeGrasp显著提升了机器人在未知物体上的抓取鲁棒性和形状重建精度，证明了视觉-触觉融合在机器人操作中的有效性。

Abstract: Humans grasp unfamiliar objects by combining an initial visual estimate with tactile and proprioceptive feedback during interaction. We present ShapeGrasp, a robotic implementation of this approach. The proposed method is an iterative grasp-and-complete pipeline that couples implicit surface visuo-haptic shape completion (creation of full 3D shape from partial information) with physics-based grasp planning. From a single RGB-D view, ShapeGrasp infers a complete shape (point cloud or triangular mesh), generates candidate grasps via rigid-body simulation, and executes the best feasible grasp. Each grasp attempt yields additional geometric constraints -- tactile surface contacts and space occupied by the gripper body -- which are fused to update the object shape. Failures trigger pose re-estimation and regrasping using the refined shape. We evaluate ShapeGrasp in the real world using two different robots and grippers. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first approach that updates shape representations following a real-world grasp. We achieved superior results over baselines for both grippers (grasp success rate of 84% with a three-finger gripper and 91% with a two-finger gripper), while improving the 3D shape reconstruction quality in all evaluation metrics used.

</details>


### [126] [Feedback Motion Planning for Stochastic Nonlinear Systems with Signal Temporal Logic Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02361)
*Liqian Ma,Zishun Liu,Glen Chou,Yongxin Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于谓词侵蚀策略的反馈运动规划框架，利用收缩理论和概率可达管将随机STL轨迹优化问题转化为确定性优化问题，并通过数值优化实现闭环随机系统以高概率满足STL规范，在仿真和真实四足机器人实验中验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决连续时间随机非线性系统在信号时序逻辑规范下的反馈运动规划问题，现有方法难以处理随机性带来的概率约束，且存在过于保守的问题。

Method: 采用谓词侵蚀策略将带机会约束的随机STL轨迹优化问题转化为确定性的STL轨迹优化问题；利用概率可达管（PRT）结合收缩理论和反馈控制器设计来量化随机轨迹与标称轨迹之间的偏差，从而确定侵蚀量。

Result: 在多个机器人系统仿真和真实四足机器人实验中，所提框架相比代表性基线方法保守性更低，且实现了更高的规范满足概率（如99.99%）。

Conclusion: 所提出的反馈运动规划框架能够有效处理随机非线性系统在STL规范下的概率约束问题，通过数值优化即可实现，具备良好的实用性和扩展性。

Abstract: We study feedback motion planning for continuous-time stochastic nonlinear systems under signal temporal logic (STL) specifications. We propose a framework that synthesizes control policies for chance-constrained STL trajectory optimization problems, with the goal of ensuring that the closed-loop stochastic system satisfies a given STL formula with high probability (e.g., 99.99\%). Our approach is based on a predicate erosion strategy that transforms the intractable stochastic problem into a deterministic STL trajectory optimization problem with tightened STL formula constraints. The amount of erosion is determined by a probabilistic reachable tube (PRT) that bounds the deviation between the stochastic trajectory and an associated nominal trajectory. To compute such bounds, we leverage contraction theory and feedback design, and develop several tracking controllers. This yields a complete feedback motion planning pipeline which can be implemented by numerical optimizations. We demonstrate the efficacy and versatility of the proposed framework through simulations on several robotic systems and through experiments on a real-world quadrupedal robot, and show that it is less conservative and achieves higher specification satisfaction probability than representative baselines.

</details>


### [127] [Robust Adaptive Predictive Control for Hook-Based Aerial Transportation Between Moving Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02370)
*Péter Antal,Andrea Carron,Melanie Zeilinger,Roland Tóth,Tamás Péni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于鲁棒自适应模型预测控制（MPC）的方法，用于挂载钩爪的空中机械臂在移动平台间自主抓取与放置任务。通过MuJoCo构建四旋翼数字孪生模型作为预测模型，结合零阶鲁棒优化（zoRO）不确定性传播与扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）参数估计，实现了对气动及负载不确定性的鲁棒约束满足与高效计算。仿真与实物飞行实验验证了该方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现空中机械臂在移动平台间的自主抓取与放置，需要应对复杂动力学建模精度、气动效应及负载变化带来的模型不确定性，现有方法难以同时保证鲁棒性、性能与计算效率。

Method: 提出鲁棒自适应MPC框架：1）利用MuJoCo构建四旋翼数字孪生模型作为MPC预测模型；2）集成zoRO进行不确定性传播以处理模型误差；3）引入EKF在线估计不确定参数，实现自适应调整。

Result: 在复杂仿真场景与真实飞行实验中验证了所提方法，结果表明该方法能有效处理模型不确定性，保证约束满足，并具有较高的控制性能与计算效率。

Conclusion: 所提鲁棒自适应MPC方法在挂载钩爪空中机械臂的移动平台抓取任务中表现优异，为存在显著模型不确定性的空中操作问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel model predictive control (MPC) approach for autonomous pick-and-place between moving platforms with a hook-equipped aerial manipulator. First, for accurate and rapid modeling of the complex dynamics, a digital twin model of the quadcopter equipped with a hook-based gripper, implemented in MuJoCo, is constructed and used as the predictive model for the MPC. To handle uncertainties of the predictive model (e.g. due to aerodynamics and uncertain payloads), a robust adaptive MPC approach is proposed. By systematic integration of zero-order robust optimization (zoRO) based uncertainty propagation and an extended Kalman filter (EKF) for parameter estimation, the MPC algorithm ensures robust constraint satisfaction, high performance, and computational efficiency. The effectiveness of the proposed method is evaluated in complex simulated scenarios and in real-world flight experiments.

</details>


### [128] [Shared Autonomy Assisted by Impedance-Driven Anisotropic Guidance Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02410)
*Sihan Chen,Hang Xu,Yupu Lu,Chen Wang,Benfang Duan,Ruixing Jia,Jia Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出IAGF-SA范式，通过基于阻抗控制的各向异性引导场，在共享自主系统中建立具身化、物理性的通信通道，使机器人能够直观、连续地向人类传达意图，从而改善人机协作效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共享自主系统主要关注提升机器人对人类意图的推断能力，却忽略了人类对机器人意图的理解。缺乏这种双向理解会导致协作效率下降，影响用户体验和任务表现。已有的显式意图传达方法依赖额外界面，不够直观且表达能力有限。

Method: 受阻抗控制启发，提出阻抗驱动各向异性引导场增强共享自主（IAGF-SA）范式。该方法通过自适应调节机器人对人类输入的动态响应，建立一种具身化、物理基础的通信通道，实现直观、连续、物理基础的机器人意图传达，同时自然引导人类行为。

Result: 在三种场景和两种遥操作界面下的用户研究表明，IAGF-SA能够提升任务表现、人机一致性以及主观体验，有效增强了人机通信与协作。

Conclusion: IAGF-SA作为一种新型共享自主范式，通过物理基础的意图通信通道有效解决了人机双向理解缺失的问题，在提升共享自主系统的人机协作效果方面具有显著优势。

Abstract: Shared autonomy (SA) enables robots to infer human intent and assist in its achievement. While most research focuses on improving intent inference, it overlooks whether humans can understand the robot's intent in return. Without such mutual understanding, collaboration becomes less effective, degrading user experience and task performance. To address this gap, previous studies have explicitly conveyed the robot intent through additional interfaces, which remain unintuitive and limited in expressiveness. Inspired by impedance control, we propose Impedance-Driven Anisotropic Guidance Field Enhanced Shared Autonomy (IAGF-SA), a novel paradigm that extends SA with an embodied, physically-grounded communication channel. This channel adaptively modulates the robot's dynamic response to human input, enabling intuitive, continuous, physically-grounded robot intent communication while naturally guiding human actions. User studies across three scenarios and two teleoperation interfaces indicate that IAGF-SA improves task performance, human-robot agreement, and subjective experience, thus demonstrating its effectiveness in enhancing human-robot communication and collaboration.

</details>


### [129] [Higher-Order Flexible Configurations of Planar Parallel Manipulators Constructed by Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02434)
*Yudi Zhao,Georg Nawratil*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究平面3-RPR并联机器人的奇异位形，通过参数化输入对并确定其相对方向，在不计算六次多项式零点的情况下提高平均构型的屈曲阶数，并进行了可视化验证。该方法可推广到球面和空间类似机构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究平面3-RPR并联机器人中由直接运动学问题解对应用平均技术产生的奇异构型，并寻求在不求解高次多项式零点的情况下分析这些奇异构型的方法。

Method: 对输入对进行参数化，确定其相对方向，使得平均构型的屈曲阶数增加；避免直接计算对应的六次多项式的零点。

Result: 成功实现了输入对的参数化与相对方向确定，通过具体算例进行了可视化展示，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法不仅适用于平面3-RPR并联机器人，还可以推广到球面和空间类似机构的奇异构型研究。

Abstract: This paper investigates singular configurations of planar 3-RPR parallel manipulators, which result from applying the averaging technique to solution pairs of their direct kinematic problem. Without computing the zeros of the corresponding degree 6 polynomial we parametrize the input pairs and determine their relative orientation in a way that the flexion order of the averaged configurations increases. Moreover, the obtained results are visualized for concrete examples. The presented methodology can also be used for studying the spherical and spatial analogues of planar 3-RPR parallel manipulators.

</details>


### [130] [Visibility-Aware Mobile Grasping in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02487)
*Tianrun Hu,Anxing Xiao,David Hsu,Hanbo Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统一移动抓取系统，包含迭代低层全身规划器（结合速度感知主动感知）和基于行为树的分层高层规划器，在未知动态环境中实现了68.8%和58.0%的成功率，比现有方法分别提升22.8%和18.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人在动态未知环境中进行抓取时，面临视野受限下“观察”与“移动”之间的根本性权衡，现有方法常假设环境已知或静态，并将目标解耦，无法保证当未观测的动态障碍物在操作过程中与机器人路径相交时的安全性。

Method: 提出统一移动抓取系统，包含两个核心组件：(1) 迭代低层全身规划器，结合速度感知主动感知，在动态环境中安全导航；(2) 基于行为树的分层高层规划器，自适应生成子目标，引导机器人进行探索和处理运行时失败。

Result: 在400个随机化仿真场景和真实的Fetch移动操作手上部署实验，在未知静态和动态环境中的成功率分别为68.8%和58.0%，相比现有方法在两种环境下分别提升22.8%和18.0%，碰撞安全性也有改善。

Conclusion: 提出的统一移动抓取系统在未知静态和动态环境中显著提升了成功率和碰撞安全性，有效解决了视野受限下移动抓取中观察与移动之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of mobile grasping in dynamic, unknown environments where a robot must operate under a limited field-of-view. The fundamental challenge is the inherent trade-off between ``seeing'' around to reduce environmental uncertainty and ``moving'' the body to achieve task progress in a high-dimensional configuration space, subject to visibility constraints. Previous approaches often assume known or static environments and decouple these objectives, failing to guarantee safety when unobserved dynamic obstacles intersect the robot's path during manipulation. In this paper, we propose a unified mobile grasping system comprising two core components: (1) an iterative low-level whole-body planner coupled with velocity-aware active perception to navigate dynamic environments safely; and (2) a hierarchical high-level planner based on behavior trees that adaptively generates subgoals to guide the robot through exploration and runtime failures. We provide experimental results across 400 randomized simulation scenarios and real-world deployment on a Fetch mobile manipulator. Results show that our system achieves a success rate of 68.8\% and 58.0\% in unknown static and dynamic environments, respectively, significantly boosting success rates by 22.8\% and 18.0\% over the \nam approach in both unknown static and dynamic environments, with improved collision safety.

</details>


### [131] [Adaptive Gait Generation for Multi-Terrain Exoskeletons via Constrained Kernelized Movement Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02513)
*Edoardo Trombin,Miroljub Mihailovic,Matheus Henrique Ferreira Moura,Luca Tonin,Emanuele Menegatti,Stefano Tortora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于核化运动基元（KMP）的自适应步态生成框架，使下肢外骨骼能够在多种室内地形（平地、斜坡、楼梯、障碍物）上实现环境感知的实时步态规划。该方法从少量人类示教中学习步态的概率表示，并结合RGB-D相机提取的环境信息，通过线性约束优化生成生理一致的运动轨迹。仿真和真实外骨骼实验验证了该方法的有效性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有下肢外骨骼仅在平坦地形上有效，缺乏环境感知的自适应步态规划能力，无法在真实复杂环境中应用。

Method: 采用核化运动基元（KMP）从少量人类示教中学习步态在关节空间和任务空间中的概率表示，利用RGB-D相机提取环境信息，将自适应步态生成建模为带经过点（via-points）的线性约束优化问题。

Result: 在平地、斜坡、楼梯、障碍物穿越等多种场景的仿真和商业下肢外骨骼真实实验中，验证了该方法的有效性和鲁棒性，实现了环境感知的步态规划。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了一种面向智能下肢外骨骼的环境感知步态规划系统的可行性，有望辅助运动障碍者在日常生活中行走。

Abstract: Lower limb exoskeletons (LLEs) present the potential to make motor-impaired individuals walk again. Their application in real-world environments is still limited by the lack of effective adaptive gait planning. Indeed, current exoskeletons are meant to walk only on a flat and even terrain. Generating environment-aware, physiologically consistent gait trajectories in real-time is an open challenge. To overcome this, we propose a novel Kernelized Movement Primitives (KMP)-based framework for adaptive gait generation (AGG) across multiple indoor terrains. The proposed approach learns a probabilistic representation of human gait in both the joint and task spaces from a limited number of human demonstrations, representing natural gait characteristics and ensuring kinematic feasibility. In addition, the learned trajectories are adapted using environmental information extracted from an onboard RGB-D camera by treating the AGG as a linearly constrained optimization problem with via-points. The proposed method has been thoroughly validated first in simulations for gait generation in different scenarios, such as flat-ground walking, slopes, stairs, and obstacles crossing. Finally, the effectiveness and robustness of the method have been demonstrated with experiments on a commercial LLE in real-world scenarios. The results obtained demonstrate the feasibility of an environment-aware gait planning system for a new generation of intelligent lower limb exoskeletons for assisting people with disabilities in their every-day life.

</details>


### [132] [A Semantic Autonomy Framework for VLM-Integrated Indoor Mobile Robots: Hybrid Deterministic Reasoning and Cross-Robot Adaptive Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02525)
*Bogdan Felician Abaza,Andrei-Alexandru Staicu,Cristian Vasile Doicin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出语义自主栈（Semantic Autonomy Stack），一种六层参考框架，通过混合确定性-VLM推理和跨机器人自适应记忆，使移动机器人能够理解自然语言指令。系统在Raspberry Pi 5上实现：88%指令在0.1毫秒内通过参数解析器处理，仅歧义指令升级到VLM推理；五类语义记忆框架支持跨会话学习和跨机器人知识迁移，实验验证100%语义迁移准确率和零训练数据需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主移动机器人（如基于ROS 2 Navigation 2的系统）能够可靠导航到公制坐标，但无法理解表达意图而非位置的自然语言指令。视觉-语言模型（VLM）提供了语义推理能力，但其推理延迟高（消费硬件上每次决策2-9秒）和会话级遗忘问题限制了实际部署。

Method: 提出语义自主栈（Semantic Autonomy Stack）六层参考框架；实现七步参数化解析器（处理88%指令，<0.1毫秒，无需VLM/相机/GPU）；设计五类语义记忆框架（全局环境知识、每操作员偏好、每机器人能力），支持跨会话学习和跨机器人知识迁移；采用混合确定性-VLM推理架构。

Result: 在82个场景级决策和3个会话中，实现100%语义迁移准确率（33/33, 95% CI [0.894, 1.000]），100%语义解析准确率；偏好从VLM交互提升至确定性解析，通过共享编译摘要实现103,000倍延迟降低；所有计算在Raspberry Pi 5上完成，无需GPU和训练数据。

Conclusion: 语义自主栈有效弥合了公制导航与自然语言理解之间的差距，通过混合推理和自适应记忆机制在低功耗边缘硬件上实现了高效、可迁移的语义导航，为零训练数据的多机器人协同语义导航提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Autonomous indoor mobile robots can navigate reliably to metric coordinates using established frameworks such as ROS 2 Navigation 2, yet they lack the ability to interpret natural language instructions that express intent rather than positions. Vision-Language Models offer the semantic reasoning required to bridge this gap, but their inference latency (2-9 seconds per decision on consumer hardware) and session-by-session amnesia limit practical deployment. This paper presents the Semantic Autonomy Stack, a six-layer reference framework for semantically autonomous indoor navigation, and validates a complete instance featuring hybrid deterministic-VLM reasoning and cross-robot adaptive memory on physical robots with off-the-shelf edge hardware. A seven-step parametric resolver handles 88% of instructions in under 0.1 milliseconds without invoking a language model, camera, or GPU; only genuinely ambiguous instructions escalate to VLM reasoning. A five-category semantic memory framework with explicit scope taxonomy (global environment knowledge, per-operator preferences, per-robot capabilities) enables cross-session learning and cross-robot knowledge transfer: preferences learned through VLM interactions on one robot are promoted to deterministic resolution and transferred to a second robot via a shared compiled digest, achieving a measured latency reduction of 103,000-fold. Experimental validation on two custom-built differential-drive robots across 82 scenario-level decisions and three sessions demonstrates 100% semantic transfer accuracy (33/33, 95% CI [0.894, 1.000]), 100% semantic resolution accuracy, and concurrent multi-robot operation feasibility - all on Raspberry Pi 5 platforms with no onboard GPU, requiring zero training data.

</details>


### [133] [Sim-to-Real Transfer and Robustness Evaluation of Reinforcement Learning Control with Integrated Perception on an ASV for Floating Waste Capture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02529)
*Luis F. W. Batista,Stéphanie Aravecchia,Cédric Pradalier*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合偏振视觉感知与轻量级深度强化学习控制器的自主水面垃圾清理系统，通过仿真到现实的测试方法，在14种扰动条件下实现了厘米级末端精度，并在实际场景中成功验证了450平方米范围内的搜索与捕获任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主水面垃圾清理船面临复杂水动力学、外部扰动和水面感知困难等挑战，现有方法缺乏可重复的现场测试手段以及对仿真到现实迁移鸿沟的系统性评估。

Method: 采用基于相机的偏振感知模块检测水面漂浮垃圾，将其转换为水面目标点；使用完全在仿真中训练的轻量级DRL控制器；提出两阶段仿真协议与感知抽象模块，模拟真实相机行为，构建完整的仿真到现实测试框架。

Result: 在14种扰动条件下的配对仿真与现场实验中实现厘米级末端精度；在450平方米真实场景中成功完成基于真实相机检测的搜索与捕获任务；发现性能下降的主要原因是执行器建模精度不足。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效暴露故障模式并评估鲁棒性；实际部署的关键经验包括提高执行器建模精度、引入针对性域随机化、仔细管理跨模块的延迟与时间戳等。

Abstract: Autonomous surface vessels for floating-waste removal operate under varying hydrodynamics, external disturbances, and challenging water-surface perception. We present a field-validated system that combines camera-based polarimetric perception with a lightweight DRL-based controller for floating-waste detection and capture. Camera detections are converted into water-surface target points and tracked by a controller trained entirely in simulation and deployed directly on a retrofitted ASV platform. Our main contribution is a sim-to-real testing methodology that combines a two-stage simulation protocol with a perception abstraction module designed to mimic real camera behavior, enabling reproducible field trials and explicit evaluation of the sim-to-real gap. We apply this framework in matched simulation and field experiments across 14 disturbance regimes to expose failure modes and evaluate robustness. The results show centimeter-level terminal accuracy and indicate robust control performance under the evaluated perturbation regimes. The main source of degradation is insufficient actuation-model fidelity. We also demonstrate the system in a search-and-capture application using real camera detections in real-world conditions over areas of up to $450~m^2$. The study distills practical lessons for reliable transfer, including improved actuation-model fidelity, targeted domain randomization, and careful management of latency and timestamps across modules, while highlighting remaining challenges.

</details>


### [134] [Orchestrating Spatial Semantics via a Zone-Graph Paradigm for Intricate Indoor Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02537)
*Meisheng Zhang,Shizhao Sun,Yang Zhao,Ziyuan Liu,Zhijun Gao,Jiang Bian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ZoneMaestro 提出了一种基于区域图编排（Zone-Graph Orchestration）的统一框架，将3D室内场景合成从以物体为中心转向以功能区域和拓扑约束为中心，配合新数据集 Zone-Scene-10K 和交替对齐策略（含 Z-GRPO），在非凸、不规则室内场景中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动方法缺乏拓扑先验，难以处理非凸房间中紧密耦合的空间约束；迭代式智能体则容易破坏语义一致性并产生几何脆弱性问题。

Method: 提出 ZoneMaestro 框架，核心包括：(1) Zone-Graph Orchestration，以功能区域和拓扑约束替代以物体为中心的合成范式；(2) 构建 Zone-Scene-10K 大规模数据集，附带显式的区域图标注；(3) 提出交替对齐策略（Alternating Alignment Strategy），在推理内化与区域感知组相对策略优化（Z-GRPO）之间交替循环，无需外部物理引擎。

Result: 在非凸、不规则室内场景中，ZoneMaestro 在结构连贯性和意图一致性上显著优于现有基线方法，有效解决了密度-安全性的二元对立问题。

Conclusion: 基于区域图编排的统一框架能有效应对复杂空间布局的挑战，为3D室内场景合成提供了一种兼顾语义丰富性与几何有效性的新范式。

Abstract: Autonomous 3D indoor scene synthesis breaks down in non-convex rooms with tightly coupled spatial constraints. Data-driven generators lack topological priors for long-horizon planning, while iterative agents fragment semantics and become geometrically brittle. We present ZoneMaestro, a unified framework that shifts the paradigm from object-centric synthesis to Zone-Graph Orchestration. By internalizing a novel zone-based logic, ZoneMaestro translates high-level semantic intent into functional zones and topological constraints, enabling robust adaptation to diverse architectural forms. To support this, we construct Zone-Scene-10K, a large-scale dataset enriched with explicit Zone-Graph annotations. We further introduce an Alternating Alignment Strategy that cycles between reasoning internalization and Zone-Aware Group Relative Policy Optimization (Z-GRPO), effectively reconciling the tension between semantic richness and geometric validity without relying on external physics engines. To rigorously evaluate spatial intelligence beyond convex primitives, we formally define the task of Intricate Spatial Orchestration and release SCALE, a stress-test benchmark for irregular indoor scenarios with complex, dense spatial relations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ZoneMaestro resolves the density-safety dichotomy, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art baselines in both structural coherence and intent adherence.

</details>


### [135] [CoRAL: Contact-Rich Adaptive LLM-based Control for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02600)
*Berk Çiçek,Mert K. Er,Özgür S. Öğüz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了CoRAL框架，通过将LLM/VLM的高层推理与低层控制解耦，利用LLM作为成本函数设计器、VLM提供语义先验，结合在线系统辨识和检索记忆单元，实现了接触丰富操作的零样本规划，在仿真和真实场景中成功率平均提升超50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM和VLM在高层次推理和语义理解上表现优异，但缺乏明确的物理基础，无法执行自适应控制，难以直接应用于接触丰富的机器人操作任务。

Method: 采用模块化框架，将LLM/VLM作为成本函数设计器而非直接控制器，为基于采样的运动规划器(MPPI)合成上下文感知的目标函数；引入神经符号自适应循环，VLM提供物理参数语义先验后通过在线系统辨识精炼，LLM基于交互反馈迭代调整成本函数结构；配备检索记忆单元以复用成功策略。

Result: 在仿真和真实硬件上验证，CoRAL在未见过的接触丰富场景中平均成功率提升超过50%，优于SOTA的VLA和基于基础模型的规划器基线，有效处理了sim-to-real差距。

Conclusion: 提出的层次化架构通过将高层语义推理与反应式执行解耦，弥合了慢速LLM推理与动态接触需求之间的差距，实现了零样本规划并具备自适应物理理解能力。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs) demonstrate remarkable capabilities in high-level reasoning and semantic understanding, applying them directly to contact-rich manipulation remains a challenge due to their lack of explicit physical grounding and inability to perform adaptive control. To bridge this gap, we propose CoRAL (Contact-Rich Adaptive LLM-based control), a modular framework that enables zero-shot planning by decoupling high-level reasoning from low-level control. Unlike black-box policies, CoRAL uses LLMs not as direct controllers, but as cost designers that synthesize context-aware objective functions for a sampling-based motion planner (MPPI). To address the ambiguity of physical parameters in visual data, we introduce a neuro-symbolic adaptation loop: a VLM provides semantic priors for environmental dynamics, such as mass and friction estimates, which are then explicitly refined in real time via online system identification, while the LLM iteratively modulates the cost-function structure to correct strategic errors based on interaction feedback. Furthermore, a retrieval-based memory unit allows the system to reuse successful strategies across recurrent tasks. This hierarchical architecture ensures real-time control stability by decoupling high-level semantic reasoning from reactive execution, effectively bridging the gap between slow LLM inference and dynamic contact requirements. We validate CoRAL on both simulation and real-world hardware across challenging and novel tasks, such as flipping objects against walls by leveraging extrinsic contacts. Experiments demonstrate that CoRAL outperforms state-of-the-art VLA and foundation-model-based planner baselines by boosting success rates over 50% on average in unseen contact-rich scenarios, effectively handling sim-to-real gaps through its adaptive physical understanding.

</details>


### [136] [AnchorD: Metric Grounding of Monocular Depth Using Factor Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02667)
*Simon Dorer,Martin Büchner,Nick Heppert,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AnchorDepth，一种无需训练的深度接地框架，通过因子图优化将单目深度估计先验与原始传感器深度对齐，在非朗伯体表面场景中显著提升深度估计精度，并提供了包含密集真实深度标注的基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度传感器在透明、镜面及非朗伯体表面存在严重误差，而大规模单目深度估计方法虽提供结构先验但度量单位可能偏差，限制了在机器人领域的直接应用。

Method: 提出无需训练的深度接地框架，通过因子图优化对单目深度基础模型的预测进行分块仿射对齐，局部地将单目预测接地到公制真实世界深度，同时保留精细几何结构和边缘不连续性。

Result: 在多种传感器和不同领域的广泛评估中，无需任何重新训练即持续改进深度估计性能；同时构建了包含非朗伯体物体密集场景深度真值的基准数据集。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效融合了单目深度先验与传感器原始深度，在无需训练的条件下提升了复杂场景中的深度估计精度，对机器人操作和导航具有实用价值。

Abstract: Dense and accurate depth estimation is essential for robotic manipulation, grasping, and navigation, yet currently available depth sensors are prone to errors on transparent, specular, and general non-Lambertian surfaces. To mitigate these errors, large-scale monocular depth estimation approaches provide strong structural priors, but their predictions can be potentially skewed or mis-scaled in metric units, limiting their direct use in robotics. Thus, in this work, we propose a training-free depth grounding framework that anchors monocular depth estimation priors from a depth foundation model in raw sensor depth through factor graph optimization. Our method performs a patch-wise affine alignment, locally grounding monocular predictions in metric real-world depth while preserving fine-grained geometric structure and discontinuities. To facilitate evaluation in challenging real-world conditions, we introduce a benchmark dataset with dense scene-wide ground truth depth in the presence of non-Lambertian objects. Ground truth is obtained via matte reflection spray and multi-camera fusion, overcoming the reliance on object-only CAD-based annotations used in prior datasets. Extensive evaluations across diverse sensors and domains demonstrate consistent improvements in depth performance without any (re-)training. We make our implementation publicly available at https://anchord.cs.uni-freiburg.de.

</details>


### [137] [Temporally Consistent Object 6D Pose Estimation for Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02708)
*Kateryna Zorina,Vojtech Priban,Mederic Fourmy,Josef Sivic,Vladimir Petrik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于因子图的方法，通过引入物体运动模型、显式估计测量不确定性以及在线优化估计，增强单视图RGB物体姿态估计的时间一致性，显著提升标准基准测试结果，并在机器人反馈控制任务中验证了其稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单视图RGB物体姿态估计方法虽然精度和效率已达到可用于视觉机器人控制的水准，但缺乏时间一致性和鲁棒性，无法满足稳定反馈控制的需求。

Method: 采用因子图方法，融合物体运动模型、在线估计姿态测量不确定性，并结合离群点剔除与平滑策略，在在线优化框架下实现时间一致的姿态估计。

Result: 在标准化姿态估计基准测试上显著提升了结果，并在基于反馈的机器人控制任务（相机安装在扭矩控制机械臂上跟踪物体）中验证了稳定性。

Conclusion: 因子图方法能有效增强姿态估计的时间一致性，为基于视觉的机器人反馈控制提供鲁棒且稳定的姿态输入。

Abstract: Single-view RGB object pose estimators have reached a level of precision and efficiency that makes them good candidates for vision-based robot control. However, off-the-shelf methods lack temporal consistency and robustness that are mandatory for a stable feedback control. In this work, we develop a factor graph approach to enforce temporal consistency of the object pose estimates. In particular, the proposed approach: (i) incorporates object motion models, (ii) explicitly estimates the object pose measurement uncertainty, and (iii) integrates the above two components in an online optimization-based estimator. We demonstrate that with appropriate outlier rejection and smoothing using the proposed factor graph approach, we can significantly improve the results on standardized pose estimation benchmarks. We experimentally validate the stability of the proposed approach for a feedback-based robot control task in which the object is tracked by the camera attached to a torque controlled manipulator.

</details>


### [138] [Tensegrity crutches with compliance from a pre-stressed self-tensile module improve ground reaction force profiles, speed, effort, comfort, and perceived stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02710)
*Jingxian Gu,Joanna Spyra,Andrew Walski,Lyla Elsaesser,Samuel Bierner,Dobromir Dotov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究设计了一种基于张拉整体结构的仿生拐杖，通过与刚性拐杖和弹簧拐杖的机械测试和人体试验对比，发现张拉整体拐杖在降低峰值载荷率、提升舒适度、减少用力感和改善可用性方面表现更优，非线性刚度、地面跟随和力反馈是其性能提升的关键机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国有600万人使用拐杖作为移动辅助工具，但传统刚性设计缺乏轴向移动性，限制了感觉反馈并易导致上肢关节继发性损伤；弹簧加载设计虽提供柔顺性但可能牺牲稳定性。因此需要一种兼具柔顺性和稳定性的新型拐杖设计。

Method: 设计了一种预张拉两单元张拉整体结构作为末端模块的仿生拐杖，与商用刚性和弹簧加载拐杖进行轴向加载机械测试、地面直线行走和转弯行走测试，并在18名健康年轻成年人中进行了人体试验，采用膝部阻滞模拟单侧下肢损伤，通过主观问卷评估用力感、舒适度、疼痛、稳定性和可用性。

Result: 与刚性设计相比，弹簧加载和张拉整体条件均降低了峰值载荷率；张拉整体设计在用力感、舒适度、疼痛和可用性方面均有改善；弹簧加载拐杖降低了感知稳定性和步行速度。

Conclusion: 仿生张拉整体拐杖是对现有设计的整体改进，其非线性刚度、地面跟随能力和力反馈等力学特性是性能提升的关键机制。

Abstract: Purpose: Six million people use crutches as mobile aids in the US. Rigid designs with no axial mobility limit sensory feedback and lead to secondary injury on the upper joints. Spring-loaded designs offer compliance but may compromise stability. We designed a biologically inspired tensegrity crutch with a compliant module aiming to achieve favorable mechanical properties. The terminal module was a pre-stressed self-tensile two-cell tensegrity structure. We compared the tensegrity crutch to commercial rigid and spring-loaded crutches in mechanical tests using axial loading, in overground straight and turning walking, and in participant experience. Methods: In human trials, healthy young adults (N=18) with no recent lower-body injury performed straight walking and turning trials at a comfortable self-selected pace. A knee blocker simulated unilateral injury of the dominant leg. After using each type of crutch, participants reported their perceived levels of effort, comfort, pain, stability, and usability. Results: Compared to the rigid design, both spring-loaded and tensegrity conditions reduced peak loading rates. The tensegrity design improved effort, comfort, pain, and usability. Spring-loaded crutches reduced perceived stability and walking speed. Conclusion: The biologically inspired tensegrity crutches were an overall improvement to existing designs. Simulations and mechanical testing suggest that nonlinear stiffness, ground-following, and force feedback are among the beneficial mechanical properties that underlie this improvement.

</details>


### [139] [Parking Assistance for Trailer-Truck Transport Vehicles Using Sensor Fusion and Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02716)
*George Alenchery,Thomas Jeske,Tova Quinones,Lentz Fortune,Tristan Lindo-Slones,Amber Jones,Jordan Fletcher*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向拖挂卡车自主泊车的集成框架，融合感知、运动规划、控制与基础设施感知，并通过改进开源A*路径规划仿真验证了铰接式车辆的路径规划能力，同时指出防折叠控制仍需进一步研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低速泊车场景对铰接式拖挂卡车而言是自动驾驶中最具挑战性的任务之一，现有研究主要集中在高速场景，缺乏针对性的系统级解决方案。

Method: 采用传感器融合、Hybrid A*路径规划、非线性模型预测控制(NMPC)与数据驱动泊车系统的组合方案，并在开源A*路径规划仿真中融入拖拉机-挂车运动学模型作为概念验证。

Result: 在命令行仿真环境中成功实现了铰接式车辆的路径规划，但防折叠(jackknife prevention)功能尚不完善，需进一步开发。

Conclusion: 系统级协调对实现可靠、可扩展的自主泊车方案至关重要，但铰接式车辆的防折叠控制仍是关键技术瓶颈。

Abstract: Autonomous driving technology has rapidly evolved over the past decade, offering significant improvements in transportation efficiency, safety, and cost reduction. While much of the progress has focused on highway driving and obstacle avoidance, low-speed maneuvers such as parking remain among the most difficult challenges for autonomous systems. This challenge is especially pronounced in trailer-truck transport vehicles due to their articulated motion and environmental constraints. This paper presents a proposed framework for autonomous truck parking that integrates perception, motion planning, control systems, and infrastructure awareness. By combining sensor fusion, Hybrid A* path planning, nonlinear model predictive control (NMPC), and data-driven parking systems, this work highlights the importance of system-level coordination for reliable and scalable autonomous parking solutions. As a proof-of-concept implementation, we adapted an open-source A* path planning simulation to incorporate a tractor-trailer kinematic model, demonstrating articulated vehicle path planning within a command-line simulation environment, with jackknife prevention identified as an area requiring further development.

</details>


### [140] [Semantic Risk-Aware Heuristic Planning for Robotic Navigation in Dynamic Environments: An LLM-Inspired Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02862)
*Hamza Ahmed Durrani,Rafay Suleman Durrani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种语义风险感知启发式（SRAH）规划器，将LLM启发的成本函数编码到A*搜索框架中，并结合闭环重规划。在200次随机试验中，SRAH的任务成功率为62.0%，优于BFS（56.5%）和Greedy（4.0%），表明轻量级LLM启发式方法能显著提升机器人导航的安全性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将大语言模型（LLM）的推理原则集成到经典机器人路径规划中是一个新兴研究方向，旨在利用语义理解提升路径规划的安全性和鲁棒性。

Method: 提出SRAH规划器，将LLM启发的成本函数（惩罚几何拥挤或高风险区域）编码到A*搜索框架中，并在检测到动态障碍物时进行闭环重规划。

Result: 在15×15网格世界（20%静态障碍物密度、随机动态障碍物）的200次随机试验中，SRAH成功率为62.0%，优于BFS（56.5%，相对提升9.7%）和Greedy（4.0%）。通过障碍物密度消融实验验证了语义成本塑形在不同环境难度下的一致性改进。

Conclusion: 即使轻量级的LLM启发式启发函数也能为自主机器人导航带来可测量的安全性和鲁棒性增益。

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning principles into classical robot path planning represents a rapidly emerging research direction. In this paper, we propose a Semantic Risk-Aware Heuristic (SRAH) planner that encodes LLM-inspired cost functions penalising geometrically cluttered or high-risk zones into an A$^*$ search framework, augmented with closed-loop replanning upon dynamic obstacle detection. We evaluate SRAH against two established baselines Breadth-First Search (BFS) with replanning and a Greedy heuristic without replanning across 200 randomised trials in a $15{\times}15$ grid-world with 20\% static obstacle density and stochastic dynamic obstacles. SRAH achieves a task success rate of 62.0\%, outperforming BFS (56.5\%) by 9.7\% relative improvement and Greedy (4.0\%) by a large margin. We further analyse the trade-off between planning overhead, path efficiency, and failure-recovery count, and demonstrate via an obstacle-density ablation that semantic cost shaping consistently improves navigation across environments of varying difficulty. Our results suggest that even lightweight, LLM-inspired heuristics provide measurable safety and robustness gains for autonomous robot navigation.

</details>


### [141] [MolmoAct2: Action Reasoning Models for Real-world Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02881)
*Haoquan Fang,Jiafei Duan,Donovan Clay,Sam Wang,Shuo Liu,Weikai Huang,Xiang Fan,Wei-Chuan Tsai,Shirui Chen,Yi Ru Wang,Shanli Xing,Jaemin Cho,Jae Sung Park,Ainaz Eftekhar,Peter Sushko,Karen Farley,Angad Wadhwa,Cole Harrison,Winson Han,Ying-Chun Lee,Eli VanderBilt,Rose Hendrix,Suveen Ellawela,Lucas Ngoo,Joyce Chai,Zhongzheng Ren,Ali Farhadi,Dieter Fox,Ranjay Krishna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MolmoAct2 是一个完全开放的动作推理模型，通过引入 MolmoER 视觉-语言骨干、大规模双机械臂数据集 MolmoAct2-BimanualYAM、开放动作分词器 OpenFAST、基于流匹配的连续动作专家架构以及自适应深度推理 MolmoThink，在 7 个仿真和真实世界基准上超越 Pi-05 等基线，MolmoER 在 13 个具身推理基准上超越 GPT-5 和 Gemini Robotics ER-1.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型在现实部署中存在诸多不足：闭源模型不可用、开源模型依赖昂贵硬件、推理增强策略延迟过高、微调成功率低。亟需一个完全开放、实用可部署的动作推理模型。

Method: 沿五个维度推进：(1) 提出 MolmoER 骨干，在 330 万样本上使用 specialize-then-rehearse 训练；(2) 发布三个新数据集，含 720 小时遥操作双机械臂轨迹；(3) 提供 OpenFAST 开放动作分词器；(4) 设计流匹配连续动作专家嫁接架构，通过逐层 KV 缓存条件化；(5) 提出 MolmoThink 自适应深度推理，仅重新预测场景变化区域的深度 token。

Result: 在 7 个仿真和真实世界基准上，MolmoAct2 超越 Pi-05 等强基线；MolmoER 在 13 个具身推理基准上超越 GPT-5 和 Gemini Robotics ER-1.5。

Conclusion: MolmoAct2 证明了开放 VLA 模型可以通过合理的架构设计和数据策略在性能和实用性上超越闭源模型，所有权重、代码和数据完全开源。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models aim to provide a single generalist controller for robots, but today's systems fall short on the criteria that matter for real-world deployment. Frontier models are closed, open-weight alternatives are tied to expensive hardware, reasoning-augmented policies pay prohibitive latency for their grounding, and fine-tuned success rates remain below the threshold for dependable use. We present MolmoAct2, a fully open action reasoning model built for practical deployment, advancing its predecessor along five axes. We introduce MolmoER, a VLM backbone specialized for spatial and embodied reasoning, trained on a 3.3M-sample corpus with a specialize-then-rehearse recipe. We release three new datasets spanning low-to-medium cost platforms, including MolmoAct2-BimanualYAM, 720 hours of teleoperated bimanual trajectories that constitute the largest open bimanual dataset to date, together with quality-filtered Franka (DROID) and SO100/101 subsets. We provide OpenFAST, an open-weight, open-data action tokenizer trained on millions of trajectories across five embodiments. We redesign the architecture to graft a flow-matching continuous-action expert onto a discrete-token VLM via per-layer KV-cache conditioning. Finally, we propose MolmoThink, an adaptive-depth reasoning variant that re-predicts depth tokens only for scene regions that change between timesteps, retaining geometric grounding at a fraction of prior latency. In the most extensive empirical study of any open VLA to date, spanning 7 simulation and real-world benchmarks, MolmoAct2 outperforms strong baselines including Pi-05, while MolmoER surpasses GPT-5 and Gemini Robotics ER-1.5 across 13 embodied-reasoning benchmarks. We release model weights, training code, and complete training data. Project page: https://allenai.org/blog/molmoact2

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [2026 Roadmap on Artificial Intelligence and Machine Learning for Smart Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00839)
*Jay Lee,Hanqi Su,Marco Macchi,Adalberto Polenghi,Wei Wu,Zhiheng Zhao,George Q. Huang,Kiva Allgood,Devendra Jain,Benedikt Gieger,Vibhor Pandhare,Soumyabrata Bhattacharjee,Ram Mohril,Lingbao Kong,Qiyuan Wang,Xinlan Tang,Sungjong Kim,Chan Hee Park,Byeng D. Youn,Guo Dong Goh,Xi Huang,Wai Yee Yeong,Yung C Shin,He Zhang,Zitong Wang,Fei Tao,Jagjit Singh Srai,Satyandra K. Gupta,Byung Gun Joung,Albin John,John W. Sutherland,Sang Won Lee,Olga Fink,Vinay Sharma,Faez Ahmed,Wei Chen,Mark Fuge,Arild Waaler,Martin G. Skjæveland,Dimitris Kyritsis,Wei Chen,VispiNevile Karkaria,Yi-Ping Chen,Ying-Kuan Tsai,Joseph Cohen,Xun Huan,Jing Lin,Liangwei Zhang,Gregory W. Vogl,Aaron W. Cornelius,Xiaodong Jia,Dai-Yan Ji,Takanobu Minami,Ruoxin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文全面综述了AI/ML在智能制造中的基础、应用与新兴方向，涵盖工业大数据分析、先进感知、自主系统、数字孪生、机器人、供应链优化及可持续制造等关键领域，并探讨了物理信息AI、生成式AI、语义AI、可解释AI、大语言模型等非传统ML前沿方法，同时指出了方法、集成策略和工业采纳方面所需的进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能制造中AI/ML的部署面临工业大数据复杂性、异构系统集成、以及高 stakes 工业环境对可信、可解释与可靠运行的迫切需求，亟需系统性的路线图来指导研究与工程实践。

Method: 采用结构化路线图形式，分为三部分：1) AI在智能制造中的基础与趋势；2) AI已推动进展的关键主题（大数据分析、先进感知、自主系统、增材/激光制造、数字孪生、机器人、供应链物流优化、可持续制造）；3) 新前沿的非传统ML方法（物理信息AI、生成式AI、语义AI、可解释AI、RAMS、数据中心计量学、LLMs与基础模型）。

Result: 识别出各领域的机遇与现存障碍，明确了在方法创新、集成策略和工业采纳三方面所需推进的方向。

Conclusion: 该路线图可为研究人员、工程师和从业者提供指导，以加速创新、对齐学术界与工业界优先级，确保AI驱动的智能制造为未来制造生态系统产生可靠、可持续且可扩展的影响。

Abstract: The evolution of artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) is reshaping smart manufacturing by providing new capabilities for efficiency, adaptability, and autonomy across industrial value chains. However, the deployment of AI and ML in industrial settings still faces critical challenges, including the complexity of industrial big data, effective data management, integration with heterogeneous sensing and control systems, and the demand for trustworthy, explainable, and reliable operation in high-stakes industrial environments. In this roadmap, we present a comprehensive perspective on the foundations, applications, and emerging directions of AI and ML in smart manufacturing. It is structured in three parts. The first highlights the foundations and trends that frame the evolution of AI in smart manufacturing. The second focuses on key topics where AI is already enabling advances, including industrial big data analytics, advanced sensing and perception, autonomous systems, additive and laser-based manufacturing, digital twins, robotics, supply chain and logistics optimization, and sustainable manufacturing. The third section explores non-traditional ML approaches that are opening new frontiers, such as physics-informed AI, generative AI, semantic AI, advanced digital twins, explainable AI, RAMS, data-centric metrology, LLMs, and foundation models for highly connected and complex manufacturing systems. By identifying both opportunities and remaining barriers across these areas, this roadmap outlines the advances needed in methods, integration strategies, and industrial adoption. We hope this roadmap will serve as a guide for researchers, engineers, and practitioners to accelerate innovation, align academic and industrial priorities, and ensure that AI-driven smart manufacturing delivers reliable, sustainable, and scalable impact for the future of manufacturing ecosystems.

</details>


### [143] [Understanding Emergent Misalignment via Feature Superposition Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00842)
*Gouki Minegishi,Hiroki Furuta,Takeshi Kojima,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于特征叠加几何的解释，阐明微调窄域无害任务为何会引发有害行为（涌现错位），并通过稀疏自编码器验证了特征间的几何邻近性，最后提出基于几何感知的样本过滤方法可有效缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涌现错位（emergent misalignment）现象中，对窄域无害任务进行微调会意外诱导出有害行为，这对大语言模型的安全性构成重大挑战。尽管已有大量实证证据，但其底层机制尚不清楚，本文旨在揭示这一现象的成因。

Method: 提出基于特征叠加几何的理论解释：由于特征以重叠表示形式编码，微调放大目标特征时会无意中增强邻近的有害特征。给出梯度级推导，并在多个LLM（Gemma-2 2B/9B/27B, LLaMA-3.1 8B, GPT-OSS 20B）上进行实证验证。使用稀疏自编码器识别与错位诱导数据和有害行为相关的特征，度量其几何距离。提出几何感知的样本过滤方法。

Result: 错位诱导数据的特征与有害行为特征在几何上比非诱导数据更接近，该趋势在多个领域（健康、职业、法律建议）泛化。几何感知的样本过滤方法将错位降低34.5%，显著优于随机移除，效果与基于LLM裁判的过滤方法相当或略优。

Conclusion: 涌现错位与特征叠加的几何结构存在因果关系，该几何理解为理解和缓解该现象提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Emergent misalignment, where fine-tuning on narrow, non-harmful tasks induces harmful behaviors, poses a key challenge for AI safety in LLMs. Despite growing empirical evidence, its underlying mechanism remains unclear. To uncover the reason behind this phenomenon, we propose a geometric account based on the geometry of feature superposition. Because features are encoded in overlapping representations, fine-tuning that amplifies a target feature also unintentionally strengthens nearby harmful features in accordance with their similarity. We give a simple gradient-level derivation of this effect and empirically test it in multiple LLMs (Gemma-2 2B/9B/27B, LLaMA-3.1 8B, GPT-OSS 20B). Using sparse autoencoders (SAEs), we identify features tied to misalignment-inducing data and to harmful behaviors, and show that they are geometrically closer to each other than features derived from non-inducing data. This trend generalizes across domains (e.g., health, career, legal advice). Finally, we show that a geometry-aware approach, filtering training samples closest to toxic features, reduces misalignment by 34.5%, substantially outperforming random removal and achieving comparable or slightly lower misalignment than LLM-as-a-judge-based filtering. Our study links emergent misalignment to feature superposition, providing a basis for understanding and mitigating this phenomenon.

</details>


### [144] [ClinicBot: A Guideline-Grounded Clinical Chatbot with Prioritized Evidence RAG and Verifiable Citations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00846)
*Navapat Nananukul,Mayank Kejriwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ClinicBot是一个AI系统，通过结构化提取临床指南、证据优先级排序和可验证界面，将指南建议转化为可信的临床支持，减少大模型的幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型语言模型在医疗场景中易产生幻觉，而传统RAG系统对所有证据同等对待，产生噪声上下文和与临床实践脱节的通用答案，亟需一种精准、可验证的临床诊断支持系统。

Method: (1) 将临床指南结构化为语义单元（建议、表格、定义、叙述）并附带显式来源；(2) 基于临床重要性和指南结构而非文本相似性对证据进行优先级排序；(3) 构建Web界面提供简洁、可操作的答案并附可验证证据。系统采用多智能体架构处理大规模临床指南。

Result: 将使用真实患者的糖尿病问题以及基于ADA 2025糖尿病护理标准的风险评估工具进行演示，展示语义知识提取和分层证据排序在多智能体设置下的可靠性。

Conclusion: ClinicBot通过结构化知识提取与分层证据排序，能够在高风险的临床场景中提供准确、可验证、基于指南的诊断支持，有效克服大语言模型的幻觉问题。

Abstract: Clinical diagnosis requires answers that are accurate, verifiable, and explicitly grounded in official guidelines. While large language models excel at natural language processing, their tendency to hallucinate undermines their utility in high-stakes medical contexts where precision is essential. Existing retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems treat all evidence equally, producing noisy context and generic answers misaligned with clinical practice. We present ClinicBot, an AI system that translates guideline recommendations into trustworthy clinical support through three key advances: (1) structured extraction of clinical guidelines into semantic units (recommendations, tables, definitions, narrative) with explicit provenance, (2) evidence prioritization that ranks content by clinical significance and guideline structure rather than textual similarity, and (3) a web-based interface that presents concise, actionable answers with verifiable evidence. We will demonstrate ClinicBot using diabetes questions from real patients and an additional diabetes risk assessment tool that is faithful to the American Diabetes Association (ADA) Standards of Care in Diabetes (2025).
