Existing approaches typically face a dichotomy: either relying on heavy-weight LLMs for intent parsing, leading to prohibitive latency (often exceeding 30 seconds), or using monolithic vector retrieval that sacrifices semantic precision for speed. To bridge this gap, we propose \textbf{GRAIL} (Granular Resonance-based Agent/AI Link), a novel framework achieving sub-400ms discovery latency without compromising accuracy. GRAIL introduces three key innovations: (1) \textbf{SLM-Enhanced Prediction}, replacing the generalized LLM parser with a specialized, fine-tuned Small Language Model (SLM) for millisecond-level capability tag prediction; (2) \textbf{Pseudo-Document Expansion}, augmenting agent descriptions with synthetic queries to enhance semantic density for robust dense retrieval; and (3) \textbf{MaxSim Resonance}, a fine-grained matching mechanism computing maximum similarity between user queries and discrete agent usage examples, effectively mitigating semantic dilution. Validated on \textbf{AgentTaxo-9K}, our new large-scale dataset of 9,240 agents, GRAIL reduces end-to-end discovery latency by over \textbf{79$\times$} compared to LLM-parsing baselines, while significantly outperforming traditional vector search in Recall@10. This framework offers a scalable, industrial-grade solution for the real-time ``Internet of Agents."

</details>


### [218] [DataClaw: A Process-Oriented Agent Benchmark for Exploratory Real-World Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02503)
*Qiaohong Zhang,Weihao Ye,Jialong Chen,Yi Luo,BoYuan Li,Bowen Deng,Zibin Zheng,Jianhao Lin,Wei-Shi Zheng,Chuan Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DataClaw是一个面向探索性真实世界数据分析的流程导向基准，包含约206万条跨领域真实记录和492个任务，用于评估AI智能体的渐进式推理能力。实验表明，当前最先进模型在此基准上表现不佳，7个模型准确率低于50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准过分强调最终答案准确性，依赖先验引导数据环境，对推理过程的评估支持有限，无法有效衡量自主数据分析智能体在未知数据环境中的探索性分析能力。

Method: 构建包含企业、产业和政策三大领域共206万条真实记录的数据库，保留原生数据噪声；设计492个源自智库咨询场景的跨领域任务，为每个任务标注中间里程碑以实现过程级评估。

Result: 8个先进LLM的实验中，7个模型总体准确率低于50%；流程分析发现错误答案背后隐藏着部分进展，且不同模型展现出差异化的探索策略。

Conclusion: DataClaw提供了一个低数据约束的诊断性测试平台，用于探测自主数据分析智能体的能力边界，并揭示了当前AI智能体在复杂真实数据分析任务中尚远未达到可靠水平。

Abstract: Evaluating autonomous data analysis agents requires testing their ability to perform exploratory analysis in underexplored data environments. However, many existing benchmarks emphasize final answer accuracy in prior-guided data settings and provide limited support for reasoning process evaluation. We introduce DataClaw, a process-oriented benchmark for exploratory real-world data analysis. DataClaw contains approximately 2.06 million real-world records across enterprise, industry and policy domains, with native data noise preserved. It further includes 492 cross-domain tasks derived from think-tank consulting scenarios, each annotated with intermediate milestones for process-level evaluation. These annotations allow DataClaw to measure how far an agent progresses and where its reasoning breaks down. Experiments with eight advanced LLMs show that current agents remain far from reliable in this setting, with seven models achieving below 50% overall accuracy. Process analysis further reveals partial progress hidden behind wrong answers and distinct exploration strategies across models. Overall, DataClaw provides a less data constrained diagnostic testbed for probing the capability boundaries of autonomous data-analysis agents.

</details>


### [219] [Improving Model Safety by Targeted Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02544)
*Abolfazl Mohammadi-Seif,Ricardo Baeza-Yates*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于双分类器GBDT流水线的后验错误修正方法，用于区分常规类人错误与高风险非人为错误。在动物品种分类、皮肤病变诊断（ISIC 2018）和前列腺组织病理学（SICAPv2）三个领域的评估显示，该方法在引入极低推理延迟（1.60%-1.84%）的同时，显著减少了危险的非人为错误（ISIC降低34.1%，SICAPv2降低12.57%），并在无需昂贵模型重训练的情况下提升了安全关键应用的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习在关键应用中的广泛采用亟需减少高后果错误的技术，尤其是区分常规类人错误与高风险非人为错误，以提升系统的安全可靠性。

Method: 采用双分类器GBDT流水线，通过后验方式区分类人错误与非人为错误，并采用保守修正策略对高风险错误进行纠正。

Result: 在三个领域（动物分类、ISIC 2018、SICAPv2）中，推理延迟仅增加1.60%-1.84%；非人为错误分别降低34.1%（ISIC）和12.57%（SICAPv2）；超类诊断安全性提升至90.41%和92.13%；修正精度优于传统最大类别概率（MCP）基线方法。

Conclusion: 安全关键系统的可靠性可以通过后验方式显著提升，而无需进行昂贵的模型重训练，证明了该方法在实际部署中的实用价值。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of machine learning in critical applications demands techniques to mitigate high-consequence errors. Our method utilizes a dual-classifier GBDT pipeline to distinguish routine human-like errors from high-risk non-human misclassifications. Evaluated across three domains, animal breed classification, skin lesion diagnosis (ISIC 2018), and prostate histopathology (SICAPv2), our framework demonstrates robust safety improvements. To address real-world deployment concerns, our results confirm the pipeline introduces negligible inference latency (1.60% overhead for the animal dataset, 1.84% for ISIC, and 1.70% for SICAPv2) while outperforming traditional Maximum Class Probability (MCP) baselines in correction precision. Our conservative correction strategy successfully reduced dangerous non-human errors by 34.1% in ISIC and 12.57% in SICAPv2, improving super-class diagnostic safety to 90.41% and 92.13% respectively. This proves that safety-critical reliability can be substantially enhanced post-hoc without expensive model retraining.
  keywords: Error Analysis, Post-hoc Correction, Trustworthy AI.

</details>


### [220] [Strategy-Aware Optimization Modeling with Reasoning LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02545)
*Ruiqing Zhao,Fengzhi Li,Yuan Zuo,Rui Liu,Yansong Liu,Yunfei Ma,Fanyu Meng,Junlan Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SAGE是一个策略感知框架，通过在数据构建和后训练中显式建模策略，提升大语言模型生成优化程序的正确性和求解器效率，在八个基准上pass@1从72.7提升至80.3，并生成更紧凑的约束系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能生成语法正确的优化程序，但难以可靠选择有效的建模策略，导致公式化错误和求解器效率低下。

Method: 1) 构建求解器验证的多策略数据集；2) 监督微调；3) 分段加权GRPO（Segment-Weighted GRPO），复合奖励涵盖格式合规性、正确性和求解器效率。

Result: pass@1从72.7提升至80.3；pass@16组件级多样性提升19-29%；约束数量减少14.2%。

Conclusion: 显式建模策略能够提升自动化优化建模的性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate syntactically valid optimization programs, yet often struggle to reliably choose an effective modeling strategy, leading to incorrect formulations and inefficient solver behavior. We propose SAGE, a strategy-aware framework that makes Modeling Strategy explicit in both data construction and post-training. SAGE builds a solver-verified multi-strategy dataset and trains a student model with supervised fine-tuning followed by Segment-Weighted GRPO using a composite reward over format compliance, correctness, and solver efficiency. Across eight benchmarks spanning synthetic and real-world settings, SAGE improves average pass@1 from 72.7 to 80.3 over the strongest open-source baseline. With multiple generations, SAGE discovers more distinct correct formulations and improves component-level diversity at pass@16 by 19-29%. At the largest scale, SAGE produces more compact constraint systems with 14.2% fewer constraints than the baseline, consistent with solver-efficient modeling. Overall, these results show that making Modeling Strategy explicit improves automated optimization modeling. Code is available at https://github.com/rachhhhing/SAGE.

</details>


### [221] [Double Rectified Linear Unit-based Modular Semantics for Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02551)
*Gianvincenzo Alfano,Sergio Greco,Lucio La Cava,Francesco Parisi,Irina Trubitsyna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的QBAF渐进语义，解决了现有语义在简单无环案例中产生分歧或反直觉结果的问题，满足理性公设，并证明了其在无环及更广泛有环框架下的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QBAF语义在处理简单无环案例时常常得出分歧或反直觉的结果，缺乏一种既符合直觉又满足理性公设的统一语义。

Method: 提出新的渐进语义，通过改进攻击者和支持者对论点最终强度的影响计算方式，同时分析其收敛性质。

Result: 新语义在无环和有环QBAF上均能收敛，结果更符合直觉期望，且满足已有的理性公设。

Conclusion: 提出的渐进语义弥补了现有QBAF方法的不足，在理论和收敛性上均具有良好的性质，适用于更广泛的论证框架。

Abstract: Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Frameworks (QBAFs) provide an alternative approach to computing argument acceptability in Bipolar Argumentation Frameworks (BAFs). Each argument is assigned an initial strength, which is then updated to a final strength by considering the influence of both its attackers and supporters. Over the years, several semantics have been proposed to compute argument acceptability in QBAFs, yet they often yield divergent or counterintuitive results, even for simple acyclic cases. We introduce novel gradual semantics for QBAFs that address these limitations, producing results that align more closely with intuitive expectations, while satisfying established rationality postulates from the literature. Furthermore, we study its convergence behavior, proving that it converges not only for acyclic QBAFs but also for broader classes of cyclic frameworks.

</details>


### [222] [On Training Large Language Models for Long-Horizon Tasks: An Empirical Study of Horizon Length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02572)
*Sunghwan Kim,Junhee Cho,Beong-woo Kwak,Taeyoon Kwon,Liang Wang,Nan Yang,Xingxing Zhang,Furu Wei,Jinyoung Yeo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过控制任务构建系统研究了任务 horizon（时间跨度/行动序列长度）对 LLM 作为交互式智能体训练动态的影响，发现增加 horizon 长度会导致训练不稳定、探索困难和信用分配问题，而 horizon 缩减则是稳定训练、提升性能的关键原则，并且能带来更好的跨 horizon 泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 LLM 作为交互式智能体已展现出潜力，但任务 horizon 长度如何影响训练动态这一基础问题尚未被充分理解。现有工作主要关注系统级优化或算法改进，忽略了任务结构本身（即行动序列长度）对训练过程的影响。

Method: 采用系统性的实证研究方法，通过构建受控任务来隔离 horizon 长度的影响。这些任务中智能体面临相同的决策规则和推理结构，仅在完成任务所需的行动序列长度上有所差异，从而能够直接观察 horizon 长度对训练动态的因果效应。

Result: (1) 增加 horizon 长度本身就会构成训练瓶颈，引发严重的训练不稳定性，其根源在于探索困难和信用分配挑战；(2) horizon 缩减能够稳定训练并在长 horizon 任务上取得更优性能；(3) 缩减 horizon 训练还能带来跨 horizon 的泛化能力提升，即模型在缩减 horizon 下训练后，在推理时能有效泛化到更长的 horizon 变体上（称为 horizon generalization）。

Conclusion: Horizon 长度是影响 LLM 交互式智能体训练的关键因素，horizon 缩减是一种有效的训练原则，不仅能提升长任务性能，还能实现跨 horizon 长度的泛化，为设计更高效、更稳定的 LLM 智能体训练策略提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise as interactive agents that solve tasks through extended sequences of environment interactions. While prior work has primarily focused on system-level optimizations or algorithmic improvements, the role of task horizon length in shaping training dynamics remains poorly understood. In this work, we present a systematic empirical study that examines horizon length through controlled task constructions. Specifically, we construct controlled tasks in which agents face identical decision rules and reasoning structures, but differ only in the length of action sequences required for successful completion. Our results reveal that increasing horizon length alone constitutes a training bottleneck, inducing severe training instability driven by exploration difficulties and credit assignment challenges. We demonstrate that horizon reduction is a key principle to address this limitation, stabilizing training and achieving better performance in long-horizon tasks. Moreover, we find that horizon reduction is related to stronger generalization across horizon lengths: models trained under reduced horizons generalize more effectively to longer-horizon variants at inference time, a phenomenon we refer to as horizon generalization.

</details>


### [223] [Universal Smoothness via Bernstein Polynomials: A Constructive Approximation Approach for Activation Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02591)
*Wentao Zhang,Yutong Zhang,Yifan Zhu,Wentao Mo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于构造逼近理论的通用平滑框架，并设计了Bernstein线性单元(BerLU)激活函数。通过Bernstein多项式构造可微二次过渡区域，在消除奇点的同时保持分段线性结构，理论保证严格连续可微且Lipschitz常数为1，实验中在ViT和CNN架构上均超越现有基线，兼具计算与内存效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活函数难以平衡优化稳定性与计算效率：分段线性函数（如ReLU）在原点不可导导致优化不稳定，而平滑函数（如GELU）依赖超越运算带来显著计算开销。

Method: 提出基于构造逼近理论的通用平滑框架，利用Bernstein多项式在激活函数原点附近构造可微二次过渡区域，消除奇点同时保持分段线性结构。理论保证严格连续可微且Lipschitz常数为1，防止梯度爆炸。

Result: 在ViT和CNN代表性架构上的标准图像分类基准测试中，BerLU一致性地超越现有最优基线，同时具有更优的计算和内存效率。

Conclusion: BerLU通过理论驱动的设计同时实现了优化稳定性与计算效率，为深度学习激活函数设计提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: The efficacy of deep neural networks is heavily reliant on the design of non-linear activation functions, yet existing approaches often struggle to balance optimization stability with computational efficiency. While piecewise linear functions offer inference speed, they suffer from optimization instability due to non-differentiability at the origin, whereas smooth counterparts typically incur significant computational overhead through their reliance on transcendental operations. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a general smoothing framework based on constructive approximation theory and introduces the Bernstein Linear Unit (BerLU). This novel activation function utilizes Bernstein polynomials to construct a differentiable quadratic transition region that effectively eliminates singularities while maintaining a piecewise linear structure. Theoretical analysis demonstrates that the proposed method guarantees strictly continuous differentiability and a non-expansive Lipschitz constant of one, which ensures stable gradient propagation and prevents the gradient explosion problems common in deep architectures. Comprehensive empirical evaluations across representative Vision Transformer and Convolutional Neural Network architectures confirm that this approach consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on standard image classification benchmarks while delivering superior computational and memory efficiency.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [224] [The Partial Testimony of Logs: Evaluation of Language Model Generation under Confounded Model Choice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01311)
*Jikai Jin,Vasilis Syrgkanis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何消除离线评估语言模型时的选择偏差，提出一种结合大规模观测日志、小规模随机实验和离线模拟器的三源设计方法，通过识别定理证明随机实验与模拟器可恢复因果模型价值，观测日志仅用于降低估计误差。在六种估计器族上的验证表明，不同方法在不同数据条件下表现各异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线评估语言模型时，模型选择与用户判断之间存在混杂偏差（confounding bias），即决定使用哪个模型的因素同样会影响对模型输出的评价，导致原始分数对比存在自选择偏差（self-selection bias）。随机实验虽能消除偏差，但成本高昂且稀缺，因此需要设计一种能结合观测数据规模优势与随机实验无偏性的评估框架。

Method: 提出三源设计（three-source design）：(1) 大规模混杂观测日志（OBS）提供数据规模；(2) 小规模随机实验（EXP）提供无偏评分；(3) 离线模拟器（SIM）在缓存上下文中回放候选模型。核心贡献是识别定理（identification theorem），证明随机实验与模拟器结合足以恢复因果模型价值，观测日志仅用于降低估计方差。在此基础上构建六种估计器族进行验证。

Result: 在半合成控制和两个真实任务缓存基准（摘要生成与代码生成）上评估六种估计器族，结果显示没有一种估计器在所有场景下占优，相对性能取决于无偏EXP监督数据的量以及目标奖励与OBS衍生结构之间的对齐程度。

Conclusion: 三源设计为离线语言模型评估提供了一种可操作的因果纠偏框架，但实际应用中需根据可用无偏数据量和奖励结构选择合适的估计器，不存在普适最优方案。

Abstract: Offline evaluation of language models from usage logs is biased when model choice is confounded: the same user-side factors that influence which model is used can also influence how its output is judged, so raw comparisons of logged scores mix self-selected populations rather than estimating a common quantity of interest. A small randomized experiment can break this bias by overriding model choice, but in practice such experiments are scarce and costly. We study a three-source design that combines a large confounded observational log (OBS) for scale, a small randomized experiment (EXP) for unconfounded scoring, and an offline simulator (SIM) that replays candidate models on cached contexts. Our main result is an identification theorem showing that the randomized experiment and the simulator are together enough to recover causal model values; the observational log enters only afterward, to reduce estimation error rather than to make the causal comparison valid. Six estimator families are evaluated in a controlled semi-synthetic validation and in two real-task cached benchmarks for summarization and coding. No family dominates every regime; relative performance depends on the amount of unbiased EXP supervision and on how closely the target reward aligns with OBS-derived structure.

</details>


### [225] [Agentopic: A Generative AI Agent Workflow for Explainable Topic Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00833)
*Brice Valentin Kok-Shun,Johnny Chan,Gabrielle Peko,David Sundaram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Agentopic 是一种基于多智能体协作的、可解释的主题建模工作流，利用大语言模型的推理能力，在 BBC 数据集上达到 F1=0.95，并生成 2045 个跨 6 个层次的主题，显著提升了可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主题建模方法（如 LDA、BERTopic）在主题分配或分组上缺乏透明度，难以追溯推理过程，不利于金融、医疗等关键领域的应用。

Method: 采用多个智能体（Agent）协作完成主题识别、验证、层次化分组和自然语言解释，将可解释性嵌入整个工作流。

Result: 种子化设置下 F1=0.95，与 GPT-4.1 持平，优于 LDA（0.93），接近 BERTopic（0.98）；无种子设置下生成 2045 个语义连贯主题，跨 6 个层次，远超原始 5 类别结构。

Conclusion: Agentopic 在保持较高准确率的同时提供可追溯的推理过程，为需要透明度的关键领域提供了黑箱模型的可解释替代方案。

Abstract: Agentopic is a novel agent-based workflow for explainable topic modeling that leverages the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). Existing topic modeling approaches such as Latent Dirichlet Allocation (LDA) and BERTopic often lack transparency on how topics are assigned or grouped. Agentopic addresses this by using multiple agents that collaboratively perform topic identification, validation, hierarchical grouping, and natural language explanation. This design enables users to trace the reasoning behind topic assignments, enhancing interpretability without sacrificing accuracy. When seeded with topics from the British Broadcasting Corporation (BBC) dataset, Agentopic achieves an F1-score of 0.95, matching GPT-4.1, improving on LDA (0.93), and close to BERTopic (0.98). We used Agentopic to augment the BBC dataset with generated explanations to improve the dataset's richness and context. The unseeded Agentopic generated 2045 semantically coherent topics organized across six hierarchical levels, vastly enriching the original five-category structure. By embedding explainability throughout the workflow, Agentopic offers an interpretable alternative to black-box models, making it particularly valuable for crucial applications like finance and healthcare.

</details>


### [226] [Linking spatial biology and clinical histology via Haiku](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00925)
*Yan Cui,Jacob S. Leiby,Wenhui Lei,Dokyoon Kim,Yanxiang Deng,Aaron T. Mayer,Zhenqin Wu,Alexandro E. Trevino,Zhi Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Haiku，一种基于多重免疫荧光（mIF）的三模态对比学习模型，整合了空间蛋白质组学、H&E组织学与临床元数据。模型在来自1,606名患者、11种器官类型的3,218张组织切片上的2,670万个空间蛋白质组学patch上训练，实现三模态交叉检索、下游分类与临床预测性能提升，并支持零样本生物标志物推理。引入反事实预测框架，通过仅修改临床元数据而固定组织形态，揭示与癌症进展和生存相关的微环境特异性分子变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子、形态学和临床数据的整合对基础与转化生物医学研究至关重要，但缺乏系统性的多模态联合建模框架。

Method: 构建三模态对比学习模型Haiku，在共享嵌入空间中对齐mIF空间蛋白质组学patch、H&E组织学图像和临床元数据；引入反事实预测框架，固定组织形态而仅修改临床元数据来揭示微环境特异性分子变化。

Result: 三模态交叉检索Recall@50达0.611（基线接近零）；生存预测C-index 0.737（相对提升+7.91%）；零样本生物标志物推理平均Pearson相关系数0.718（52种生物标志物）；反事实分析在肺腺癌中复现了CD8+和颗粒酶B增加、PD-L1降低、Ki67降低等与良好预后一致的模式。

Conclusion: 三模态对齐通过Haiku实现了空间生物学的整合分析，弥合了分子测量与临床上下文之间的鸿沟，为生物探索提供了新工具。

Abstract: Integrating molecular, morphological, and clinical data is essential for basic and translational biomedical research, yet systematic frameworks for jointly modeling these modalities remain limited. Here we present Haiku, a tri-modal contrastive learning model trained on multiplexed immunofluorescence (mIF). It comprises 26.7 million spatial proteomics patches from 3,218 tissue sections across 1,606 patients spanning 11 organ types, with matched hematoxylin and eosin (H&E) histology and clinical metadata aligned in a shared embedding space. Haiku enables three-way cross-modal retrieval, improves downstream classification and clinical prediction tasks over unimodal baselines, and supports zero-shot biomarker inference through fusion retrieval conditioned on clinical metadata-only text descriptions. Across tasks, Haiku outperforms competing approaches, achieving cross-modal retrieval (Recall@50 up to 0.611 versus near-zero baseline), survival prediction (C-index 0.737, +7.91% relative improvement), and zero-shot biomarker inference (mean Pearson correlation 0.718 across 52 biomarkers). Furthermore, we introduce a counterfactual prediction framework in which modifying only clinical metadata while fixing tissue morphology surfaces niche-specific molecular shifts associated with breast cancer stage progression and lung cancer survival outcomes. In a lung adenocarcinoma case study, the counterfactual analysis recovers niche-specific shifts characterized by increased CD8 and granzyme B, reduced PD-L1, and decreased Ki67, broadly consistent with patterns reported for favorable outcomes. We present these counterfactual results as exploratory, hypothesis-generating signals rather than mechanistic claims. These capabilities demonstrate that tri-modal alignment via Haiku enables integrative analysis of spatial biology, bridging molecular measurements with clinical context for biological exploration.

</details>


### [227] [Polynomial-Time Optimal Group Selection via the Double-Commutator Eigenvalue Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00834)
*Mitchell A. Thornton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了代数多样性框架中的群选择问题可归约为广义特征值问题，通过双交换子矩阵的最小特征向量直接构造最优群生成器，实现了多项式时间、闭式且可认证的求解算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代数多样性框架中群选择问题需要从M维观测中寻找与协方差结构最佳匹配的有限群，而穷举对称群S_M的所有子群需要指数时间，亟需高效算法。

Method: 将组合群选择问题归约为协方差矩阵双交换子的广义特征值问题，通过双交换子矩阵的最小特征向量直接闭式构造最优群生成器，算法复杂度为O(d²M²+d³)。

Result: 双交换子矩阵的最小特征值为零当且仅当最优生成器位于基的张成空间中；特征值大小提供可认证的最优性差距。该方法同时满足多项式时间、闭式求解和可认证性。

Conclusion: 该问题不属于标准计算复杂性目录中的已知类别，是群论、矩阵分析和统计估计的新交叉领域，并与独立成分分析(JADE)、结构化矩阵逼近和同步矩阵对角化问题建立联系。

Abstract: The algebraic diversity framework replaces temporal averaging over multiple observations with algebraic group action on a single observation for second-order statistical estimation. The central open problem in this framework is $\textit{group selection}$: given an $M$-dimensional observation with unknown covariance structure, find the finite group whose spectral decomposition best matches the covariance. Naive enumeration of all subgroups of the symmetric group $S_M$ requires exponential time in $M$. We prove that this combinatorial problem reduces to a generalized eigenvalue problem derived from the double commutator of the covariance matrix, yielding a polynomial-time algorithm with complexity $O(d^2M^2 + d^3)$, where $d$ is the dimension of a generator basis. The minimum eigenvector of the double-commutator matrix directly constructs the optimal group generator in closed form, with no iterative optimization. The reduction is exact: the double-commutator minimum eigenvalue is zero if and only if the optimal generator lies in the span of the basis, and its magnitude provides a certifiable optimality gap when it does not. This problem does not appear in the standard catalogs of computational complexity (Garey and Johnson, 1979) and represents a new class linking group theory, matrix analysis, and statistical estimation. We establish connections to independent component analysis (JADE), structured matrix nearness problems, and simultaneous matrix diagonalization, and we show that the double-commutator formulation is the unique approach that is simultaneously polynomial-time, closed-form, and certifiable.

</details>


### [228] [Sparse Regression under Correlation and Weak Signals: A Reproducible Benchmark of Classical and Bayesian Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00835)
*Hao Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在模拟数据和真实数据上系统比较了六种稀疏回归方法（OLS、Ridge、Lasso、Elastic Net、Horseshoe、Spike-and-Slab），发现在预测误差上贝叶斯方法优于频率派方法，其中Horseshoe覆盖率达到近标称水平（94.8%），但在变量选择上Lasso与Spike-and-Slab表现相当（F1≈0.47），表明在不需要后验分布时Lasso是实用默认选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典稀疏回归方法（如Lasso）速度快但无法提供不确定度量，而贝叶斯方法（如Horseshoe）可给出完整后验但计算成本高，且很少有研究在真实困难条件（特征相关、弱信号、高维度）下对两类方法进行系统对比。

Method: 在合成数据上设置三种协方差结构（ρ高达0.9）、四个信噪比水平、三种维度（p=20,50,100）以及Diabetes真实数据集，共超过2600次实验，比较OLS、Ridge、Lasso、Elastic Net、Horseshoe和Spike-and-Slab六种方法。

Result: 贝叶斯方法在预测误差上优于频率派方法（MSE 72 vs 108-267）；Horseshoe覆盖率达94.8%（接近标称95%）；Spike-and-Slab区间更窄但覆盖率为91.9%（欠覆盖）；变量选择上Lasso与Spike-and-Slab持平（F1≈0.47）。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯方法在预测和不确定性量化上表现更优，但变量选择上Lasso足以胜任；实践中应根据是否需要后验分布来选择方法。

Abstract: Choosing between classical and Bayesian sparse regression methods involves a real trade-off: penalized estimators like Lasso run in milliseconds but give no uncertainty estimates,while Horseshoe and Spike-and-Slab priors produce full posteriors but need MCMC chains that take minutes per fit.Surprisingly few studies compare these two families head-to-head under the conditions that actually make sparse regression hard -- correlated features, weak signals, and growing dimensionality. We benchmark six methods (OLS, Ridge,Lasso, Elastic Net, Horseshoe, Spike-and-Slab) on synthetic data with three covariance structures (rho up to 0.9), four SNR levels, and p in {20, 50, 100}, plus the Diabetes dataset,totalling over 2,600 experiments. The results are clear on some points and nuanced on others. Bayesian methods win on prediction error (MSE 72 vs. 108-267), and the Horseshoe delivers near-nominal 95% coverage (94.8%). But Spike-and-Slab,despite narrower intervals, under-covers at 91.9% -- its continuous relaxation likely plays a role. For variable selection, Lasso and Spike-and-Slab tie at F1 ~ 0.47, making Lasso the practical default when posteriors are not needed. Code and data are available at https://github.com/xiao98/sparse-bayesian-regression-bench.

</details>


### [229] [Benchmarking Single-Pose Docking, Consensus Rescoring, and Supervised ML on the LIT-PCBA Library: A Critical Evaluation of DiffDock, AutoDock-GPU, GNINA, and DiffDock-NMDN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01681)
*Youssef Abo-Dahab,Xiaoiang Xiang,Xiaoiang Xiang,Xiaoiang Xiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在LIT-PCBA库（15个靶点，578,295个配体-靶点对）上系统评估了经典对接（AutoDock-GPU）与AI方法（DiffDock、NMDN）在虚拟筛选中的实际性能。AutoDock-GPU结合GNINA重打分（AutoDock-GNINA）是表现最好的单一方法（中位数EF1%=2.14），而DiffDock方法表现不佳。基于监督学习的重排序方法取得最大提升（中位数EF1%=4.49，提升110%），但即使最优的混合流程在实际基准测试中早期富集效果仍然有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI虚拟筛选工具（如DiffDock、NMDN）在基准测试中表现优异，但其在真实实验数据集上的实用性尚不明确。本研究旨在通过大规模、真实实验来源的LIT-PCBA库，系统评估经典对接方法与新兴AI方法在实际场景中的虚拟筛选性能。

Method: 使用LIT-PCBA库（15个靶点，578,295个配体-靶点对，含实验确认的活性和非活性化合物）。对比AutoDock-GPU和DiffDock进行构象生成，分别用GNINA和NMDN重打分。进一步评估基于排名的共识策略以及基于对接特征的监督机器学习模型进行重排序。

Result: AutoDock-GNINA为最强单一方法（中位数EF1%=2.14）。DiffDock方法表现较差，尤其在OPRK1等挑战性靶点上。共识排名提高了鲁棒性但未超越最优单一打分器。监督ML重排序取得最大提升（中位数EF1%=4.49，较AutoDock-GNINA提升110%）。但即使最佳混合方法在现实基准中早期富集效果仍较有限。

Conclusion: 没有单一的对接方法能在所有靶点上占据主导地位。经过严格验证的、成本效益高的经典方法结合监督重排序的组合策略，目前为虚拟筛选提供最实用的价值。即使最好的经典+ML混合工作流程在实际基准测试中只能提供适度的早期富集。

Abstract: Virtual screening performance depends heavily on the chosen docking and scoring methods. Recent AI-based tools such as DiffDock and NMDN have reported strong benchmark results, but their practical utility on realistic, experimentally-derived datasets remains unclear. Here we perform a large-scale evaluation on the LIT-PCBA library (15 targets, 578,295 ligand-target pairs with experimentally confirmed actives and inactives). We compare AutoDock-GPU and DiffDock for pose generation, followed by rescoring with GNINA and NMDN. We further evaluate rank-based consensus strategies and supervised machine learning models trained on docking features.
  GNINA rescoring of AutoDock-GPU poses (AutoDock-GNINA) emerged as the strongest single method with a median EF1% of 2.14. DiffDock-based approaches underperformed relative to AutoDock-GNINA, particularly on challenging targets such as OPRK1. Carefully designed consensus ranking improved robustness but did not surpass the best single scorer. Supervised ML re-ranking delivered the largest gains, achieving a median EF1% of 4.49 (+110% over AutoDock-GNINA).
  Our results highlight that even the best classical+ML hybrid workflows provide only modest early enrichment on realistic benchmarks. We conclude that no single docking method dominates across targets and that rigorously validated, cost-effective combinations with supervised re-ranking currently offer the most practical value for virtual screening.

</details>


### [230] [Bolek: A Multimodal Language Model for Molecular Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02745)
*Frederic Grabowski,Jacek Szczerbiński,Maciej Jaśkowski,Kalina Jasińska-Kobus,Paweł Dąbrowski-Tumański,Tomasz Jetka,Bartosz Topolski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Bolek是一种紧凑的多模态语言模型，通过注入Morgan指纹嵌入到指令微调文本解码器中，将自然语言推理与分子结构相结合。它在分子对齐任务和15个TDC二分类任务上微调，使用基于具体分子特征的合成思维链。Bolek在13/15个二分类任务上超越Qwen3-4B-Instruct和TxGemma-9B-Chat（尽管体积小一半），将平均ROC/PR AUC从0.55提升至0.76，其解释更接地气（引用数值描述符频率高10-100倍，与RDKit高度一致），并能泛化到未见任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子性质模型在高风险药物发现决策中日益重要，但经典预测器只返回分数缺乏解释，而语言模型会生成与输入分子关联薄弱的流畅解释。因此需要一种既能准确预测又能给出可审计、可验证推理过程的分子推理模型。

Method: 提出Bolek，将Morgan指纹嵌入注入到指令微调文本解码器（Qwen3-4B-Instruct）中，在分子描述、RDKit描述符预测、子结构检测等对齐任务以及15个TDC二分类任务上微调，使用锚定在具体分子特征上的合成思维链进行推理监督。

Result: Bolek在yes/no模式下全面超越基座模型，在CoT模式下13/15任务胜出，平均ROC/PR AUC从0.55升至0.76；超越TxGemma-9B-Chat在13/15二分类任务上；解释中引用数值描述符频率高10-100倍，与RDKit在TPSA、MolLogP、MolWt上Spearman ρ=0.87-0.91；在15个未见TDC分类任务上与TxGemma持平5个，在3个未见回归任务上产生非平凡秩相关。

Conclusion: 针对性的模态注入和基于可验证分子特征的推理监督可以产生紧凑、可审计的分子推理模型。

Abstract: Molecular property models increasingly support high-stakes drug-discovery decisions, but their outputs are often difficult to audit: classical predictors return scores without rationale, while language models can produce fluent explanations weakly grounded in the input molecule.
  We introduce Bolek, a compact multimodal language model that grounds natural-language reasoning in molecular structure by injecting a Morgan fingerprint embedding into an instruction-tuned text decoder. Bolek is fine-tuned on molecular alignment tasks, including molecule description, RDKit descriptor prediction, and substructure detection, and on downstream reasoning over 15 TDC binary classification tasks using synthetic chains-of-thought anchored in concrete molecular features.
  Across these tasks, Bolek outperforms its Qwen3-4B-Instruct base on all endpoints in yes/no mode and on 13 of 15 in chain-of-thought mode, raising mean ROC/PR AUC from 0.55 to 0.76. It also outperforms TxGemma-9B-Chat on 13 of 15 binary classification tasks despite being less than half its size. Bolek's explanations are more grounded than those of the baseline LLMs: it cites numerical descriptors 10-100x more often per chain-of-thought, and the cited values agree strongly with RDKit for key descriptors such as TPSA, MolLogP, and MolWt (Spearman rho = 0.87-0.91). Generalisation extends beyond the training panel: on 15 unseen TDC classification endpoints, Bolek matches TxGemma on five, and it produces non-trivial rank correlations on three held-out regression endpoints despite never seeing downstream regression during training.
  These results suggest that targeted modality injection and reasoning supervision tied to verifiable molecular features can yield compact, auditable molecular reasoning models.

</details>


### [231] [Fast Log-Domain Sinkhorn Optimal Transport with Warp-Level GPU Reductions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00837)
*Hao Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FastSinkhorn，一种轻量级的原生CUDA对数域Sinkhorn算法实现，通过warp-level shuffle归约和共享内存分块实现高GPU利用率，在正则化参数小至10^{-4}时仍保持数值稳定，在8192×8192密集OT问题上比POT库快12倍、比PyTorch基线快5.9倍，且仅占用256 MB显存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最优传输的Sinkhorn算法实现存在两难：小正则化参数时数值不稳定，或依赖深度学习框架带来显著开销。亟需一种既数值稳定又轻量高效的原生GPU实现。

Method: 采用纯对数域Sinkhorn算法，结合warp-level shuffle归约（减少显存带宽压力）和共享内存分块（提高数据局部性），以原生CUDA内核实现，避免深度学习框架额外开销。

Result: 在n=m=8192的密集OT问题中，相比POT库实现12倍加速，相比PyTorch GPU基线实现5.9倍加速，显存仅256 MB；正则化参数可低至10^{-4}仍保持稳定。

Conclusion: 原生CUDA内核配合精细的数值处理，为大规模最优传输计算提供了实用高效的解决方案，在图像颜色迁移、3D点云匹配等任务中验证了有效性。

Abstract: Entropic regularized optimal transport (OT) via the Sinkhorn algorithm has become a fundamental tool in machine learning, yet existing implementations either suffer from numerical instability for small regularization parameters or incur significant overhead from deep learning frameworks. We present FastSinkhorn, a lightweight, native CUDA implementation of the log-domain Sinkhorn algorithm that combines warp-level shuffle reductions with shared-memory tiling to achieve high GPU utilization without sacrificing numerical stability. Our solver operates entirely in the log-domain, enabling robust computation for regularization parameters as small as epsilon = 10^{-4} where standard-domain methods fail. On dense OT problems with n = m = 8192, our implementation achieves 12x speedup over the widely-used POT library and 5.9x speedup over GPU-accelerated PyTorch baselines, while consuming only 256 MB of GPU memory. We validate our solver on image color transfer, 3D point cloud matching, and convergence analysis, demonstrating that native CUDA kernels with careful numerical treatment provide a practical and efficient foundation for large-scale optimal transport computation.

</details>


### [232] [GAZE: Grounded Agentic Zero-shot Evaluation with Viewer-Level Tools and Literature Retrieval on Rare Brain MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00876)
*Duaa Alim,Mogtaba Alim,Liam Chalcroft*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GAZE 是一个让医学视觉语言模型通过迭代调用工具（缩放、窗宽窗位、对比度、边缘检测、PubMed 检索、Open-i 检索）来模拟放射科医生工作流程的框架。在 NOVA 基准（906 例脑 MRI，281 种罕见病）上，无需任务特定微调即可达到 58.2 mAP@0.3 病灶定位和 34.9% Top-1 诊断准确率。工具使用对罕见病帮助更大（IoU>0.3 从 17% 提升至 58% vs 常见病 25% 到 68%），且不同模型对工具的使用效果存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 以单次前馈方式处理图像，而放射科医生会多次查看图像并查阅文献后才撰写报告。这种一次性处理范式与临床实际工作流程存在巨大差距，限制了 VLM 在医学影像分析中的表现，尤其是对罕见病的诊断能力。

Method: 提出 GAZE 框架，允许医学 VLM 通过调用五种 viewer-level 工具（缩放、窗宽窗位、对比度、边缘检测）和两种检索工具（PubMed 文献检索、Open-i 影像检索）进行迭代推理。输出经过结构化的 schema 验证，并记录完整工具调用轨迹以便审计。在 NOVA 基准上评估，包含 906 例脑 MRI，覆盖 281 种罕见神经系统疾病。

Result: GAZE 达到 58.2 mAP@0.3 病灶定位和 34.9% Top-1 诊断准确率。结构化提示和 schema 验证本身就将基线从 20.2 提升至 29.4 mAP@0.3。工具使用对罕见病帮助更大：罕见病 IoU>0.3 从 17% 升至 58%，常见病从 25% 升至 68%。不同模型效果差异显著（Gemini 3 Flash: Cohen's d=0.79, 11.8 次调用/例；Gemini 2.0 Flash: 仅 8.2% 用例使用工具，无显著收益）。检索消融实验显示诊断提升可能伴随定位下降的模型依赖权衡。

Conclusion: GAZE 框架证明了工具增强的迭代推理可有效提升医学 VLM 在罕见病诊断和病灶定位方面的表现，且框架设计本身即是重要的实验变量。研究强调了在医学 VLM 评估中应联合评估诊断、定位和描述三项指标，而非单独考察某一项。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) read an image and produce text in a single forward pass, whereas radiologists typically inspect an image several times and consult the literature before writing a report. We introduce GAZE (Grounded Agentic Zero-shot Evaluation), a framework that lets a medical VLM work in this iterative way by calling viewer-level tools (zoom, windowing, contrast, edge detection) and two retrieval tools backed by the U.S. National Library of Medicine (PubMed for medical literature, Open-i for radiological images), with structured outputs validated against a schema and full tool-call traces recorded for auditability. On NOVA, a benchmark of 906 brain MRI cases covering 281 rare neurological conditions, GAZE reaches 58.2 mean average precision (mAP) at intersection-over-union (IoU) 0.3 for lesion localisation and 34.9% Top-1 diagnostic accuracy under a joint protocol that scores captioning, diagnosis, and localisation from the image alone, without task-specific fine-tuning. Before any tool is used, structured prompting and schema-validated outputs already improve over the published Gemini 2.0 Flash baseline (20.2 to 29.4 mAP@0.3), so framework design is itself an experimental variable. Tool use helps rare pathologies disproportionately: the fraction of cases with IoU > 0.3 rises from 17% to 58% for diagnoses with three or fewer examples versus 25% to 68% for common conditions ($\geq$10 cases), with gains tracking engagement (Gemini 3 Flash: Cohen's d = 0.79, 11.8 tool calls per case; Gemini 2.0 Flash: tools used in 8.2% of cases, no significant benefit). Retrieval ablations additionally reveal a model-dependent trade-off in which gains in diagnosis can coincide with losses in localisation, reinforcing the case for joint evaluation of diagnosis, localisation, and captioning in medical VLMs.

</details>


### [233] [A Review of the Receiver Operating Characteristic Curve and a Proof About the Area Beneath It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00926)
*Steven Redolfi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化了ROC曲线下面积（AUC）的概率解释——即随机选取的正样本得分高于随机选取的负样本的概率——并在假设不满足时给出了该解释与真实值之间的误差界，同时提供了ROC曲线的小型文献综述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ROC曲线及其AUC被广泛用于衡量二分类器性能，其概率解释常被引用但缺乏严格的数学形式化证明。本文旨在严格证明该解释的正确性，并量化当某些假设不满足时该解释的偏差程度。

Method: 通过形式化数学推导证明AUC等于P(正样本得分 > 负样本得分)的概率解释，并推导出当假设不满足时该解释的误差上界；同时进行文献综述。

Result: 严格形式化了AUC的概率解释，给出了假设不满足时的误差界，并完成了ROC曲线相关文献的综述。

Conclusion: AUC的概率解释在严格条件下成立，当条件偏离时存在可量化的误差界，为ROC曲线的理论理解和实际应用提供了更坚实的基础。

Abstract: The Receiver Operating Characteristic (ROC) curve of a binary classifier has often been utilized to measure the performance of the classifier. The area beneath this curve is used in particular because of its quoted probabilistic interpretation as being equal to the probability that the classifier will rank a random positive observation above a random negative observation. This paper formalizes this claim, produces a bound on how far away from the truth it is if a hypothesis is not met, and gives a small literature review of the ROC curve.

</details>


### [234] [CGM-JEPA: Learning Consistent Continuous Glucose Monitor Representations via Predictive Self-Supervised Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00933)
*Hada Melino Muhammad,Zechen Li,Flora Salim,Ahmed A. Metwally*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CGM-JEPA和X-CGM-JEPA自监督预训练框架，通过跨视图抽象表征解决连续血糖监测中的模态迁移和表征不一致问题，在多个临床场景下取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CGM数据存在多视图（CGM时间序列、静脉OGTT、Glucodensity摘要）且单视图表征无法跨模态迁移，现有基线在不同场景下表现不稳定，需要一种能捕捉更高层时空与分布结构、跨视图泛化的表征学习方法。

Method: 提出CGM-JEPA，采用自监督预训练框架，预测掩码隐层表征而非原始值，实现跨模态抽象；X-CGM-JEPA额外引入掩码Glucodensity跨视图目标，补充分布信息。预训练数据为228名受试者的约38.9万条无标签CGM读数。

Result: 在三个场景（队列泛化、静脉到CGM迁移、家庭CGM）下，X-CGM-JEPA在AUROC上均排名第一或第二，最强基线无法做到；相比最强基线，队列泛化提升最高+6.5pp，静脉到CGM迁移提升+3.6pp（p<0.001）；模态迁移下缩小种族AUROC差距25-54%；分布视图在稀疏静脉数据上提升标签感知聚类（ARI+39%, NMI+40%）。

Conclusion: X-CGM-JEPA通过跨视图抽象表征有效解决了CGM多视图不一致和模态迁移问题，在多个临床场景下表现稳定，并有助于缩小子组公平性差距。

Abstract: Continuous Glucose Monitoring (CGM) can detect early metabolic subphenotypes (insulin resistance, IR; $β$-cell dysfunction), but population-scale deployment faces two coupled problems. First, the same physiological state appears through multiple views (CGM time series, venous OGTT, Glucodensity summaries), so single-view representations fail to transfer when deployment shifts the modality or setting. Second, baselines perform inconsistently across these shifts. Both problems point to one remedy: representations that abstract away from any single view to capture higher-level temporal and distributional structure. We propose CGM-JEPA, a self-supervised pretraining framework which predicts masked latent representations rather than raw values, yielding abstraction that transfers across modalities. X-CGM-JEPA adds a masked Glucodensity cross-view objective for complementary distributional information. We pretrain on $\sim$389k unlabeled CGM readings from 228 subjects and evaluate on two clinical cohorts ($N=27$ and $N=17$ public-release subsets) across three regimes (cohort generalization, venous-to-CGM transfer, home CGM) under 20-iteration $\times$ 2-fold cross-validation. X-CGM-JEPA ranks first or second on AUROC for both endpoints across all three regimes while no baseline does, exceeding the strongest baseline by up to $+6.5$ pp in cohort generalization and $+3.6$ pp in venous-to-CGM transfer (paired Wilcoxon, $p<0.001$). Under modality shift, it matches mean AUROC while redistributing toward weaker subgroups (ethnicity AUROC gap shrinks 25-54%); on sparse in-domain venous data, the distributional view lifts label-aware clustering (ARI $+39\%$, NMI $+40\%$). Code and weights: https://github.com/cruiseresearchgroup/CGM-JEPA

</details>


### [235] [Structured Analytic Coherent Point Drift for Non-Rigid Point Set Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00934)
*Wei Feng,Haiyong Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Analytic-CPD，一种将CPD的软对应框架与有限维解析映射估计器相结合的非刚性点集配准方法。通过重心理恒等式将后验概率凝聚为加权软目标点，并用截断多变量泰勒映射表示变形场，利用度延续策略稳定大变形配准。实验表明在二维和三维变形场景中，该方法在精度和收敛速度上均优于标准CPD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准CPD使用点索引的高斯核位移场M-step，变形参数数量受M×M核系统控制，在大变形场景下计算效率低且可解释性差。作者希望用结构化解析映射替代高斯核表示，实现更紧凑、高效且可解释的非刚性配准。

Method: 1) 保留CPD的高斯混合模型后验对应层；2) 通过重心理恒等式将后验概率凝聚为加权软目标点；3) 用截断多变量泰勒映射（有限维解析映射）替代高斯核位移场；4) 变形参数由环境维度和解析阶数控制，而非M×M核矩阵；5) 提出度延续策略，逐步激活高阶解析模态以稳定大变形配准。

Result: 在二维解析变形和三维平滑非解析变形的实验中，Analytic-CPD在代表性的形变场景下比标准CPD实现了更低的最终误差和更快的收敛速度。

Conclusion: CPD风格的软对应与结构化解析映射的组合，为基于核的非刚性配准提供了一种紧凑且可解释的替代方案。

Abstract: We introduce Analytic-CPD, a structured analytic variant of coherent point drift for non-rigid point set registration. The method retains the CPD posterior correspondence layer, but replaces the point-indexed Gaussian-kernel displacement-field M-step with a finite-dimensional structured analytic mapping estimator. Posterior probabilities from the Gaussian mixture model are condensed through a barycentric identity into weighted soft target points, converting the CPD pairwise soft-correspondence objective into a weighted analytic fitting problem. The deformation is represented by a truncated multivariate Taylor mapping of a vector-valued function, so the number of deformation parameters is controlled by the ambient dimension and the analytic order rather than by an M-by-M kernel system over the moving points. A degree-continuation strategy is further introduced to stabilize large-deformation registration by progressively activating higher-order analytic modes. Experiments on two-dimensional analytic deformations and three-dimensional smooth non-analytic deformations show that Analytic-CPD achieves lower final errors and faster convergence than standard CPD in representative large-deformation settings. The results suggest that CPD-style probabilistic correspondences and structured analytic mappings provide a compact and interpretable alternative to kernel-based non-rigid registration. Code is available at https://github.com/monge-ampere/Analytic-CPD.

</details>


### [236] [Watch Your Step: Information Injection in Diffusion Models via Shadow Timestep Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00935)
*An Huang,Junggab Son,Zuobin Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Shadow Timestep Embedding (STE)机制，揭示了扩散模型中时间步嵌入空间可作为侧信道进行恶意信息注入，并通过调度器接口实现攻击与防御，为对抗性生成建模提供了新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前扩散模型研究主要集中在提升生成效率和质量，而时间步嵌入组件虽蕴含丰富信息却未被充分探索，尤其是在安全风险和可靠溯源方面存在研究空白。

Method: 通过理论分析将时间步嵌入建模为位置编码映射，推导互相干性评估来解释不同时间步区间的可分离性；利用调度器接口在时间步嵌入空间中编码侧信道信息，实现恶意信息的注入与提取。

Result: 发现不同时间步具有不同的表示能力，可以编码侧信道信息；时间步嵌入空间中的信息可通过调度器接口用于攻击和防御目的。

Conclusion: 扩散模型的时间步是一个强大的侧信道，可用于携带专用信息，这启发了通过理解时间维度来构建对抗性生成建模的新研究方向。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become the foundation of modern generative systems, with most research focusing primarily on improving generation efficiency and output quality. The timestep embedding component is a crucial part of the diffusion pipeline, which provides a temporal conditioning signal to the denoising network, enabling it to adapt its predictions across different noise levels throughout the process. Despite their potential to contain substantial information, timestep embeddings remain underexplored in current research, especially for security risks and reliable provenance. To fill this gap, we introduce Shadow Timestep Embedding (STE), a novel mechanism that investigates the underutilized temporal space for malicious information injection into diffusion models. In particular, when zooming in on the timestep embedding space, we find that different timesteps exhibit distinct representational capabilities that can encode side-channel information. Moreover, such encoded information can be utilized for attack and defense purposes through the scheduler interface. We present a theoretical analysis of timestep embeddings as position-encoding mappings and derive a mutual coherence evaluation that explains the separability of disjoint timestep intervals. Our findings reveal the diffusion model's timestep as a powerful side channel for carrying dedicated information, motivating new directions for adversarial generative modeling by understanding the temporal dimension.

</details>


### [237] [EventADL: Open-Box Anomaly Detection and Localization Framework for Events in Cloud-Based Service Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00936)
*Luan Pham,Victor Nicolet,Joey Dodds,Hui Guan,Daniel Kroening*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EventADL是首个面向云服务系统的基于事件数据的开箱式异常检测与定位框架，通过学习事件语义模式（ESP）和事件频率模式（EFP）检测异常，并构建干预图进行根因定位，在真实系统中检测F1≥90%、根因定位Top-3准确率100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常检测与定位（ADL）方法主要针对指标和日志数据，忽略了事件数据这一重要来源。作者通过对520个真实 incidents 的系统分析，发现异常及其根因在事件数据中有着独特的表现，因此需要专门针对事件数据的ADL框架。

Method: EventADL包含三个阶段：离线训练阶段学习事件语义模式（ESP）捕捉系统实体间的正常交互，以及事件频率模式（EFP）捕捉已知ESP的正常频率；在线检测阶段识别偏离任一模式的事件流为异常；根因定位阶段构建干预图（Intervention Graph）建模系统交互与检测到的异常之间的关系，实现自动根因定位。

Result: 在三个真实云服务系统和两个真实 incidents 上的评估表明，EventADL异常检测F1分数≥90%，根因定位Top-3准确率达到100%，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: EventADL填补了基于事件数据的异常检测与定位的空白，能够在无标签数据上高效运行，生成可解释的异常及其对应根因，有效提升云服务系统的可靠性和可用性。

Abstract: Anomaly detection and localization (ADL) is critical for maintaining reliability and availability in cloud systems. Recent ADL developments focus on metric and log data, leaving event data unexplored. To address this gap, we propose EventADL, the first open-box event-based ADL framework for cloud-based service systems. To motivate the design of our framework, we conduct a systematic analysis on 520 real-world incidents, and provide insights into how anomalies and their root causes manifest through event data. EventADL has three phases: offline training, online anomaly detection, and root cause localization. During the training phase, EventADL first learns Event Semantic Patterns (ESPs), which capture normal interactions between system entities using historical event data, and then learns Event Frequency Patterns (EFPs), which capture the normal frequency of known ESPs. In the online anomaly detection phase, any data in the event stream that deviates significantly from either pattern is identified as anomalous. For localization, EventADL constructs an Intervention Graph that models the relationships between recent system interactions and the detected anomalies for automatic root cause localization. The framework is designed to operate efficiently with unlabeled data and to produce interpretable anomalies with their corresponding root causes. Our evaluation on three real cloud service systems and two real-world incidents demonstrates that EventADL outperforms existing methods, achieving F1-scores of at least 90% for anomaly detection and 100% top-3 accuracy in root cause localization.

</details>


### [238] [Fusing Urban Structure and Semantics: A Conditional Diffusion Model for Cross-City OD Matrix Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00938)
*Bin Chen,Zhuoya Meng,Fang Yang,Runkang Guo,Jingtao Ding,Yin Zhang,Chuan Ai,Zhengqiu Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SEDAN模型，通过结构增强的扩散模型融合语义信息与空间约束，实现跨城市泛化的通勤OD矩阵生成，在真实数据集上RMSE比最优基线提升7.38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通勤流建模对城市治理、交通规划和资源分配至关重要，但个体意图、地理约束和社会动态的联合影响导致通勤模式存在显著异质性，现有生成模型难以跨城市泛化。

Method: 将城市建模为属性图（区域为节点，通勤流为加权边），通过图-Transformer交互建模区域语义特征以捕获潜在出行需求；同时将邻接矩阵（引导相邻区域注意力权重）和距离矩阵（作为扩散条件）作为显式空间约束注入生成过程，实现语义与空间信息的联合融合。

Result: 在美国多个城市真实OD数据集上，SEDAN的RMSE比最优基线WEDAN提升7.38%，并在异构城市场景和不同结构模式下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 融合城市语义与空间约束的扩散模型能够生成行为合理且地理一致的OD矩阵，为通勤OD矩阵生成提供了一种有效且可泛化的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate modeling of commuting flows is important for urban governance, traffic planning, and resource allocation. However, the combined influence of individual intentions, geographic constraints, and social dynamics leads to considerable heterogeneity in commuting patterns, making it difficult to develop generation models that generalize across cities. To address this issue, we propose SEDAN, a Structure-Enhanced Diffusion model conditioned on Attributed Nodes for generalizable OD matrix generation. SEDAN models a city as an attributed graph. Each region is treated as a node with demographic and point-of-interest features, and commuting flows are modeled as weighted edges. Adjacency and distance matrices are incorporated to characterize spatial structure. Based on this representation, we design a fusion mechanism within SEDAN to jointly model semantic information and spatial information. Regional semantic attributes are used to model latent travel demand through graph-transformer-based node interactions, while spatial structure is injected into the generation process as explicit constraints. The adjacency matrix guides attention weights to strengthen interactions between neighboring regions. Meanwhile, the distance matrix serves as a diffusion condition to capture spatial proximity and travel impedance. The fusion of urban semantics and spatial constraints enables SEDAN to generate OD matrices that are both behaviorally plausible and geographically coherent. Experiments on real-world OD datasets from U.S. cities show that SEDAN achieves a 7.38\% improvement in RMSE over the state-of-the-art baseline, WEDAN. It also remains robust across heterogeneous urban scenarios and varying structural patterns. Our work provides an effective and generalizable solution for commuting OD matrix generation. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SEDAN.

</details>


### [239] [Divergence is Uncertainty: A Closed-Form Posterior Covariance for Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00941)
*Jiarui Xing,Song Wang,Jian Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出散度-不确定性恒等式，证明任意预训练流匹配模型的协方差迹等于速度场散度（乘以已知时间因子加常数），无需重新训练或集成即可精确计算后验不确定性，在MNIST上以约万分之一的计算量得到语义有意义的不确定性图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配生成模型的样本不确定性量化仍是一个未解决问题，现有方法需重新训练辅助头、维护昂贵集成或通过多步传播近似协方差，在训练成本、推理成本或精度间存在权衡。

Method: 证明任意预训练流匹配速度场的后验协方差迹等于其散度乘以已知时间依赖因子加常数（散度-不确定性恒等式）；矩阵形式仅依赖速度雅可比矩阵；该恒等式是精确且后验的，适用于任意预训练模型。

Result: 在MNIST上，每像素不确定性图集中在数字边界（样本间差异最大处），标量不确定性分数追踪实际预测误差，计算量仅为集成或MC dropout方法的约万分之一。

Conclusion: 不需要训练成本、推理成本或精度的权衡，任何预训练流匹配模型均可直接获得精确的后验不确定性，One-step生成器（如MeanFlow）只需单次前向传播即可获得端到端生成不确定性。

Abstract: Flow matching has become a leading framework for generative modeling, but quantifying the uncertainty of its samples remains an open problem. Existing approaches retrain the model with auxiliary variance heads, maintain costly ensembles, or propagate approximate covariance through many integration steps, trading off training cost, inference cost, or accuracy. We show that none of these trade-offs is necessary. We prove that, for any pre-trained flow matching velocity field, the trace of the posterior covariance over the clean data given the current state equals, in closed form, the divergence of the velocity field, up to a known time-dependent prefactor and an additive constant. We call this the \emph{divergence-uncertainty identity} for flow matching. The matrix-level form of the identity is similarly closed-form, depending solely on the velocity Jacobian. Because the identity is exact and post-hoc, it is computable on any pre-trained flow matching model, with no retraining and no architectural modification. For one-step generators such as MeanFlow, the same identity yields the exact end-to-end generation uncertainty in a single forward pass, eliminating the multi-step variance propagation required by all prior methods. Experiments on MNIST confirm that the resulting per-pixel uncertainty maps are semantically meaningful, concentrating on digit boundaries where inter-sample variation is highest, and that the scalar uncertainty score tracks actual prediction error, all at roughly 10,000$\times$ less total compute than ensembling or Monte Carlo dropout.

</details>


### [240] [Finite-Sample Analysis of Elimination in Active Hypothesis Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01039)
*Ziyuan Lin,Hoang Ngoc Nguyen,Jie Xu,Ivan Ruchkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种消除增强型Track-and-Stop算法，通过在主动假设检验中逐步剪枝对手集并重新分配感知资源，在有限样本下实现了更紧的追踪和集中常数，从而缩短了预期停止时间。实验验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全关键应用中，固定置信度的主动假设检验需要在有限样本下高效决策。现有方法的停止时间受非领先项影响较大，论文旨在通过假设消除机制来优化这一项，从而提升有限样本性能。

Method: 提出消除增强型Track-and-Stop算法：1) 逐步剪枝冠军特定的主动对手集；2) 将感知资源重新分配到存活的备选假设上；3) 引入激进参数来调节消除速度与置信保证之间的权衡。

Result: 推导出了预期停止时间的非渐近上界，证明消除带来的有限样本增益体现在非领先项尺度上（更紧的追踪和集中常数）。合成高斯数据实验验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 假设消除机制能有效提升主动假设检验的有限样本效率，其增益来自对更小假设集的更紧追踪；激进参数提供了一种灵活的权衡手段。

Abstract: A fixed-confidence, finite-sample problem of active hypothesis testing arises in many safety-critical applications. Situated in the context of sequential hypothesis testing, this paper studies the effect of hypothesis elimination on the stopping time. We introduce an elimination-augmented Track-and-Stop algorithm, in which champion-specific active-opponent sets are progressively pruned, and sensing effort is reallocated toward the surviving alternatives. Our analysis derives a non-asymptotic upper bound on the expected stopping time. The gain in finite-sample from elimination appears on the scale of the non-leading term, resulting from tighter tracking and concentration constants on the reduced hypothesis set. Furthermore, we introduce an aggressiveness parameter to modulate the trade-off between faster elimination and weaker confidence guarantee. An experimental study on synthetic Gaussian instances confirms the theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [241] [Learning in the Fisher Subspace: A Guided Initialization for LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01046)
*Zhi-Quan Feng,Ying-Jia Lin,Hung-Yu Kao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Fisher信息的数据感知LoRA初始化方法，通过利用下游数据产生的曲率信息来选择适配子空间，显著优于仅依赖预训练权重的传统初始化策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LoRA初始化方法仅依赖预训练权重的内在属性（如权重大小），假设权重几何本身能反映任务相关性，忽视了模型与下游数据分布之间的交互，导致初始化可能将容量分配给与任务无关的方向，严重制约下游性能。

Method: 将LoRA初始化形式化为在目标数据分布下识别参数空间中方向的影响程度，提出Fisher引导框架，利用下游数据诱导的曲率信息表征参数扰动对模型预测的影响，从而得到原则性的、任务依赖的方向选择准则。

Result: 跨多种任务和模态的实验表明，数据感知的初始化方法在一致性上显著优于现有方法，持续提升下游性能。

Conclusion: 数据感知的敏感性（而非仅权重大小）应当主导适配子空间的选择，本文的Fisher引导框架为这一观点提供了有效的实现路径。

Abstract: LoRA adapts large language models (LLMs) by restricting updates to low-rank subspaces of pre-trained weights. While this substantially reduces training cost, the effectiveness of adaptation critically depends on which subspace is chosen at initialization: a poor initialization that allocates capacity to task-irrelevant directions can severely hinder downstream performance. Existing initialization strategies primarily rely on the intrinsic properties of pre-trained weights, implicitly assuming that weight geometry alone reflects task relevance. However, such criteria overlook how the model interacts with the downstream data distribution. In this work, we formulate LoRA initialization as identifying the degree of impact of directions in parameter space under the target data distribution. We argue that data-aware sensitivity, rather than weight-only magnitude, should govern the choice of adaptation subspaces. Building on this perspective, we propose a Fisher-guided framework that leverages curvature information induced by downstream data to characterize how parameter perturbations influence model predictions. This perspective yields a principled, task-dependent criterion for selecting LoRA directions that better align adaptation with the target objective. Empirical results across diverse tasks and modalities demonstrate that data-aware initialization consistently and significantly improves downstream performance over existing approaches.

</details>


### [242] [LEAP: Layer-wise Exit-Aware Pretraining for Efficient Transformer Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01058)
*Shashank Kapadia,Deep Naryan Mishra,Sujal Reddy Alugubelli,Haoan Wang,Saipraveen Vabbilisetty,Rishi Bhatia,Anupriya Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现层对齐蒸馏与基于收敛的早停机制在Transformer推理中存在系统性不兼容——蒸馏会抑制中间层的表征收敛，导致早停失效。为此提出LEAP（逐层退出感知预训练），通过附加约束使中间层逼近最终层表征，在无需架构修改的情况下实现1.61倍实测加速（LEAP-MiniLM），而标准蒸馏模型加速为0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层对齐蒸馏与基于收敛的早停机制是Transformer推理效率的两大主流范式，但现有工作发现二者在标准部署条件下存在系统性不兼容：蒸馏目标使得中间层与教师表征对齐，反而抑制了早停机制所依赖的表征收敛性，导致早停在蒸馏模型上完全失效。

Method: 提出LEAP训练目标，在标准蒸馏基础上增加一个辅助约束，强制中间层逼近最终层表征，从而在不改变架构的前提下调和蒸馏与早停的矛盾。

Result: LEAP-MiniLM在batch=1、NVIDIA L4上达到1.61倍实测墙钟加速（θ=0.95），91.9%样本在层7前退出，理论层数缩减1.80倍；标准蒸馏模型有效加速为0。在STS-B（0.760±0.006）和BEIR基准上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: LEAP成功解决了蒸馏与早停的不兼容问题，为部署高效Transformer推理提供了包含延迟测量、决策阈值和部署准则的操作指导。

Abstract: Layer-aligned distillation and convergence-based early exit represent two predominant computational efficiency paradigms for transformer inference; yet we establish that they exhibit systematic incompatibility under standard deployment conditions for convergence-based early exit. Distillation objectives that align intermediate student layers to teacher representations suppress the representational convergence that early-exit mechanisms exploit, rendering such mechanisms ineffective on distilled models. We introduce LEAP (Layer-wise Exit-Aware Pretraining), an auxiliary training objective that reconciles this incompatibility. LEAP requires no architectural modifications; it augments standard distillation with a single constraint ensuring intermediate layers approximate final-layer representations. LEAP-MiniLM achieves 1.61$\times$ measured wall-clock speedup (batch=1, NVIDIA L4) at $θ$=0.95, with 91.9% of samples exiting by layer 7 and 1.80$\times$ theoretical layer reduction, where standard distilled models achieve zero effective speedup. We validate across sentence similarity (STS-B: 0.760 $\pm$ 0.006) and retrieval benchmarks (BEIR), providing operational guidance including latency measurements, decision thresholds, and deployment criteria.

</details>


### [243] [GEODE: Angle-Adaptive OOD Detection with Universal Scorer Compatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01063)
*Bruno Abrahao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GEODE方法，通过角度自适应的范数损失合成边界校准（无需额外数据），在七种OOD评分器上均表现优异（AUROC 89.0-95.0），解决了传统Outlier Exposure的评分器依赖问题，并从神经坍缩理论提供了四重定理证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Outlier Exposure虽强但存在评分器依赖（MSP强、KNN弱）且需要人工筛选辅助数据，缺乏对OE工作机制的理解。作者意图揭示OE的本质是边界校准而非OOD覆盖，并设计一种保持几何结构的合成方法替代OE。

Method: 1) 理论分析OE：发现OE的特征与真实近OOD数据处于相同几何轨迹，且边界相邻的四分位数贡献了几乎全部增益，证明OE本质是边界校准。2) 提出GEODE：基于神经坍缩理论设计角度自适应范数损失，每个样本的目标范数与到最近类均值的余弦相似度成比例，从而保留特征几何结构。3) 给出四个定理证明设计的合理性。

Result: CIFAR-10近OOD AUROC 89.0-92.3，远OOD达93.05；无评分器失效。CIFAR-100上全面超越OE。与OE联合使用达到MSP 95.0/KNN 94.8。WRN-28-10上Energy提升+4.5。相比PFS等方法（KNN仅14.38，低于随机），GEODE保持了几何结构。

Conclusion: OE的本质是边界校准而非OOD覆盖；GEODE通过合成边界校准在无需辅助数据的情况下实现全评分器鲁棒的OOD检测，验证了几何保持对于距离依赖性评分器的重要性。

Abstract: Outlier Exposure (OE) is among the strongest training-based OOD detectors on standard benchmarks but exhibits scorer-dependent tradeoffs (e.g., strong on MSP, weak on KNN) and requires curated auxiliary data. We show why OE works: its features sit at the same geometric locus as real near-OOD data, with the boundary-adjacent quartile driving nearly all of OE's gain. OE is boundary calibration, not OOD coverage. GEODE (GEOmetry-preserving DEtection) replicates this calibration synthetically through an angle-adaptive norm loss in which targets scale per-sample with cosine similarity to the nearest class mean, preserving feature geometry where boundary structure matters. Four theorems grounded in neural collapse justify the design. GEODE works across all seven standard scorers on CIFAR-10 (near-OOD AUROC 89.0-92.3, far-OOD reaching 93.05; no catastrophic failure on any scorer). Since the OOD regime is unknown at deployment, this is the test that matters. GEODE outperforms vanilla CE at matched epoch counts. Combined with OE, GEODE reaches 95.0 MSP / 94.8 KNN on CIFAR-10 and beats OE on every scorer on CIFAR-100. The gains hold on WRN-28-10 (+4.5 Energy, 3 seeds). Unlike methods that push OOD into the classifier null space (e.g., PFS, 14.38 KNN AUROC, worse than random), GEODE's adaptive target preserves the geometry that distance-based scorers depend on.

</details>


### [244] [A dimensional R2 regression metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01066)
*Jaesung Yoo,Stefan Lemke,Jian Zhong Guo,Kanaka Rajan,Adam Hantman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Dim-R2指标，扩展传统R2分数以支持任意维度数据、提供多维精度视图并降低噪声敏感性，在合成数据和真实多维回归数据集上验证了其优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统R2分数存在三个关键局限：仅限于最多二维输入、将精度简化为单一标量而隐藏了预测精度的丰富模式、对低方差噪声通道敏感易产生大而不可解释的负值。因此需要一种更灵活、可解释的多维回归评估指标。

Method: 提出Dimensional R2 score (Dim-R2)，是R2的简单扩展，能够接受任意维度数据，提供多维精度视图，并降低对噪声的敏感性。

Result: 在合成正弦数据和三个多维回归数据集上验证了Dim-R2相比传统R2的优势。

Conclusion: Dim-R2提供了一种可解释且灵活的回归评估指标，能够突出回归精度的模式，指导回归建模。

Abstract: R2 score is the standard metric for evaluating regression tasks, offering a normalized magnitude-agnostic measure of accuracy that captures variance. However, R2 has three key limitations: it is limited to at most two dimensional inputs, it reduces the score to a single scalar that hides rich patterns of prediction accuracy, and it is sensitive to low-variance noise channels which can yield large, uninterpretable negative values. We introduce the Dimensional R2 score (Dim-R2), a simple extension of R2 that accepts data of arbitrary dimensionality, provides a multidimensional view of accuracy, and reduces sensitivity to noise. We demonstrate its advantages on both synthetic sinusoidal data and three multidimensional regression datasets. Dim-R2 offers an interpretable and flexible metric that highlights patterns in regression accuracy, guiding regression modeling.

</details>


### [245] [Learning Discriminators for Resampling in the Ensemble Gaussian Mixture Filter through a Normalizing Flow Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01089)
*Zain Jabbar,Andrey A. Popov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对集成高斯混合滤波（EnGMF）在重采样阶段可能产生物理上不真实的后验样本问题，提出了一种判别器知情的重采样过程，通过归一化流学习判别器来接受或拒绝候选粒子，在Ikeda map和Lorenz '63系统上的实验表明，该方法在低集成规模下持续降低了误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EnGMF的重采样步骤可能生成物理上不真实的后验样本，导致后续预测无物理意义，需要一种机制来保证重采样粒子的物理合理性。

Method: 提出判别器知情的重采样过程：在EnGMF的后验重采样步骤中引入判别器，基于粒子的物理合理性进行接受或拒绝；判别器通过归一化流（normalizing flow）进行学习。

Result: 在Ikeda map和Lorenz '63系统上的数值实验表明，判别器知情的重采样过程在低集成规模下始终优于标准EnGMF，持续降低了误差。

Conclusion: 判别器知情的重采样能够有效改善EnGMF在低集成场景下的滤波精度，通过保障粒子的物理合理性提升了预测质量。

Abstract: The ensemble Gaussian mixture filter (EnGMF) is a powerful, convergent particle filter capable of medium-to-high dimensional non-linear filtering. The EnGMF relies on a resampling step that can generate physically unrealistic posterior samples, that would subsequently produce physically meaningless forecasts. This work introduces the discriminator-informed resampling procedure, that augments the posterior resampling step with a discriminator that accepts or rejects candidate particles based on their physical plausibility. In this work these discriminators are learned through a normalizing flow approach. Numerical experiments on both the Ikeda map and the Lorenz '63 system show that discriminator informed resampling procedure consistently reduces error relative to the standard EnGMF in low-ensemble regimes.

</details>


### [246] [Learning to Race in Minutes: Infoprop Dyna on the Mini Wheelbot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01096)
*Devdutt Subhasish,Henrik Hose,Sebastian Trimpe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Infoprop Dyna，一种基于不确定性感知的模型强化学习框架，使欠驱动独轮机器人Mini Wheelbot能够在11分钟的真实世界交互中学会在赛道上竞速，无需物理仿真器或域随机化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL方法依赖精心设计的物理仿真器和域随机化才能实现从仿真到真实的迁移，耗时且复杂。本文旨在绕过仿真器，实现机器人直接从真实世界交互中学习。

Method: 采用Infoprop Dyna，一种基于不确定性感知的模型强化学习框架，让机器人在真实世界中直接进行交互学习。

Result: 欠驱动独轮机器人Mini Wheelbot在11分钟的真实世界交互内学会了在赛道上竞速。

Conclusion: 不确定性感知的MBRL框架可以有效绕过仿真器需求，使具有快速、非线性、不稳定动态的机器人在合理时间内从真实世界交互中直接学习。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has the potential to enable robots with fast, nonlinear, and unstable dynamics to reach the limits of their performance. However, most recent advances rely on carefully designed physics-based simulators and domain randomization to achieve successful sim-to-real transfer within reasonable wall-clock time. In this work, we bypass the need for such simulators and demonstrate that Infoprop Dyna, a state-of-the-art uncertainty-aware model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) framework, can enable robots to learn directly from real-world interactions. Using Infoprop Dyna, the Mini Wheelbot, an underactuated unicycle robot, learns to race around a track within 11 minutes of real-world experience.

</details>


### [247] [Topological Neural Tangent Kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01110)
*Sanjukta Krishnagopal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出拓扑神经切线核（TopoNTK），将图神经切线核推广到单纯复形上的高阶交互，通过上下Hodge交互捕捉拓扑结构，并揭示了拓扑谱偏差现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图神经切线核仅能处理成对关系（图结构），无法建模单纯复形中的高阶交互（如填充单纯形），缺乏对拓扑结构的敏感性。

Method: 引入TopoNTK，结合下Hodge交互（通过共享顶点捕捉类图耦合）和上Hodge交互（通过填充单纯形捕捉耦合），利用Hodge分解将边信号分解为梯度、调和与环流分量。

Result: 在合成单纯形任务和DBLP高阶链接预测上验证了表达性、Hodge对齐、谱学习与稳定性性质。

Conclusion: 拓扑不仅是额外结构，还能为关系学习提供更忠实、可解释且有效的坐标。

Abstract: Graph neural tangent kernels give a principled infinite-width theory for graph neural networks, but inherit a basic limitation of graph models: they see only pairwise structure. Many relational systems contain higher-order interactions that are more naturally represented by simplicial complexes. We introduce the Topological Neural Tangent Kernel (TopoNTK), an infinite-width kernel for simplicial message passing on edge features. TopoNTK combines lower Hodge interactions, capturing graph-like coupling through shared vertices, with upper Hodge interactions, capturing coupling through filled simplices. This makes the kernel sensitive to topology invisible to graph kernels, allowing complexes with the same graph but different filled simplices to induce different kernels. Beyond expressivity, the Hodge structure gives the kernel an interpretable learning geometry. Edge signals decompose into gradient-like, harmonic, and local circulation components, and the spectrum of the TopoNTK determines how quickly each component is learned. This yields a topological form of spectral bias: components aligned with large-eigenvalue modes are learned quickly, while global harmonic modes, retained through the residual channel, often lie at smaller eigenvalues and are learned more slowly. We prove expressivity, Hodge-alignment, spectral learning, and stability properties, and validate them on synthetic simplicial tasks and DBLP higher-order link prediction. The results show that topology is not merely extra structure; it can provide coordinates that make relational learning more faithful, interpretable, and effective.

</details>


### [248] [When Less is Enough: Efficient Inference via Collaborative Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01111)
*Yilei Chen,Sharut Gupta,Yannis Paschalidis,Ayush Sekhari,Aldo Pacchiano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DUET是一种双模型协作推理框架，通过将推理过程分解为两个阶段（能力模型生成推理信号，轻量模型解读信号生成最终答案），并采用长度惩罚联合训练目标，在保持推理性能的同时，可节省大模型高达60%的输出token成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单个大模型进行端到端推理预测的推理成本过高，需要探索更高效的推理范式来降低计算开销。

Method: 提出DUET双阶段协作推理框架：能力模型先生成推理信号，轻量模型再解读该信号生成最终答案；并设计长度惩罚联合训练目标，鼓励能力模型仅传输对轻量模型解决问题足够的信息，避免冗余输出。

Result: 在AIME和GPQA等具有挑战性的推理基准上，相比单独使用大模型进行端到端推理，DUET节省了高达60%的大模型输出token，同时保持强推理性能。

Conclusion: DUET通过将推理密集型计算交给能力模型、非推理密集型部分委托给轻量模型，实现了在大幅降低推理成本的同时保持任务性能，是一种高效的协作推理方案。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce DUET (Dual-model Efficient Two-stage inference), a collaborative inference framework in which a capable model and a lightweight model work together to solve a task. Relying on a single large model to perform end-to-end reasoning and prediction often incurs substantial inference cost. In contrast, DUET decomposes inference into two stages: the capable model produces a reasoning signal, and the lightweight model interprets this signal to generate the final answer, allowing reasoning-intensive computation to be handled by the capable model while non-reasoning-intensive components are delegated to the lightweight model without sacrificing task performance. To achieve this objective, we propose a length-penalized joint training objective that encourages the capable model to transmit only the information that is sufficient for the lightweight model to solve the task. As a result, DUET maintains strong reasoning performance with substantially lower inference cost than end-to-end inference using a large model alone, saving up to 60% of the large model's output tokens on challenging reasoning benchmarks, including AIME and GPQA.

</details>


### [249] [Machine Learning-Augmented Acceleration of Iterative Ptychographic Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01122)
*Bowen Zheng,Katayun Kamdin,David Shapiro,Alexander Ditter,Dayne Sasaki,Emma Bernard,Roopali Kukreja,Petrus H. Zwart,Slavomír Nemšák,Apurva Mehta,Nicholas Schwarz,Alexander Hexemer,Tanny Chavez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种机器学习增强的迭代叠层成像重构算法，通过学习快速前馈算子加速收敛，在保持物理一致性的前提下实现两倍以上的加速，并已部署到同步辐射束线实际生产中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统迭代叠层成像重构算法在实际实验条件下收敛缓慢，需要大量迭代才能达到满意的重构质量，影响实时实验操作的效率。

Method: 在标准迭代重构过程中引入一个机器学习训练的快速前馈算子：先进行标准迭代预热，然后由学习的前馈算子将重构状态快速推进到更收敛的状态，之后恢复常规迭代更新。

Result: 在多个叠层成像数据集上训练，并在不同年份采集的实验数据上验证，表现出鲁棒性和时间泛化能力；相比传统方法，重构质量相当，但Poisson负对数似然收敛速度更快，实际运行时间降低两倍以上。

Conclusion: 该方法兼顾了物理一致性和灵活性，已集成到现有重构流水线并在同步辐射束线投产，证明了其在实时实验操作中的实用性。

Abstract: Iterative ptychographic reconstruction algorithms are widely used for coherent diffractive imaging but can exhibit slow convergence under realistic experimental conditions. We propose a machine learning-augmented approach that accelerates iterative ptychographic reconstruction by introducing a learned fast-forward operator applied during reconstruction. Following an initial warm-up using standard iterations, the fast-forward operator advances the reconstruction toward a more converged state, after which conventional iterative updates are resumed. This strategy preserves the physical consistency and flexibility of established ptychographic solvers while reducing the number of iterations required for convergence. The model is trained on diverse ptychographic datasets and evaluated on experimental data acquired in a different year, demonstrating robustness and temporal generalization. Compared with conventional iterative solvers, the machine learning-augmented method achieves comparable reconstruction quality while converging faster in terms of Poisson negative log-likelihood, yielding over a two-fold reduction in wall-clock time. The approach has been integrated into an existing reconstruction pipeline and deployed in production at a synchrotron beamline, demonstrating practicality for real-time experimental operation.

</details>


### [250] [Extreme Weather Bench: A framework and benchmark for evaluation of high-impact weather](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01126)
*Amy McGovern,Taylor Mandelbaum,Daniel Rothenberg,Nicholas Loveday,Corey Potvin,Montgomery Flora,Linus Magnusson,Eric Gilleland,John Allen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 Extreme Weather Bench (EWB)，一个社区驱动的开源基准测试套件，用于评估AI和数值天气预报模型在多种高影响天气事件上的表现，提供标准化案例研究、观测数据、基于影响的指标和开源代码，旨在提升模型的可信度和促进公平比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI天气模型的评估存在局限性：要么进行全球尺度评估，要么手动挑选少量案例或特定区域，缺乏一个标准化、开源、专注于高影响天气的基准测试套件来推动模型验证和科学进步。

Method: EWB提供了一个标准化的案例研究集合（涵盖多个时空尺度和天气谱系的不同部分）、观测数据、基于影响的指标以及开源代码，供用户评估其模型。该系统是社区驱动的，并将持续演化以纳入更多现象、测试案例和指标。

Result: EWB作为开源社区驱动的基准套件，能够帮助验证模型在标准化案例上的表现，特别是对公众产生高影响的事件，从而提升AI模型的可信度，并实现跨模型的真正比较。

Conclusion: EWB将推动所有天气模型的科学发展，通过案例研究实现对特定高影响现象的模型评估，并与全球天气和预报验证社区合作持续演进。

Abstract: Forecasting the wide variety of high-impact weather events experienced globally is a challenge for both Artificial Intelligence (AI) and Numerical Weather Prediction (NWP) models and it is critical that such models be properly verified before deployment. Although AI weather models are rapidly evolving, much of their evaluation is currently done either with a global-scale evaluation or by hand-picking a small number of case studies or a region. A widely-used open-source benchmark suite focusing on high-impact weather will help to drive the science forward for all scales of weather models, as it has for other AI fields. Here we introduce Extreme Weather Bench (EWB), a new community-driven benchmark suite that facilitates model validation and verification on a variety of high-impact hazards that matter to people around the globe. EWB provides a standard set of case studies (spanning across multiple spatial and temporal scales and different parts of the weather spectrum), observational data, impact-based metrics, and open-source code for users to evaluate their models. Verifying that a model works against a standard set of case studies, especially events that are high-impact for the general public, is a key piece of improving the trustworthiness of AI models. EWB will help to drive the science forward for all weather models, enabling true comparisons across models and evaluating models on specific high-impact phenomena through the use of case studies. EWB is a free open-source community-driven system and will continue to evolve to include additional phenomena, test cases and metrics in collaboration with the worldwide weather and forecast verification community.

</details>


### [251] [Forager: a lightweight testbed for continual learning with partial observability in RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01131)
*Steven Tang,Xinze Xiong,Anna Hakhverdyan,Andrew Patterson,Jacob Adkins,Jiamin He,Esraa Elelimy,Parham Mohammad Panahi,Martha White,Adam White*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Forager，一个轻量级、部分可观测的持续强化学习环境，用于研究智能体在持续学习中的塑性损失问题。实验表明，利用状态构建是缓解塑性损失最有效的方法，并验证了当前CRL智能体在无限新任务流中的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CRL实验多聚焦于完全可观测MDP中加入非平稳性的单次实验，忽视了部分可观测性和记忆/循环机制的重要性；原因在于部分可观测CRL环境计算成本过高。

Method: 设计Forager环境：轻量级、恒定内存占用、部分可观测。在该环境中进行实验，测试当前CRL智能体性能，并评估塑性损失缓解方法的有效性。

Result: 智能体在Forager中表现出塑性损失；提出的缓解方法有一定效果，但利用状态构建（state construction）最为有效；在无限新任务流变体中，当前CRL智能体的局限性被清晰暴露。

Conclusion: Forager是一个有效的部分可观测CRL基准环境；缓解塑性损失的关键在于状态构建；现有CRL智能体在持续学习新任务方面仍有明显不足。

Abstract: In continual reinforcement learning (CRL), good performance requires never-ending learning, acting, and exploration in a big, partially observable world. Most CRL experiments have focused on loss of plasticity -- the inability to keep learning -- in one-off experiments where some unobservable non-stationarity is added to classic fully observable MDPs. Further, these experiments rarely consider the role of partial observability and the importance of CRL agents that use memory or recurrence. One potential reason for this focus on mitigating loss of plasticity without considering partial observability is that many partially-observable CRL environments are prohibitively expensive. In this paper, we introduce Forager, a light-weight partially-observable CRL environment with a constant memory footprint. We provide a set of experiments and sample tasks demonstrating that Forager is challenging for current CRL agents and yet also allows for in-depth study of those agents. We demonstrate that agents exhibit loss of plasticity, proposed mitigations can help, but that most useful is to leverage state construction. We conclude with a variant of Forager that generates an unending stream of new tasks to learn that clearly highlights the limitations of current CRL agents.

</details>


### [252] [Metric-Normalized Posterior Leakage (mPL): Attacker-Aligned Privacy for Joint Consumption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01137)
*Gaoyi Chen,Minghao Li,Weishi Shi,Yan Huang,Yusheng Wei,Sourabh Yadav,Chenxi Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出度量归一化后验泄露（mPL）作为联合观测场景下的隐私度量，发现传统度量差分隐私（mDP）在联合观测下仍存在高泄露风险，并设计了自适应mPL（AmPL）框架通过扰动-审计-自适应机制实现概率有界mPL（PBmPL），在词嵌入案例中验证了其有效降低泄露频率且保持低效用损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 度量差分隐私（mDP）在独立发布场景下可提供距离校准的本地差分隐私保证，但在机器学习系统的联合观测（joint observation）场景中，模型无关的逐记录隐私保证无法捕捉来自证据聚合（evidence aggregation）的泄露风险，因此需要一种攻击者对齐的、可量化联合观测下隐私泄露的新度量。

Method: (1) 形式化度量归一化后验泄露（mPL），定义为释放导致的后验几率偏移的距离校准度量；(2) 证明单次或独立发布下均匀有界mPL等价于mDP；(3) 刻画联合观测下mDP仍可导致高mPL的问题；(4) 提出概率有界mPL（PBmPL）限制mPL超预算的频率；(5) 设计自适应mPL（AmPL）框架，包含扰动、基于学习型攻击者的审计、参数自适应（可选贝叶斯重映射）三阶段。

Result: 在词嵌入案例中，神经攻击者在联合消费场景下能够违反mPL约束，尽管逐记录已施加mDP扰动；而AmPL框架显著降低了此类违反的频率，同时保持了较低的效用损失。

Conclusion: PBmPL为联合消费场景提供了一种实用的可认证保护机制，AmPL框架能够在隐私与效用之间取得良好平衡，弥补了传统mDP在联合观测下的不足。

Abstract: Metric differential privacy (mDP) strengthens local differential privacy (LDP) by scaling noise to semantic distance, but many machine learning (ML) systems are consumed under joint observation, where model-agnostic, per-record guarantees can miss leakage from evidence aggregation. We introduce metric-normalized posterior leakage (mPL), an attacker-aligned, distance-calibrated measure of posterior-odds shift induced by releases, and show that for single or independent releases, uniformly bounding mPL is equivalent to mDP. Under joint observation, however, satisfying mDP may still leave mPL high because learned aggregators compound evidence across correlated items. To make control practical, we formalize probabilistically bounded mPL (PBmPL), which limits how often mPL may exceed a target budget, and we operationalize it via Adaptive mPL (AmPL), a trust-and-verify framework that perturbs, audits with a learned attacker, and adapts parameters (with optional Bayesian remapping) to balance privacy and utility. In a word-embedding case study, neural adversaries violate mPL under joint consumption despite per-record mDP perturbations, whereas AmPL substantially lowers the frequency of such violations with low utility loss, indicating PBmPL as a practical, certifiable protection for joint-consumption settings.

</details>


### [253] [Multi-Perspective Transformers in ARC-AGI-2 Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01154)
*Caleb Talley,Vedant Tibrewal,Seun Adekunle,Weiwen Dong,Xinyu Wu,Fariha Sheikh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于TinyLM结合测试时训练（TTT）和专家乘积（POE）的方法，在ARC-AGI-2视觉谜题基准上取得训练集96.1%、评估集21.7%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARC-AGI-2基准测试旨在衡量机器从有限样例中泛化、解释符号含义并灵活应用规则的能力，现有方法在此类人类直觉性视觉谜题上表现有限。

Method: 采用TinyLM作为基础模型，并在测试时额外进行微调，具体包括测试时训练（Test-Time-Training, TTT）和专家乘积（Products of Experts, POE）两种策略。

Result: 在ARC-AGI-2训练集上达到96.1%准确率，在评估集上达到21.7%准确率。

Conclusion: 结合测试时微调策略能够有效提升模型在少样本泛化任务上的表现，但评估集与训练集之间的显著性能差距表明该基准仍然具有较大挑战性。

Abstract: ARC-AGI-2 is a benchmark of human-intuitive visual puzzles that measures a machine's ability to generalize from limited examples, interpret symbolic meaning, and flexibly apply rules in varying contexts. In this paper, we discuss our approach to solving the ARC-AGI-2 puzzles with TinyLM, with additional fine-tuning at test time, including Test-Time-Training (TTT) and Products of Experts (POE). Our model achieves 96.1% accuracy on the training set and 21.7% accuracy on the evaluation set.

</details>


### [254] [Minimizing Collateral Damage in Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01167)
*Tam Nguyen,Tu Anh Nguyen,Sina Alemohammad,Richard G. Baraniuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于约束优化的激活引导方法，通过考虑激活的二阶矩矩阵来最小化非目标特征的期望平方附带变化，从而在控制LLM行为时减少对无关任务性能的损害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的激活引导方法（如向量加法）会导致“附带损害”——即非目标特征方向的激活对齐发生意外改变。这是因为标准方法隐含地假设非目标特征是各向同性的，而实际并非如此。本文旨在数学化定义并解决这一问题。

Method: 将引导建模为约束优化问题，寻找新激活使得非目标特征的期望平方附带变化最小化，并以激活的经验二阶矩矩阵作为权重。这一权重编码了不同特征方向上扰动的非均匀代价，区别于各向同性方法对所有方向统一惩罚。

Result: 通过考虑激活的经验二阶矩，该方法实现了更精确的控制，同时减少了对无关任务模型性能的退化。

Conclusion: 考虑激活分布的异质性（二阶矩）能有效降低引导操作对非目标特征的副作用，实现更精准的模型行为控制。

Abstract: Activation steering is a method for controlling Large Language Model (LLM) behavior by intervening in its internal representations to increase the alignment with a specific target feature direction. However, standard interventions, such as vector addition, often cause ``collateral damage", defined as unintended changes in the alignment of activations along other non-target feature directions. This damage occurs because standard methods implicitly assume the isotropy of non-target features. In this work, we provide a mathematical formalization of collateral damage and introduce a principled framework that models steering as a constrained optimization problem. Our method finds a new activation that minimizes the expected squared collateral change weighted by the empirical second-moment matrix of activations. This weighting encodes the nonuniform cost of the perturbation in different feature directions, in contrast to isotropic approaches that penalize changes uniformly in all feature directions.
