By accounting for the empirical second-moment of activations, our approach achieves more precise control while reducing the degradation of model performance on unrelated tasks.

</details>


### [255] [A Theory of Generalization in Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01172)
*Elon Litman,Gabe Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了深度学习泛化性的非渐近理论，通过经验神经切分函数将输出空间划分为信号和噪声通道。信号通道中误差快速衰减，噪声通道中残差被零特征值陷阱捕获。证明了即使在核函数发生O(1)算子范数演化的完全特征学习机制下，泛化性依然成立。该理论解释了良性过拟合、双下降、隐式偏差和顿悟（grokking）等现象，并推导出一个无需验证数据的精确种群风险目标函数，可作为Adam上的SNR预处理器，加速顿悟5倍、抑制PINNs中的记忆化、改进DPO微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习理论缺乏统一的非渐近视角来解释泛化性，尤其是在特征学习机制（kernel evolution为O(1)）下的行为。需要一种能够同时解释良性过拟合、双下降、隐式偏差、顿悟等看似矛盾现象的理论框架。

Method: 通过经验神经切分函数（empirical NTK）将输出空间划分为信号通道和噪声通道。在信号通道中使用minibatch SGD的线性漂移分析，在噪声通道中利用核函数的近零特征值形成"测试不可见"残差陷阱。理论推导在kernel evolution为O(1)下仍成立，并构造了一个可计算的种群风险目标函数（SNR预处理器）。

Result: 证明了泛化性在核函数O(1)算子范数演化下依然成立；导出的种群风险目标函数在实践中可作为Adam的SNR预处理器，仅增加一个状态向量且无额外开销；加速顿悟5倍，抑制PINNs和隐式神经表示中的记忆化，改进带噪偏好下的DPO微调，同时保持与参考策略3倍的距离。

Conclusion: 该理论统一了深度学习中多个离散现象，提供了一个可计算、无验证数据的泛化性度量工具，具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: We present a non-asymptotic theory of generalization in deep learning where the empirical neural tangent kernel partitions the output space. In directions corresponding to signal, error dissipates rapidly; in the vast orthogonal dimensions corresponding to noise, the kernel's near-zero eigenvalues trap residual error in a test-invisible reservoir. Within the signal channel, minibatch SGD ensures that coherent population signal accumulates via fast linear drift, while idiosyncratic memorization is suppressed into a slow, diffusive random walk. We prove generalization survives even when the kernel evolves $\mathcal{O}(1)$ in operator norm, the full feature-learning regime. This theory naturally explains disparate phenomena in deep learning theory, such as benign overfitting, double descent, implicit bias, and grokking. Lastly, we derive an exact population-risk objective from a single training run with no validation data, for any architecture, loss, or optimizer, and prove that it measures precisely the noise in the signal channel. This objective reduces in practice to an SNR preconditioner on top of Adam, adding one state vector at no extra cost; it accelerates grokking by $5 \times$, suppresses memorization in PINNs and implicit neural representations, and improves DPO fine-tuning under noisy preferences while staying $3 \times$ closer to the reference policy.

</details>


### [256] [Linear-Readout Floors and Threshold Recovery in Computation in Superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01192)
*Hector Borobia,Elies Seguí-Mas,Guillermina Tormo-Carbó*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证了两类叠加计算方法（Hänni等人的近似线性递归模板与Adler & Shavit的阈值布尔恢复）因保持不同的接口不变量而得出不同递归容量标度，并非相互矛盾。通过推导双正交线性读出器的Welch型下界，并分析线性读出与非线性阈值恢复在二次特征负载下的性能差异，解释了$d^{3/2}$标度为特定模板的兼容性阈值而非通用上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调和Hänni等人与Adler & Shavit在叠加计算中关于可递归容量标度的看似矛盾的结果（$O(d^{3/2})$ vs 近二次容量），澄清分歧根源在于保持了不同的接口不变量。

Method: 1) 形式化区分两类方法保持的接口不变量；2) 推导双正交线性读出器的秩-迹Welch型下界，证明单位对角线性读出器的最坏情况串扰下界为$Ω(d^{-1/2})$；3) 对比线性读出与随机支持阈值恢复在二次特征负载$F=d^2$下的性能差异；4) 将Welch下界与Hänni校正层容差匹配，解释$d^{3/2}$标度的由来。

Result: 1) 双正交线性读出器最坏情况串扰下界为$Ω(d^{-1/2})$，对单位范数紧框架平均而言是紧的；2) 在$F=d^2$时，随机支持阈值恢复对稀疏度$s=O(d/\log d)$成功，而线性读出器在Bernoulli稀疏状态上仍有$Ω(s/d)$的平均每坐标平方误差；3) $d^{3/2}$标度是Hänni模板的兼容性阈值，非通用上界。

Conclusion: 两种递归容量标度非矛盾，因保持不同接口不变量；线性读出存在基础性串扰下界限制了近似线性模板；非线性鲁棒重置的扩展仍是开放问题。

Abstract: Two recent approaches to computation in superposition reach different recursive capacity regimes: Hänni et al. certify $\tilde{O}(d^{3/2})$ computable features in width $d$ via an approximate-linear recursive template, while Adler and Shavit reach near-quadratic capacity (up to logarithmic factors) using thresholded Boolean recovery. The main contribution of this paper is conceptual: we argue these results are not contradictory because they maintain different interface invariants, and we formalize the distinction.
  As a tool, we record a rank-trace Welch-type lower bound for biorthogonal linear readouts: for $F \gg d$, the worst-case off-diagonal cross-talk of any unit-diagonal linear readout is $Ω(d^{-1/2})$, and the bound is tight on average for unit-norm tight frames. At quadratic feature load $F=d^2$, random-support threshold recovery succeeds for sparsities $s=O(d/\log d)$, while linear readouts still incur $Ω(s/d)$ average per-coordinate squared error on Bernoulli sparse states. Matching the Welch floor against the published tolerance of the Hänni correction layer explains the $d^{3/2}$ scale as a compatibility threshold for that template, not a universal upper bound. Robust nonlinear reset beyond the Hänni template is left open.

</details>


### [257] [Focus and Dilution: The Multi-stage Learning Process of Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01199)
*Zheng-An Chen,Pengxiao Lin,Zhi-Qin John Xu,Tao Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过梯度流分析，在单层Transformer和马尔可夫数据设定下，识别并严格解释了注意力学习中存在的循环“聚焦-稀释”周期，并分解为多个阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型在众多领域取得巨大成功，但其训练动力学的理解仍然有限，本文旨在揭示注意力学习中的动态机制。

Method: 采用梯度流分析，在临界点附近进行逐阶段线性化，将聚焦-稀释循环分解为嵌入与投影的快速秩一结构凝聚、注意力参数增长引发高频聚焦、嵌入扰动导致质量重分配并稀释注意力、低频token不对称性打破退化临界点开启新周期等阶段。

Result: 实验在合成马尔可夫数据、WikiText和TinyStories上验证了所预测的阶段和循环动态。

Conclusion: 揭示了Transformer注意力训练中的循环聚焦-稀释动力学，为理解其训练过程提供了严格的理论基础。

Abstract: Transformer-based models have achieved remarkable success across a wide range of domains, yet our understanding of their training dynamics remains limited. In this work, we identify a recurrent focus-dilution cycle in attention learning and provide a rigorous explanation in a one-layer Transformer setting for Markovian data via gradient-flow analysis. Using stage-wise linearization around critical points, we show that a single focus-dilution cycle can be decomposed into a sequence of distinct stages. First, embedding and projection rapidly condense to a rank-one structure, while attention parameters remain effectively frozen. Then, the attention parameters begin to increase, inducing a frequency-driven focus toward high-frequency tokens. As attention continues to evolve, it generates next-order perturbations in embeddings, leading to a mass-redistribution mechanism that progressively dilutes this focus. Finally, small asymmetries among low-frequency tokens lift a degenerate critical point, opening new embedding directions and initiating the next cycle. Experiments on synthetic Markovian data as well as WikiText and TinyStories corroborate the predicted stages and cyclical dynamics.

</details>


### [258] [Local Hessian Spectral Filtering for Robust Intrinsic Dimension Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01221)
*Genki Osada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出局部Hessian谱维度(LHSD)，通过对log密度Hessian进行谱滤波来估计局部本征维度，解决了高维空间中扩散模型LID估计失效的问题，且实现了线性复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的局部本征维度(LID)估计方法在高维空间中失效，因为大量法线方向的噪声淹没了切线信号，导致估计不准确。

Method: 提出LHSD方法，对log密度Hessian矩阵进行谱滤波，显式截断与法线方向相关的大特征值，保留零曲率切线方向。利用随机Lanczos求积(SLQ)实现，避免完整Hessian构造，达到O(D)线性复杂度。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上验证了LHSD的鲁棒性优势，并成功应用于大规模扩散模型中的记忆化检测。

Conclusion: LHSD通过谱滤波策略有效解决了高维空间LID估计的噪声干扰问题，且计算效率高，实用性强。

Abstract: While diffusion models enable new approaches for estimating Local Intrinsic Dimension (LID), existing methods fail in high-dimensional spaces where noise from vast normal directions overwhelms the tangent signal. We propose Local Hessian Spectral Dimension (LHSD), which resolves this by applying spectral filtering to the log-density Hessian, explicitly cutting off large eigenvalues associated with normal directions to count zero-curvature tangent directions. Implemented using Stochastic Lanczos Quadrature (SLQ), LHSD avoids full Hessian construction, achieving linear scalability with dimension $D$. Experiments on synthetic and real data confirm LHSD's superior robustness and its utility in detecting memorization in large-scale diffusion models.

</details>


### [259] [Arbitrarily Conditioned Hierarchical Flows for Spatiotemporal Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01226)
*Keyan Chen,Qiwei Yuan,Zhitong Xu,Bin Shen,Shandian Zhe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ARCH（任意条件层次流模型），一个基于层次流匹配的框架，用于时空事件建模。它采用历史编码器-生成解码器架构和混合掩码策略，支持任意条件推断（如预测、逆向推理、轨迹重建），在合成和真实数据上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有点过程模型依赖强结构假设，局限于自回归逐事件预测，无法支持逆向推理、轨迹重建和缺失位置恢复等更广泛的推断任务。

Method: 提出了ARCH框架，包含层次流匹配、历史编码器-生成解码器架构，以及一种混合掩码策略，实现对任意观测事件的灵活条件设置，统一处理预测、逆向推断和部分轨迹恢复。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，ARCH在预测和条件推断任务上均持续优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: ARCH是一种表达能力强且计算可处理的时空事件建模框架，能够有效捕获复杂事件分布，支持多种条件推断任务，展现出优越的综合性能。

Abstract: Events in spatiotemporal systems are ubiquitous, yet modeling their complex distributions remains challenging. Existing point process models often rely on strong structural assumptions and are typically limited to autoregressive, event-by-event prediction. As a result, they struggle to support broader inference tasks such as inverse inference, trajectory reconstruction, and recovery of missing event locations. We introduce Arbitrarily Conditioned Hierarchical Flows (ARCH), a hierarchical flow matching framework for spatiotemporal event modeling. ARCH is expressive enough to capture complex event distributions while enabling tractable and accurate computation of conditional intensities, which quantify instantaneous event risk. Built on a history-encoder-generative-decoder architecture, ARCH introduces a hybrid masking strategy for flexible conditioning on arbitrary observed events. This enables a unified treatment of forecasting, inverse inference, and partial trajectory recovery within a single framework. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets show that ARCH consistently outperforms existing baselines across both prediction and conditional inference tasks.

</details>


### [260] [Attention Sinks in Massively Multilingual Neural Machine Translation:Discovery, Analysis, and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01229)
*Hillary Mutisya,John Mugane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NLLB-200(600M)的跨注意力分析中，非内容词元（结束符、语言标签、标点）占用了83%-91%的注意力权重（称为"注意力汇"），导致原始指标严重低估内容相似度。通过内容过滤方法可恢复真实语言结构信号，揭示教师强制与生成模式间的显著差异及语言簇聚类特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NMT中广泛使用跨注意力模式研究多语言模型的对齐结构，但现有分析未注意到非内容词元主导注意力权重的系统性伪影，导致不可靠的结论。

Method: (1) 识别NLLB-200跨注意力中的"注意力汇"现象并分析其词表设计根源；(2) 提出内容过滤方法（移除非内容词元+重归一化）；(3) 在非洲语言（斯瓦希里语、基库尤语、索马里语、卢奥语）和非非洲语言（德语、土耳其语、中文、印地语）的1000个平行句上验证。

Result: (1) 非内容词元捕获83%-91%注意力；(2) 原始内容相似度36.7% vs 过滤后70.7%；(3) 教师强制与生成模式差16.9个百分点；(4) 注意力熵呈现语言家族簇；(5) 发现索马里语SOV语序与单调对齐的隐藏悖论。

Conclusion: 跨注意力中的注意力汇伪影是普遍的，必须进行内容过滤才能获得可靠的语言结构分析；该工作为多语言NMT可解释性研究提供了方法和数据基础。

Abstract: Cross-attention patterns in neural machine translation (NMT) are widely used to study how multilingual models align linguistic structure. We report a systematic artifact in cross-attention analysis of NLLB-200 (600M): non-content tokens - primarily end-of-sequence tokens, language tags, and punctuation - capture 83 percent to 91 percent of total cross-attention mass. We term these "attention sinks," extending findings from LLMs [Xiao et al., 2023] to NMT cross-attention and identifying a causal mechanism rooted in vocabulary design rather than position bias. This artifact causes raw metrics to underestimate content-level similarity by nearly half (36.7 percent raw vs. 70.7 percent filtered), rendering uncorrected analyses unreliable. To address this, we validate a content-only filtering methodology that removes non-content tokens and renormalizes the distribution. Applying this to 1,000 parallel sentences across African languages (Swahili, Kikuyu, Somali, Luo) and non-African benchmarks (German, Turkish, Chinese, Hindi), we confirm the artifact is universal and recover masked linguistic signals: a 16.9 percentage-point gap between teacher-forcing and generation modes, clear language-family clustering in attention entropy, and a hidden Somali paradox linking SOV word order to monotonic alignment. We release our filtering toolkit and corrected datasets to support reproducible interpretability research on multilingual NMT.

</details>


### [261] [Rhamba: Region-Aware Hybrid Attention-Mamba Framework for Self-Supervised Learning in Resting-State fMRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01240)
*Ruthwik Reddy Doodipala,Pankaj Pandey,Pratheek Eranki,Carolina Torres-Rojas,Manob Jyoti Saikia,Ranganatha Sitaram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Rhamba框架，结合区域感知掩码（Any/Majority/Pure三种策略）与混合Attention-Mamba架构用于fMRI自监督预训练。在ABIDE数据集预训练后，在COBRE和ADHD-200上微调分类。结果表明：掩码策略强烈影响重构损失，但下游性能差异较小且依赖数据集；混合MA架构平均AUROC最高，整体优于SOTA方法；最佳性能取决于掩码策略与架构的交互而非单一配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区域感知掩码和混合序列建模（Attention-Mamba）在大规模神经影像自监督预训练中的影响尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出Rhamba框架，包含：(1) 基于解剖分区的对齐补丁嵌入；(2) 三种区域感知掩码策略（Any/Majority/Pure）；(3) 四种架构变体（纯Mamba、交替Mamba-Attention、AM编码器-解码器、MA编码器-解码器）；(4) 在ABIDE上预训练，在COBRE和ADHD-200上微调分类；使用Integrated Gradients进行可解释性分析。

Result: (1) 重构损失：Any > Majority > Pure，掩码策略影响显著；(2) 下游性能差异较小且依赖数据集；(3) MA混合架构平均AUROC最高；(4) Rhamba优于SOTA方法；(5) 最优性能取决于掩码策略与架构的交互。

Conclusion: Rhamba提供了一个灵活框架，能在可解释性、可扩展性和性能之间取得平衡，适用于大规模fMRI表征学习。最佳配置依赖于特定任务和数据集，而非单一通用设置。

Abstract: Self-supervised pretraining is promising for large-scale neuroimaging, yet the impact of region-aware masking and hybrid sequence modeling remains underexplored. In this work, we introduce Rhamba, a region-aware pretraining framework that integrates anatomically guided masking with hybrid Attention-Mamba architectures for resting state functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) analysis. Models were pretrained on the ABIDE dataset using region-aligned patch embeddings and three masking strategies (Any, Majority, and Pure) with increasing spatial specificity. We evaluated four architectural variants: a Mamba only model, an Alternate architecture with interleaved Mamba and Attention blocks, and two hybrid encoder-decoder configurations (Attention-Mamba (AM) and Mamba-Attention (MA)). The pretrained models were fine-tuned on downstream classification tasks using the COBRE and ADHD-200 datasets for schizophrenia and attention-deficit/hyperactivity disorder discrimination. We employed Integrated Gradients, an explainable AI method, to identify the brain regions contributing to model predictions. Masking strategy strongly influenced reconstruction behavior, with reconstruction loss following a consistent ordering (Any > Majority > Pure). However, this trend did not directly translate into downstream performance, where differences were modest and dataset-dependent. The hybrid architecture with the MA configuration achieved the highest average AUROC across both datasets, and Rhamba outperformed state-of-the-art methods in comparative evaluation. Region-wise analysis showed that peak performance depends on the interaction between masking strategy and architecture rather than a single dominant configuration. Overall, Rhamba offers a flexible framework for balancing interpretability, scalability, and performance in large-scale fMRI representation learning.

</details>


### [262] [S^3-R1: Learning to Retrieve and Answer Step-by-Step with Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01248)
*Harsh Goel,Akhil Udathu,Susmija Jabireddy,Pradnesh Kalkar,Atharva Parulekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: S^3-R1通过合成数据生成管道和稠密奖励信号，提升强化学习后训练中模型的多跳搜索与问答能力，在域外数据集上实现最高10%的泛化提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习后训练存在两个主要局限：一是稀疏的基于结果的奖励信号导致信用分配困难；二是缺乏包含不同难度问题的训练数据，使得模型无法执行深度搜索以收集证据进行问答。

Method: 提出S^3-R1框架：(1) 合成数据生成与筛选管道，从文档中程序化推导多跳问题，并利用检索验证步骤专门筛选中等难度问题；(2) 奖励结构同时评估中间搜索质量和最终答案正确性，缓解稀疏奖励带来的信用分配问题。

Result: S^3-R1在域外数据集上的鲁棒泛化性能提升最高达10%，优于现有基线模型。

Conclusion: 通过数据增强和稠密奖励信号的结合，能有效训练模型学习更有效的搜索与综合策略，克服稀疏奖励的局限。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training has enabled newer capabilities in models, such as agentic tool-use for search. However, these models struggle primarily due to limitations with sparse outcome-based rewards and a lack of training data that encapsulates questions of differing hardness, which results in models not performing deeper searches with tools to collect evidence for question-answering. To address these limitations, we introduce S^3-R1 (Synthetic data and stabilized Search R1), a framework that couples a data-centric approach with denser learning signals. We first develop a synthetic generation and curation pipeline that programmatically derives diverse, multi-hop questions from existing documents. This pipeline incorporates a retrieval-based verification step to specifically isolate questions of intermediate difficulty. We then pair this expanded training set with a reward structure that evaluates both intermediate search quality and the correctness of the final answer. This setup directly mitigates the credit assignment problems inherent to sparse rewards. Our evaluations show that S^3-R1 outperforms existing baselines by learning more effective search and synthesis strategies, yielding up to a 10% improvement in robust generalization on out-of-domain datasets.

</details>


### [263] [Activation Compression in LLMs: Theoretical Analysis and Efficient Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01255)
*Wen-Da Wei,Han-Bin Fang,Yang-Di Liu,Jiang-Xin Shi,James Kwok,Yu-Feng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了激活压缩的理论框架，证明了无偏激活压缩对线性算子安全但对非线性算子有问题，并基于此设计了激活-梯度协同压缩方法，在保持收敛率的同时显著减少训练内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型训练内存开销巨大，现有方法主要集中在梯度和优化器状态的压缩，而对激活压缩缺乏理论保障和有效方法。

Method: 1) 建立理论框架证明无偏激活压缩对线性算子安全，对非线性算子有问题；2) 推导梯度方差界并在L-光滑假设下证明收敛性不变；3) 提出激活-梯度协同压缩方法，复用低秩激活因子压缩线性层梯度，不引入额外计算或梯度误差。

Result: 在Qwen和LLaMA模型的预训练和多种微调基准上验证了理论，在准确率和压缩效率上均表现出竞争力。

Conclusion: 激活压缩在理论上对线性算子安全且不改变收敛率，所提方法能有效降低训练内存开销。

Abstract: Training large language models (LLMs) is highly memory-intensive, as training must store not only weights and optimizer states but also intermediate activations for backpropagation. While existing memory-efficient methods largely focus on gradients and optimizer states, activation compression is less well established due to the lack of LLM-tailored theory and guarantees. In this work, we develop a theoretical framework showing that activation compression is safe for linear operators when activation compression is unbiased, but problematic for nonlinear ones. We further derive gradient variance bound and establish convergence guarantees for applying activation compression to all linear operators under the standard $L$-smoothness assumption, showing that it does not change the convergence rate. Guided by the theory, we propose an activation-gradient co-compression method that reuses low-rank activation factors to compress linear-layer gradients without extra computation or additional gradient error. We conduct extensive experiments on Qwen and LLaMA models using a pretraining benchmark and multiple fine-tuning benchmarks to validate our theory and demonstrate competitive performance of our method in both accuracy and compression efficiency. We provide our code in the supplementary material for reproducibility.

</details>


### [264] [Continuous Temporal Representations of Event-Based Signals via Interference-Based Wave Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01270)
*Magnus Bengtsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于干涉波表示的连续时间建模框架，将事件驱动生物信号（如表面肌电信号）映射为复值潜波场，通过相位调制和能量投影实现时空结构建模，在生物力学控制系统（如假肢和外骨骼）中展现出优于纯实值表示的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离散或纯实值表示难以有效建模表面肌电信号（sEMG）等事件驱动生物过程中呈现的非同步、高度结构化的时空激活模式。

Method: 提出基于干涉波表示的连续时间建模框架：将事件输入信号映射为复值潜波场，通过相位调制和潜分量间的交互编码时间结构，再投影到能量域以诱导结构化的激活模式，无需显式循环或因果状态传播。

Result: 该干涉波模型相较于纯实值表示提供了更优的表示质量，同时保持了适合实际部署的计算效率。

Conclusion: 所提方法特别适用于事件驱动的生物信号，能够支持高效梯度优化和鲁棒特征提取，适合假肢和外骨骼等生物力学系统的下游控制任务。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal signals arising from event-driven biological processes, such as surface electromyography (sEMG), exhibit asynchronous and highly structured activation patterns that are challenging to model using conventional discrete or purely real-valued representations.
  In this work, we propose a continuous temporal modeling framework based on interference-based wave representations. The approach maps event-like input signals into a complex-valued latent wave field, where temporal structure is encoded through phase modulation and interactions between latent components.
  By projecting the resulting wave field onto an energy domain, the model induces structured activation patterns that capture both temporal localization and relational dependencies within finite observation windows, without relying on explicit recurrence or causal state propagation.
  The proposed formulation is particularly suited for event-driven biosignals, where continuous representations enable efficient gradient-based optimization and robust feature extraction. In particular, the method is designed to support learning from sEMG data for downstream control tasks in biomechanical systems, such as prosthetic devices and exoskeletons.
  Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed interference-based wave model provides improved representation quality compared to purely real-valued representations, while maintaining computational efficiency suitable for practical deployment.

</details>


### [265] [Congestion-Aware Dynamic Axonal Delay for Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01291)
*Dewei Bai,Hongxiang Peng,Yunyun Zeng,Ziyu Zhang,Hong Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种拥塞感知的动态轴突延迟机制（Congestion-Aware Dynamic Axonal Delay），将延迟分解为通道级静态基延迟和全局活动条件偏移，通过可微线性插值学习延迟参数并在推理时离散化。在SHD、SSC和GSC-35语音基准上分别达到93.75%、80.49%和95.53%的准确率，同时相比现有基于延迟的方法减少了约50%的参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SNN延迟学习方法主要为单个突触分配静态延迟，导致延迟参数数量庞大且难以适应输入依赖的活动动态。本文旨在解决这些问题，提出一种更高效、适应性更强的动态延迟机制。

Method: 将延迟分解为通道级静态基延迟（用于时间结构化）和全局活动条件偏移（根据尖峰强度动态调节状态更新率）；采用可微线性插值学习延迟参数，推理时离散化，仅在最小额外成本下保留动态延迟的优势。

Result: 在SHD上达到93.75%准确率，SSC上80.49%，GSC-35上95.53%；与同架构的最先进延迟方法相比，参数数量减少约50%。

Conclusion: 引入拥塞感知延迟可有效提高SNN在时间任务上的准确性，同时显著降低参数开销，验证了动态延迟机制在事件驱动任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) are widely regarded as an energy-efficient paradigm for modeling and processing temporal and event-driven information. Incorporating delays in SNNs has been proven to be an effective mechanism for improving spike alignment in event-driven tasks. However, existing delay learning approaches predominantly assign static delays to individual synapses, resulting in a large number of delay parameters and limited adaptability to input-dependent activity dynamics. To this end, we propose a Congestion-Aware Dynamic Axonal Delay mechanism, decomposing the delay into a channel-wise static base delay for temporal structuring and a global, activity-conditioned shift that dynamically regulates the state update rate under varying spike intensities. The delay parameters are learned using differentiable linear interpolation and discretized at inference time, preserving the benefits of our dynamic delay while incurring only minimal additional cost. Experiments on speech benchmarks, including the Spiking Heidelberg Dataset, Spiking Speech Commands, and Google Speech Commands, demonstrate that introducing congestion-aware delays into synaptic signal transmission effectively improves accuracy on temporal tasks, notably achieving 93.75\% accuracy on SHD, 80.49\% accuracy on SSC, and 95.53\% on GSC-35, while reducing the parameter count by approximately 50\% compared to state-of-the-art delay-based methods with the same architecture.

</details>


### [266] [Autonomous Drift Learning in Data Streams: A Unified Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01295)
*Xiaoyu Yang,En Yu,Jie Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于系统运行状态的三维漂移分类法，将非平稳环境下的学习系统挑战从传统的时间概念漂移扩展至时间流漂移、数据流漂移和模型流漂移三个维度，系统综述了193篇文献，为构建自演化自主学习系统提供了路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统研究主要关注时间维度上的概念漂移，但随着自主学习和复杂系统的发展，仅适应时间非平稳性已不足以应对挑战。作者旨在超越传统视角，系统化梳理漂移适应、持续学习和时间泛化等碎片化范式，为构建自演化智能系统奠定基础。

Method: 提出三维分类框架：1) 时间流漂移（区分随机任意模式与结构节律动力学）；2) 数据流漂移（区分特征表示漂移与语义漂移）；3) 模型流漂移（通过序列可塑性、去中心化异质性和策略不稳定性刻画学习系统的内部内生发散）。基于该框架系统综述193篇代表性研究。

Result: 识别了关键开放挑战，并展示了该三维分类法如何桥接漂移适应、持续学习和时间泛化等原本碎片化的研究范式。

Conclusion: 该综述为构建能够在持续变化中自主学习、具备自演化能力的智能系统指明了方向。

Abstract: In the pursuit of autonomous learning systems, the foundational assumption of stationarity, the premise that data distributions and model behaviors remain constant, is fundamentally untenable. Historically, the research community has addressed non-stationary environments almost exclusively under the scope of concept drift, focusing primarily on temporal shifts in streams. However, as learning systems become increasingly autonomous and complex, merely adapting to temporal non-stationarity is no longer sufficient. Evolving beyond this traditional perspective, we propose a novel, three-dimensional taxonomy that systematizes the field based on the operational state of the system. First, time stream drift distinguishes between stochastic arbitrary patterns and structural rhythmic dynamics. Second, data stream drift disentangles shifts in feature representations, identified as representation drift, from changes in underlying semantics, recognized as semantic drift. Third, model stream drift characterizes the internal endogenous divergence of learning systems through the lenses of sequential plasticity, decentralized heterogeneity, and policy instability. Based on this framework, we systematically review 193 representative studies and identify key open challenges. By bridging the fragmented paradigms of drift adaptation, continual learning, and temporal generalization, this survey outlines a roadmap for building self-evolving intelligent systems capable of learning autonomously through continuous change.

</details>


### [267] [GA-VisAgent: A Multi-Agent application for code generation and visualization in interactive learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01299)
*Wang Jian,Zhou Jianbo,Xiong Yuhao,Liu Zhenxia,Luo Wen,Yuan LinWang,Yu ZhaoYuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GA-VisAgent，一个基于几何代数大语言模型的多智能体交互学习应用，通过任务规划与ReAct推理策略将复杂操作分解为五个标准化子任务，在40个共形几何代数任务上达到90%代码生成成功率，比GPT-4o提升70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何代数（GA）的抽象数学结构与复杂运算规则导致学习门槛高，现有GA软件包依赖手动脚本且学习曲线陡峭，而基于大语言模型的方法在生成特定GA脚本时逻辑错误频发、准确率低。

Method: 提出GA-VisAgent多智能体交互学习应用，基于GAGPT（几何代数大语言模型），集成任务规划机制与ReAct推理策略，将复杂操作分解为几何积、旋转、反射等五个标准化子任务，支持自然语言与数学公式输入，自动生成可执行代码并附带交互式可视化。

Result: 在40个典型共形几何代数任务上，GA-VisAgent达到90%代码生成成功率，较GPT-4o提升70%。

Conclusion: 该应用为GA教学及相关数学概念的可视化工具开发提供了可扩展的新范式，项目在线服务将于http://gagis.cn/gacrac开放。

Abstract: Geometric Algebra (GA) presents challenges to learners due to its highly abstract mathematical structure and complex operational rules, as translating algebraic manipulations into concrete geometric interpretations is a non-intuitive process when developing related code. Currently, some existing GA software packages rely on manually written scripts for code generation and visualization, but their high learning curve hinders widespread adoption. Meanwhile, methods based on Large Language Models (LLMs) often produce logical errors when generating specific GA scripts, such as GAALOPScript, resulting in generally low accuracy. To address these issues, this study proposes GA-VisAgent -- a multi-agent interactive learning application for GA code generation and visualization -- building upon a Geometric algebra large language model (GAGPT). Integrating task planning mechanisms with ReAct reasoning strategies, GA-VisAgent can decompose complex operations into five standardized subtasks, including core operations like geometric products, rotations, and reflections. It supports natural language and mathematical formulas as input to automatically generate executable code, accompanied by interactive visualizations to aid user comprehension. Experimental results show that GA-VisAgent achieved a 90% code generation success rate across 40 typical Conformal GA tasks, representing a 70% improvement over GPT-4o. This application introduces an extensible new paradigm for teaching GA and developing visualization tools for related mathematical concepts. The online service for this project will be available at http://gagis.cn/gacrac.

</details>


### [268] [GraphSculptor: Sculpting Pre-training Coreset for Graph Self-supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01310)
*Chuang Liu,Zelin Yao,Xueqi Ma,Luzhi Wang,Mukun Chen,Pinghua Xu,Wenbin Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GraphSculptor通过内在结构和上下文语义双重视角构建预训练核心集，在仅使用10%数据时达到99.6%的完整性能，同时减少近90%预训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图自监督学习依赖大规模无标注数据集导致计算成本高昂，而现有证据表明这些数据存在大量冗余（均匀采样50%数据仍保留96%性能），亟需一种无需额外训练信号的数据精简方法。

Method: GraphSculptor从两个互补视角构建核心集：(1) 利用内在图统计量量化结构多样性，生成结构特征向量；(2) 通过预训练语言模型编码图到文本的描述，捕获语义多样性。将两者融合到统一度量空间，执行聚类感知选择以保留联合结构-语义多样性。

Result: 10%核心集达到99.6%的完整数据性能，预训练时间减少近90%。理论推导了核心集与全数据预训练之间损失差距的边界。

Conclusion: GraphSculptor提供了一种无标签、高效的数据精简方案，为可扩展的图预训练提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Graph self-supervised learning typically relies on large-scale unlabeled datasets, heavily inflating computational costs. However, empirical evidence suggests that these datasets contain substantial redundancy-our analysis reveals that uniformly subsampling 50% of graphs retains over 96% of downstream performance. To exploit this redundancy, we introduce GraphSculptor for pre-training coreset construction. Unlike methods dependent on additional training-time signals or limited solely to topological statistics, GraphSculptor provides a label-free solution that constructs coresets via two complementary perspectives: intrinsic structure and contextual semantics. Concretely, structural diversity is quantified using intrinsic graph statistics, yielding a structural feature vector for each graph, while semantic diversity is captured by utilizing a pre-trained language model to encode descriptions generated via graph-to-text. GraphSculptor integrates these signals into a unified metric space and performs cluster-aware selection to preserve joint structural-semantic diversity. We further derive a theoretical bound on the loss gap between coreset and full-data pre-training, offering theoretical motivation for our selection formulation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GraphSculptor effectively sculpts the dataset: a 10% coreset achieves 99.6% of full-data performance while reducing pre-training time by nearly 90%, offering a scalable solution for data-efficient graph pre-training.

</details>


### [269] [Robust Parameter Learning for Uncertain MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01339)
*Yannik Schnitzer,Alessandro Abate,David Parker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出使用参数化MDP学习未知MDP，通过将统计不确定性投影到参数空间来保留转移依赖关系，并构建多面体外近似实现策略综合，相比传统区间方法取得了更紧的不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于区间MDP的不确定性量化方法独立处理每个转移概率，忽略了因共享潜在量导致的依赖关系，导致不确定性估计过于保守。

Method: 采用参数化MDP（pMDP）建模，将转移概率表示为参数的函数；将经验转移频率的统计不确定性投影到pMDP的参数空间，得到PAC置信集；提出一组层次化的多面体外近似方法以解决计算难题。

Result: 所提方法相比经典的基于区间MDP的不确定性学习技术，获得了显著更紧的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 利用pMDP的结构化参数表示可以捕获转移概率间的代数依赖关系，从而在保持PAC保证的同时降低不确定性估计的保守性，且通过多面体近似可有效进行策略综合。

Abstract: Learning-based approaches to verifying unknown Markov decision processes (MDPs) often employ uncertain MDPs. These models use, for example, confidence intervals to capture transition uncertainty and allow synthesis of policies that are robust to this uncertainty. However, this approach typically quantifies uncertainty independently for individual transition probabilities, ignoring dependencies due to shared latent quantities. We propose to learn such models using parametric MDPs (pMDPs), where transition probabilities are expressions over a set of parameters. We project statistical uncertainty from empirical transition frequencies onto the pMDP's parameter space, yielding a probably approximately correct (PAC) uncertainty model for the underlying MDP that respects the algebraic dependencies between transitions. The resulting models are algorithmically challenging to solve, so we propose a hierarchy of sound polytopic outer approximations of the induced confidence set. We implement and evaluate our approach, demonstrating substantially tighter uncertainty estimates than classical interval-based uncertain MDP learning techniques.

</details>


### [270] [Model-Based Proactive Cost Generation for Learning Safe Policies Offline with Limited Violation Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01356)
*Ruiqi Xue,Lei Yuan,Kainuo Cheng,Jing-Wen Yang,Yang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PROCO是一种基于模型的离线安全强化学习框架，利用大语言模型将自然语言知识整合到策略中，解决数据集中缺乏不安全样本时约束违反问题，通过构建保守成本函数和生成反事实不安全样本，有效识别可行状态并提升安全性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险场景下，离线数据中极少或没有不安全样本，现有方法将所有数据视为均匀安全，忽略了"安全但不可行"的状态（当前满足约束但几步内必然违反），导致部署失败。

Method: PROCO首先从离线数据学习动力学模型，然后利用LLM将不安全状态的自然语言知识转化为保守成本函数，从而实现无需观察违规即可估计风险；接着通过基于模型的rollout合成多样化的反事实不安全样本，支持可靠的可行性识别和引导策略学习。

Result: 在Safety-Gymnasium任务中（仅包含安全或极少风险训练数据），PROCO能与多种离线安全RL算法无缝集成，相比原始方法和行为克隆基线，持续降低约束违反并提升安全性能。

Conclusion: PROCO通过知识-数据融合的方式，有效解决了极端数据稀疏场景下离线安全强化学习的核心挑战，证明了LLM引入先验知识在安全关键决策中的价值。

Abstract: Learning constraint-satisfying policies from offline data without risky online interaction is crucial for safety-critical decision making. Conventional methods typically learn cost value functions from abundant unsafe samples to define safety boundaries and penalize violations. However, in high-stakes scenarios, risky trial-and-error is infeasible, yielding datasets with few or no unsafe samples. Under this limitation, existing approaches often treat all data as uniformly safe, overlooking safe-but-infeasible states - states that currently satisfy constraints but inevitably violate them within a few steps - leading to deployment failures. Drawing inspiration from the concept of knowledge-data integration, we leverage large language models (LLMs) to incorporate natural language knowledge into the policy to address this challenge. Specifically, we propose PROCO, a model-based offline safe reinforcement learning (RL) framework tailored to datasets largely free of violations. PROCO first learns a dynamics model from offline data and constructs a conservative cost function by grounding natural-language knowledge of unsafe states in LLMs, enabling risk estimation even without observed violations. Using the cost function and learned model, PROCO performs model-based rollouts to synthesize diverse counterfactual unsafe samples, supporting reliable feasibility identification and feasibility-guided policy learning. Across a range of Safety-Gymnasium tasks with exclusively safe or minimally risky training data, PROCO integrates seamlessly with a variety of offline safe RL algorithms and consistently demonstrates reduced constraint violations and improved safety performance compared to both the original methods and other behavior cloning baselines.

</details>


### [271] [Decision-Focused Learning via Tangent-Space Projection of Prediction Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01361)
*Junhyeong Lee,Sangjin Jin,Yongjae Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PEAR方法，通过将预测误差投影到活动约束的切空间来等效计算遗憾梯度，避免了通过求解器求导或替代损失函数，在LP/QP任务上实现了最佳决策质量与最高计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策驱动学习（DFL）方法在计算遗憾梯度时，要么需要微分求解器（计算昂贵），要么依赖替代损失函数（偏离真实目标），缺乏既精确又高效的计算途径。

Method: 在局部稳定活动约束的正则性假设下，证明遗憾梯度等于MSE梯度投影到活动约束切空间并乘以局部曲率；进而提出PEAR，通过求解活动约束上的降维线性系统直接计算遗憾梯度，无需求解器迭代或额外优化求解。

Result: 在LP基准测试和真实QP任务上，PEAR在所有基线中实现了最佳决策质量，同时计算效率最高，且在约束偏移场景下仍保持优势。

Conclusion: 遗憾梯度可以几何解释为过滤掉决策无关分量后的MSE梯度，PEAR方法实现了更简单、更直接的DFL替代方案，兼顾精度与效率。

Abstract: Decision-Focused Learning (DFL) trains predictors to improve downstream decision quality, but computing regret gradients typically requires differentiating through solvers or relying on surrogate losses, which can be computationally expensive or deviate from the true objective. We show that, under standard regularity with locally stable active constraints, the regret gradient admits a closed-form geometric characterization, equivalent to the prediction error projected onto the tangent space of active constraints, scaled by local curvature. This reveals that regret gradients can be obtained by filtering decision-irrelevant components from the MSE gradient, providing a simpler and more direct alternative to existing approaches. Based on this, we propose PEAR (Projected Error As Regret-gradient), which computes regret gradients via a reduced linear system over active constraints, avoiding differentiation through solver iterations or additional optimization solves. Experiments on LP benchmarks and a real-world QP task show that PEAR achieves the best decision quality among all baselines while being the most computationally efficient, with gains that persist under constraint shifts.

</details>


### [272] [Toward a foundational thermal model for residential buildings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01364)
*Ting-Yu Dai,Kingsley Nweye,Dev Niyogi,Zoltan Nagy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理信息增强的Transformer架构，用于构建建筑热力学通用基础模型。该模型在CityLearn数据集上对247栋住宅建筑进行验证，实现了0.29-0.30°C的RMSE预测精度，并展示了零样本迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑能源领域缺乏一个能够跨建筑、气候和控制策略泛化的基础热学模型，现有模型需要针对每栋建筑进行单独标定，缺乏通用性。

Method: 提出物理信息增强的Transformer架构：采用仅解码器结构，嵌入导数增强和欧拉数值积分等物理知识，利用仿真模型提取静态建筑特征，使用旋转位置编码（RoPE）捕捉时间依赖关系。

Result: 在CityLearn数据集（247栋住宅、3个气候区）上，单步预测RMSE达0.30°C（德克萨斯）和0.29°C（佛蒙特），超越传统基线和微调的时间序列基础模型；仅用2栋建筑训练即可零样本迁移到未见建筑和气候区。

Conclusion: 物理信息增强的架构原则为构建通用建筑热学模型提供了有前景的基础，尽管当前局限在模拟住宅建筑数据。

Abstract: The building energy community lacks a foundational thermal model, i.e., a single pretrained model capable of generalizing across diverse buildings, climates, and control strategies without building-specific calibration. Achieving this vision requires architectural principles that capture universal thermal dynamics rather than memorizing building-specific patterns. We take a step toward this goal by presenting a physics-informed transformer architecture that embeds domain knowledge, e.g., derivative enrichment and Euler-based numerical integration, into a decoder-only framework. We incorporate static building features extracted from simulation models and employ Rotary Position Embedding attention to capture temporal dependencies. Evaluated on the CityLearn dataset spanning 247 residential buildings across three climate zones, our model achieves one-step prediction accuracy (RMSE of 0.30°C in Texas, 0.29°C in Vermont) while outperforming both traditional baselines and fine-tuned Time-Series Foundation Models. We also demonstrate zero-shot transferability: models trained on as few as two buildings generalize to unseen buildings and climate zones without fine-tuning. Despite the limitation of simulated residential buildings, our results establish physics-informed architectural principles as a promising foundation for universal building thermal models.

</details>


### [273] [Quantifying Multimodal Capabilities: Formal Generalization Guarantees in Pairwise Metric Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01424)
*Richeng Zhou,Xuelin Zhang,Liyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对多模态度量学习模型的泛化性能进行了细粒度理论分析，建立了不同模态子集对应函数类之间的层次关系，推导了新颖的泛化误差上界和下界，证明细粒度模态特征通过增强模态互补性降低了假设空间复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态学习在实际场景中常面临模态数据不完整或冗余的问题，但模态选择与算法性能之间的理论关系尚不清晰，存在理解上的关键空白。

Method: 采用细粒度理论分析，建立模态子集函数类的层次关系，通过多模态学习框架内的成对复杂度分析推导泛化误差界。

Result: 导出新的泛化误差上界和下界，揭示模态数量和粒度对模型性能的联合影响；证明细粒度模态特征通过增强模态互补性降低假设空间复杂度。

Conclusion: 为提升多模态学习系统的收敛速度和准确性提供了理论基础和实践启示。

Abstract: Multimodal learning leverages the integration of diverse data modalities to enhance performance in complex tasks. Yet, it frequently encounters incomplete or redundant modality data in real-world scenarios. This paper presents a fine-grained theoretical analysis of the generalization properties of multimodal metric learning models, addressing critical gaps in understanding the relationship between modality selection and algorithmic performance. We establish hierarchical relationships between function classes corresponding to different modality subsets and quantify the discrepancy between learned mappings and ground truth. Through rigorous analysis of pairwise complexity within the multimodal learning framework, we derive novel generalization error bounds that reveal the joint impact of modality quantity and granularity on model performance. Our theoretical findings on both upper and lower bounds demonstrate that incorporating fine-grained modality features reduces the complexity of the hypothesis space by enhancing modality complementarity. This work offers both theoretical foundations and practical implications for improving convergence rates and accuracy in multimodal learning systems.

</details>


### [274] [Towards Efficient and Expressive Offline RL via Flow-Anchored Noise-conditioned Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01663)
*Sungyoung Lee,Dohyeong Kim,Eshan Balachandar,Zelal Su Mustafaoglu,Keshav Pingali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FAN算法，通过单次流策略迭代和单高斯噪声样本结合行为正则化，在保持高性能的同时显著提升离线强化学习的训练和推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流策略和分布性评论家虽然能提升离线RL性能，但计算成本极高——流策略需要迭代采样生成单个动作，分布性评论家需要多个样本来估计价值。作者希望解决这一效率瓶颈。

Method: 引入流锚定噪声条件Q学习（FAN），采用行为正则化技术，仅需单次流策略迭代和单个高斯噪声样本用于分布性评论家，大幅简化计算流程。

Result: 在机器人操作和运动任务上达到最先进性能，同时显著减少训练和推理时间。理论分析证明了收敛性和性能界的优势。

Conclusion: 简化后的方法不仅提升了效率，还带来了更优的任务性能，表明计算效率与算法性能可以兼得。

Abstract: We propose Flow-Anchored Noise-conditioned Q-Learning (FAN), a highly efficient and high-performing offline reinforcement learning (RL) algorithm. Recent work has shown that expressive flow policies and distributional critics improve offline RL performance, but at a high computational cost. Specifically, flow policies require iterative sampling to produce a single action, and distributional critics require computation over multiple samples (e.g., quantiles) to estimate value. To address these inefficiencies while maintaining high performance, we introduce FAN. Our method employs a behavior regularization technique that utilizes only a single flow policy iteration and requires only a single Gaussian noise sample for distributional critics. Our theoretical analysis of convergence and performance bounds demonstrates that these simplifications not only improve efficiency but also lead to superior task performance. Experiments on robotic manipulation and locomotion tasks demonstrate that FAN achieves state-of-the-art performance while significantly reducing both training and inference runtimes. We release our code at https://github.com/brianlsy98/FAN.

</details>


### [275] [Barriers to Counterfactual Credit Attribution for Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01425)
*Aloni Cohen,Chenhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了生成式AI中的反事实信用归因（CCA）问题，发现在自回归模型中，逐词预测的CCA无法组合成整体模型的CCA，且通过“改造”方式实现CCA在最坏情况下需要指数级查询复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI模型的输出常依赖于先前工作，但现有模型缺乏对贡献来源的明确归因。作者希望正式化“模型应归因其输出的重要依赖”这一目标。

Method: 首先证明CCA不具备自回归组合性（与DP不同），其次定义并分析“改造”（retrofitting）方法，证明在黑盒访问下其查询复杂度与输出长度呈指数关系。

Result: 1）CCA在自回归设置下不能通过逐词预测的CCA保证整体CCA；2）改造方法在最坏情况下需要指数级查询才能实现CCA。

Conclusion: 构建CCA自回归生成模型面临根本性挑战，简单的方法（逐词CCA或黑盒改造）均不可行，需要新的设计思路。

Abstract: Generative AI disrupts the practice of giving credit to work that came before. Ideally, a generative model would give credit to any work on which its output depends in a significant way. \emph{Counterfactual credit attribution} (CCA) is a technical condition formalizing this goal--a relaxation of differential privacy--recently introduced by Livni, Moran, Nissim, and Pabbaraju [2024] who studied it in the PAC learning setting.
  We initiate the study of CCA generative models. Specifically, we consider autoregressive models giving credit to a deployment-time dataset (e.g., a RAG database). We uncover barriers to two natural approaches to CCA autoregressive models. First, we show that imposing CCA on the underlying next-token predictor does not guarantee that the model is CCA: CCA does not compose autoregressively (unlike DP). Second, we consider a different approach to building CCA models which we call \emph{retrofitting}. Retrofitting takes a model that does not attribute credit, and adds credit onto it. We prove a lower bound for CCA retrofitting under a weak optimality requirement. Given black-box access to the starting model, retrofitting requires query complexity exponential in the length of the model's outputs.

</details>


### [276] [Evaluating LLMs on Large-Scale Graph Property Estimation via Random Walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01484)
*Sunil Kumar Maurya,Xin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EstGraph基准，用于评估LLM在超大图（百万级节点）且无法完全访问时的推理能力，通过随机游走采样在有限上下文长度内传递图信息，并设计了四种估计图属性的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图基准仅测试小图上的算法推理，而真实场景中图规模巨大且不可完全访问，现有LLM上下文长度无法处理，因此需要专门评估LLM在大规模不可完全访问图上的推理能力。

Method: 构建EstGraph基准数据集，设计四种图属性估计任务；采用基于随机游走采样的提示构建方法，在上下文长度限制内将大图（百万节点级）的有效信息传递给LLM。

Result: 在多种图数据集上评估了LLM在这四项任务上的推理能力，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的采样方法和基准任务能够有效评估LLM在超大图上的推理极限，为未来LLM在图数据上的应用提供了评估框架。

Abstract: With the rapidly improving reasoning abilities of Large Language Models (LLMs), there is also a rising demand to use them in a wide variety of domains. This brings about the need to carefully evaluate the limits of the capabilities of these models with various tests and benchmarks. Graph structures are ubiquitous in real-world data, and are often used to represent and analyze relationship patterns within data. Many benchmarks have already been proposed in the graph literature to test the reasoning ability of LLMs to follow and execute graph algorithms. However, due to the limited context length of LLMs, these benchmarks consist of very small graphs. In real-world data, the size of graphs can be significantly larger, and in many cases, not fully accessible. In this paper, we examine a class of problems that arises with very large graphs having limited accessibility. We propose a large graph benchmark dataset, EstGraph, and introduce four distinct tasks designed to estimate large graph properties. We evaluate the reasoning abilities of LLMs on these tasks using a wide variety of graph datasets. In addition, we provide task-specific prompt constructions based on random walk sampling of large graphs (up to millions of nodes) that effectively convey sufficient information to LLMs within the limits of context length.

</details>


### [277] [Protein-Conditioned Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning for Full-Length mRNA Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01513)
*Zixi Shao,Tao Wang,Yibei Xiao,Tianyi Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProMORNA是一个多目标生成框架，能够直接从目标蛋白质序列从头设计完整的mRNA转录本。该框架首先在超过600万天然蛋白质-mRNA对上训练BART式编码器-解码器模型，然后引入多目标组相对策略优化（MO-GRPO）同时优化稳定性、翻译效率和免疫安全性等多个生物学目标。在萤火虫荧光素酶目标上的评估显示，ProMORNA在预测半衰期和翻译效率方面改进了Pareto前沿，并取得了比现有基线更高的预测功能分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计治疗性mRNA需要创建在稳定性、翻译效率和免疫安全性之间精心平衡的全长转录本，现有方法难以同时优化这些相互冲突的目标。

Method: 首先在超过600万天然蛋白质-mRNA对上训练BART风格的编码器-解码器模型，然后引入多目标组相对策略优化（MO-GRPO）以统一的方式同时优化多个生物学目标。

Result: 在萤火虫荧光素酶目标上（排除在训练数据和提示池之外），ProMORNA改进了预测半衰期和翻译效率的in silico Pareto前沿，并在同一评估流程下取得了比最先进基线更高的预测功能分数。

Conclusion: 计算结果表明，使用多目标强化学习对未见目标进行全长mRNA设计是可行的。

Abstract: Designing therapeutic messenger RNA (mRNA) requires creating full-length transcripts that carefully balance stability, translation efficiency, and immune safety. To address this challenge, we propose ProMORNA, a multi-objective generation framework that produces complete mRNA transcripts \textit{de novo} directly from a target protein sequence. Our approach begins by training a BART-style encoder-decoder model on over 6 million natural protein-mRNA pairs. We then introduce Multi-Objective Group Relative Policy Optimization (MO-GRPO) to simultaneously optimize for various biological objectives in a unified way. As a case study, we evaluated ProMORNA on the widely used firefly luciferase target, excluding it from both our supervised training data and the prompt pool. The results indicate that ProMORNA improves the \textit{in silico} Pareto frontier for predicted half-life and translation efficiency relative to standard supervised baselines. Additionally, it achieves higher predicted functional scores than a state-of-the-art baseline under the same evaluation pipeline. These computational findings demonstrate the feasibility of using multi-objective reinforcement learning for full-length mRNA design on unseen targets.

</details>


### [278] [ECG-biometrics-bench: A Unified Framework for Reproducible Benchmarking of ECG Biometrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01548)
*Milad Parvan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ECG-biometrics-bench基准框架，系统揭示了ECG生物特征识别中因数据泄露（如随机划分）导致的性能虚高问题，并通过跨数据集、跨会话和长期时间隔离等现实评估协议，证明了时间漂移和未见身份会导致性能严重下降，且该问题具有模型无关性。部分缓解方案采用基于动态多会话模板融合的重注册-轻验证策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ECG生物特征识别研究因采用同会话内随机划分等评估协议导致数据泄露，报告了过于乐观的结果，缺乏标准化、可复现的基准测试框架来真实评估方法在现实场景下的性能。

Method: 构建了模块化、可复现的ECG-biometrics-bench基准框架，统一7个公开ECG数据集的预处理、分割和评估流程；支持闭集和开集评估，以及跨会话、长期时间分离等现实协议；通过多数据集、多架构（DeepECG、ResNet1D、CNN-LSTM）的综合分析暴露随机划分谬误，并提出基于动态多会话模板融合的重注册-轻验证策略进行缓解。

Result: 证明了同会话内评估协议会人为膨胀性能，掩盖时间漂移和未见身份导致的严重退化；该失败现象不限于特定模型，而是当前监督特征学习范式的固有问题；基于动态多会话模板融合的策略可部分缓解时间老化导致的性能退化。

Conclusion: 建立了更现实的ECG生物特征识别基线，指出了实现可靠现实部署所必须解决的关键挑战。

Abstract: Electrocardiogram (ECG) biometrics have emerged as a promising modality for continuous, liveness-aware authentication in wearable systems. However, many prior studies report overly optimistic results due to data leakage (e.g., random splits within the same session). To address this issue, we introduce ECG-biometrics-bench, a modular, reproducible benchmarking framework that standardizes preprocessing, segmentation, and evaluation across seven widely used public ECG datasets spanning clinical, ambulatory, and large-scale cohort settings. The framework supports both closed-set and open-set (i.e., subject-disjoint generalization in this work) evaluation, as well as progressively realistic protocols including cross-session and long-term temporal separation. To facilitate reproducible research in the community, the ECG-biometrics-bench repository will be made publicly accessible on GitHub upon the acceptance of this manuscript. Through a comprehensive multi-dataset analysis, we expose the Random Split Fallacy, demonstrating that intra-session evaluation protocols artificially inflate performance while masking severe degradation caused by temporal drift and unseen identities. Furthermore, by evaluating multiple architectures, including DeepECG, ResNet1D, and CNN-LSTM, we show that these failures are not model-specific but are likely inherent to current supervised feature-learning paradigms. Finally, we demonstrate that performance degradation due to temporal aging can be partially mitigated through a heavy enrollment, lightweight authentication strategy based on dynamic multi-session template fusion. These findings establish a more realistic baseline for ECG biometrics and highlight critical challenges that must be addressed for reliable real-world deployment.

</details>


### [279] [Enhancing RL Generalizability in Robotics through SHAP Analysis of Algorithms and Hyperparameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02867)
*Lingxiao Kong,Cong Yang,Oya Deniz Beyan,Zeyd Boukhers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于SHAP的可解释框架，用于量化强化学习算法和超参数配置对泛化性能的贡献，并通过SHAP引导的配置选择来提升RL的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL模型性能对算法和超参数配置高度敏感，且跨环境的泛化差距阻碍了实际部署。虽然已有研究探讨了RL泛化问题，但尚未定量分解特定配置对泛化差距的相对贡献，也无法系统性地利用这些信息进行配置选择。

Method: 提出一个可解释框架，使用SHAP（Shapley Additive Explanations）量化配置对RL性能的影响。建立了Shapley值与泛化能力之间的理论基础，实证分析配置影响模式，并引入SHAP引导的配置选择策略来增强泛化性。

Result: 不同算法和超参数展现出不同的影响模式，且配置的影响在不同任务和环境间具有一致性。基于这些洞察进行配置选择，显著提升了RL的泛化能力。

Conclusion: SHAP能够有效量化RL配置对泛化差距的贡献，基于SHAP的配置选择可提升泛化性能，为实践者提供了可操作的指导。

Abstract: Despite significant advances in Reinforcement Learning (RL), model performance remains highly sensitive to algorithm and hyperparameter configurations, while generalization gaps across environments complicate real-world deployment. Although prior work has studied RL generalization, the relative contribution of specific configurations to the generalization gap has not been quantitatively decomposed and systematically leveraged for configuration selection. To address this limitation, we propose an explainable framework that evaluates RL performance across robotic environments using SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) to quantify configuration impacts. We establish a theoretical foundation connecting Shapley values to generalizability, empirically analyze configuration impact patterns, and introduce SHAP-guided configuration selection to enhance generalization. Our results reveal distinct patterns across algorithms and hyperparameters, with consistent configuration impacts across diverse tasks and environments. By applying these insights to configuration selection, we achieve improved RL generalizability and provide actionable guidance for practitioners.

</details>


### [280] [Concepts Whisper While Syntax Shouts: Spectral Anti-Concentration and the Dual Geometry of Transformer Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01609)
*Pratyush Acharya,Nuraj Rimal,Habish Dhakal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过因果内积框架（Park et al., 2024）检验跨语言概念迁移，发现白化因果对齐与谱正则化无显著差异（p=0.95），但揭示了残差流中均值差向量的反集中现象（p<10^{-33}），并发现双重几何结构：激活空间概念方向在谱尾部反集中，而静态解嵌入行对比在高方差方向集中。因果干预和POS标注探测进一步表明，Transformer可能将语义内容旋转到谱安静区域进行编码，以减少语法干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验Park et al. (2024)提出的由解嵌入协方差Σ定义的因果内积能否实现跨语言概念迁移，并探究其失败背后的深层几何结构。

Method: 在17个模型和4种语言对上使用匹配谱随机化检验，结合SAE特征、线性探针、分裂注入因果干预和POS标注探测等方法，分析残差流均值差向量和激活空间方向的谱分布特性。

Result: 白化因果对齐与谱正则化不可区分（p=0.95）；发现残差流均值差向量的反集中现象（p<10^{-33}）；发现双重几何结构：激活空间概念方向在谱尾部反集中，静态解嵌入行对比在高方差方向集中；因果干预效应量Cohen's d高达1.80；8个模型中6个的语法信息优先编码在高方差子空间（p<0.013），Qwen 2.5家族呈现显著反转。

Conclusion: Transformer可能通过将语义内容旋转到谱安静区域（低方差方向）进行上下文处理，从而在减少语法干扰的前提下实现概念操作。

Abstract: We test whether the causal inner product of \citet{park2024linear} -- defined by the unembedding covariance $Σ$ -- enables cross-lingual concept transport. Across 17 models and 4 language pairs, a matched-spectrum randomization test finds that Whitened Causal Alignment is indistinguishable from spectral regularization alone ($p = 0.95$). However, this failure reveals a broader phenomenon: anti-concentration is observed in residual-stream difference-of-means vectors across five architecture families ($p < 10^{-33}$) and supported by SAE features (e.g., $p = 4.5 \times 10^{-19}$) and linear probes on Gemma and Llama. We discover a \emph{dual geometry}: activation-space concept directions anti-concentrate in the spectral tail, while static unembedding-row contrasts \emph{concentrate} in high-variance directions ($p < 10^{-4}$). Split-injection causal interventions support the functional basis on Gemma and Llama (Cohen's $d$ up to $1.80$), and POS-tag probing across 8 models shows syntax preferentially encodes in the high-variance subspace in 6 of 8 architectures ($p < 0.013$), with the Qwen~2.5 family showing a significant reversal consistent with architecture-specific spectral structure. These results suggest transformers may rotate semantic content into spectrally quiet regions during contextualized processing, encoding concepts where they can be manipulated with reduced grammatical disruption.

</details>


### [281] [Importance-Guided Basis Selection for Low-Rank Decomposition of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01627)
*Daniel Agyei Asante,Ernie Chang,Yang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BSI是一种基于二阶泰勒展开和海森对角线估计的低秩分解剪枝框架，通过评估移除每个基对任务损失的预期增量来选择保留的奇异向量基，在数学推理基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低秩分解方法如Basel在任务数据上重新学习奇异值系数并剪枝小幅度基，但这种启发式方法忽略了损失曲面的局部几何结构，导致所选基与任务性能之间存在偏差。

Method: BSI框架从任务损失关于奇异值的二阶泰勒展开出发，推导出基于导数的基重要性分数，融合一阶敏感性和二阶曲率；通过将Hutchinson随机化探测方法适配到对称参数扰动下的损失曲率估计，实现高效的海森对角线估计。

Result: 在数学推理基准上的实验表明，BSI在低秩压缩（尤其是深度压缩场景下）持续优于当前最优的低秩分解基线方法。

Conclusion: BSI提供了完整的理论分析，包括基剪枝下的损失增量界、海森对角线估计误差的传播、与海森谱相关的方差刻画、达到目标估计精度的高概率样本复杂度保证以及扰动强度指导，是一种 principled 的低秩压缩框架。

Abstract: Low-rank decomposition is a compelling approach for compressing large language models, but its effectiveness hinges on selecting which singular-vector bases to retain for a target task. Existing methods such as Basel adapt singular-value coefficients on downstream data and prune bases with small re-learned magnitudes, a heuristic that can be misaligned with task performance because it ignores the local geometry of the loss landscape. We present Basis Selection with Importance (BSI), a principled low-rank compression framework that ranks and prunes bases by directly estimating the expected loss increase incurred when each basis is removed. BSI derives a derivative-based importance score from a second-order Taylor expansion of the task loss with respect to singular values, combining first-order sensitivity and second-order curvature to quantify pruning impact. To make this criterion practical for LLMs, we develop an efficient Hessian-diagonal estimator by adapting the Hutchinson randomized-probing method to loss curvature with symmetric parameter perturbations. We provide a comprehensive theoretical analysis, including loss-increase bounds under basis pruning, explicit propagation of Hessian-diagonal estimation error into these bounds, variance characterization tied to the Hessian spectrum, high-probability sample-complexity guarantees for achieving a target estimation accuracy, and guidance on perturbation intensity. Extensive experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that BSI consistently outperforms state-of-the-art low-rank decomposition baselines, with especially strong improvements under deep compression.

</details>


### [282] [Prescriptive Scaling Laws for Data Constrained Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01640)
*Justin Lovelace,Christian Belardi,Srivatsa Kundurthy,Shriya Sudhakar,Kilian Q. Weinberger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个适用于数据受限场景的新缩放定律，通过在Chinchilla定律中引入一个简单的过拟合惩罚项，描述了重复训练数据带来的额外损失，并据此给出了计算最优分配的新建议：当数据重复达到一定程度后，应将更多计算资源分配给模型容量而非继续重复数据。该定律仅增加一个参数即可隔离过拟合效应，案例表明强权重衰减（λ=1.0）可将过拟合系数降低约70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练计算量快速增长，但高质量数据的可用性却日益受限。传统Chinchilla缩放定律假设每个训练token都是唯一的，无法有效指导数据受限场景下的预训练决策，亟需能反映重复数据影响的新缩放定律。

Method: 在Chinchilla缩放定律基础上，引入一个简单的加性过拟合惩罚项来建模重复训练数据带来的额外损失，形成一个单参数扩展的缩放定律。

Result: 新缩放定律能准确描述重复数据下模型的行为，给出了质上不同的计算最优分配建议：超过某个阈值后，继续重复数据反而适得其反，应将计算资源优先分配给增加模型容量。案例研究发现强权重衰减（λ=1.0）可将过拟合系数降低约70%。

Conclusion: 该定律为数据受限场景下的预训练提供了可操作的配置指导，同时其单参数形式隔离了过拟合效应，便于跨配置比较，为近期关于数据受限下最优权重衰减远大于标准实践的发现提供了缩放定律层面的解释。

Abstract: Training compute is increasingly outpacing the availability of high-quality data. This shifts the central challenge from optimal compute allocation to extracting maximum value from limited data. The widely adopted Chinchilla scaling law assumes every training token is unique. This limits its ability to guide pretraining decisions in data-constrained regimes. We model the excess loss under repetition with a simple additive overfitting penalty and find that it accurately describes model behavior. Our scaling law yields qualitatively new compute-optimal allocation advice. Beyond a point, further repetition is counterproductive and compute is better spent on model capacity. We show that following our law's recommended configuration improves performance in data-constrained regimes. Finally, because our one-parameter form isolates overfitting in a single coefficient, it enables direct comparison across training configurations. As a case study, we show that strong weight decay ($λ=1.0$) reduces this coefficient by approximately 70%, providing a scaling-law explanation for recent findings that optimal weight decay in data-constrained regimes is an order of magnitude larger than standard practice.

</details>


### [283] [AI Alignment via Incentives and Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01643)
*Rohit Agarwal,Joshua Lin,Mark Braverman,Elad Hazan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从法律与经济学的威慑与执法模型视角研究AI对齐问题，将不当行为视为对激励的策略性响应。通过构建双主体模型（解题者-审计者），提出对齐是固定点问题：惩罚既可能抑制解题者不当行为，也可能降低审计者监督意愿。在此框架下，奖励设计成为双层优化问题，需基于行为均衡而非语义含义评估奖励。实验表明，自适应奖励配置相比静态手工奖励能维持有效的监督压力并改善对齐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI对齐方法通常将模型的不当行为视为外部失败，忽视了其作为对激励策略性响应的本质。作者希望引入法律经济学中的威慑理论，将对齐问题重新概念化为一个包含解题者和审计者的战略互动均衡问题，从而更准确地建模和解决AI对齐挑战。

Method: 1) 建立双主体数学模型：解题者（solver）和审计者（auditor/verifier），其中解题者可能产生错误但获益，审计者需决定是否进行代价高昂的监控。2) 将对齐形式化为固定点问题，分析惩罚强度与监督激励之间的权衡。3) 将奖励设计定义为双层优化问题，主层选择奖励配置，子层求解行为均衡。4) 提出基于多臂老虎机（bandit）的外循环程序，利用有噪声的交互反馈搜索最优奖励配置。5) 在LLM编码流水线上进行实验验证。

Result: 实验结果表明，自适应奖励配置相比静态手工设计的奖励能够保持有效的监督压力，提高主层（principal）对齐结果，大幅减少幻觉性错误尝试。

Conclusion: AI对齐本质上是一个战略互动问题，而非单纯的技术故障诊断。通过将法律经济学的威慑模型引入对齐研究，可以更系统地设计激励结构，使得解题者和审计者在均衡状态下自然表现出对齐行为。奖励设计应被视为行为均衡的诱导问题，而不仅仅是语义正确的信号分配。

Abstract: We study AI alignment through the lens of law-and-economics models of deterrence and enforcement. In these models, misconduct is not treated as an external failure, but as a strategic response to incentives: an actor weighs the gain from violation against the probability of detection and the severity of punishment. We argue that the same logic arises naturally in agentic AI pipelines. A solver may benefit from producing a persuasive but incorrect answer, hiding uncertainty, or exploiting spurious shortcuts, while an auditor or verifier must decide whether costly monitoring is worthwhile. Alignment is therefore a fixed-point problem: stronger penalties may deter solver misbehavior, but they can also reduce the auditor's incentive to inspect, since auditing then mainly incurs cost on a population that appears increasingly aligned.
  This perspective also changes what should count as a post-training signal. Standard feedback often attaches reward to the final answer alone, but a solver-auditor pipeline exposes the full correction event: whether the solver erred, whether the auditor inspected, whether the error was caught, and whether oversight incentives remained active. We formalize this interaction in a two-agent model in which a principal chooses rewards over joint correction outcomes, inducing both solver behavior and auditor monitoring. Reward design is therefore a bilevel optimization problem: rewards are judged not by their immediate semantic meaning, but by the behavioral equilibrium they induce. We propose a bandit-based outer-loop procedure for searching over reward profiles using noisy interaction feedback. Experiments on an LLM coding pipeline show that adaptive reward profiles can maintain useful oversight pressure and improve principal-aligned outcomes relative to static hand-designed rewards, including a substantial reduction in hallucinated incorrect attempts.

</details>


### [284] [Geospatial foundation-model embeddings improve population estimation unevenly across space and scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01650)
*Wenbin Zhang,Eimear Cleary,Francisco Rowe,Somnath Chaudhuri,Maksym Bondarenko,Shengjie Lai,Andrew J. Tatem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Population Dynamics Foundation Model (PDFM)，利用地理空间基础模型嵌入替代传统手工构建的地理协变量，用于次国家人口估计。在巴西、尼日利亚和美国验证显示，PDFM解释未解释方差的中位改善20.1%，KL散度降低23.2%，但在空间尺度匹配不足时性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人口空间化依赖手工构建的地理协变量（如聚落范围、夜间灯光、环境条件），这些数据需要跨尺度和地理区域进行繁琐的组装与协调。在地理空间基础模型出现后，如何利用其从多源异构数据中学习到的可复用地点表征来改进人口估计，是本文的核心动机。

Method: 使用Population Dynamics Foundation Model (PDFM)嵌入作为预测变量，替代传统手工地理协变量，在巴西、尼日利亚和美国三个国家进行次国家人口估计基准测试。采用地理结构化验证方法，比较两种特征集在人口估计中的预测性能和KL散度。

Result: PDFM相比传统协变量，中位改善20.1%（IQR: 10.0-33.2%）的未解释方差减少，KL散度降低23.2%（9.2-26.2%）。PDFM在传统协变量难以刻画聚居环境的大面积欠发达地区优势最明显，但其性能与空间尺度耦合，跨空间聚合的迁移灵活性低于传统协变量。

Conclusion: 地理空间基础模型的地点表征能改善数据匮乏地区的人口估计，但在空间尺度不匹配时其优势会可预测地丧失，揭示了当前地理空间AI的一个根本局限。

Abstract: Reliable subnational population estimates are essential for applications, yet remain difficult where censuses are sparse, outdated or spatially coarse. Existing population-mapping workflows rely on hand-built geospatial covariates, such as settlement extent, night-time lights, and environmental conditions, which must be assembled and harmonised across scales and geographies. Geospatial foundation models offer an alternative by learning reusable representations of place from more multifaceted and heterogeneous data sources. Here, we benchmark Population Dynamics Foundation Model (PDFM) embeddings against the harmonised geospatial covariates for subnational population estimation in Brazil, Nigeria and the United States. Under geographically structured validation, PDFM increased predictive fit by a median of 20.1% (IQR: 10.0-33.2%, across country-model comparisons) reduction in unexplained variance, and reduced Kullback-Leibler divergence by 23.2% (9.2-26.2%). However, these gains were uneven. PDFM was most advantageous where the geospatial covariates weakly characterised settlement context, such as larger and less-developed subnational areas. Moreover, PDFM performance was scale-coupled with embeddings providing less flexible transfer across spatial aggregations than geospatial covariates. These findings showed that geospatial foundation-model representations of place can improve population estimation in data poor settings, but their benefits break down predictably under spatial scale mismatch, revealing a fundamental limitation of current geospatial AI.

</details>


### [285] [Complex Diffusion Maps with $ω$-Parameterized Kernels Revealing Inherent Harmonic Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01691)
*Tongzhen Dang,Weiyang Ding,Michael K. Ng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Complex Diffusion Maps (CDM)框架，通过构建ω参数化的复值核函数统一局部与非局部连接，基于算子谱理论建立理论基础，在复扩散空间中保留角度结构。实验表明CDM在合成与真实数据上提升了判别能力，对噪声鲁棒，在fMRI和EEG数据上表现优异且计算高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散映射方法多依赖实值核函数（如高斯核），难以同时捕获局部热扩散与非局部薛定谔扩散特性，且在处理易混淆样本和高噪声场景时判别力不足。

Method: 提出ω参数化的复值核函数族，统一局部高斯核与非局部薛定谔核；基于算子谱理论定义扩散算子、扩散距离与复调和映射；通过优化框架在复扩散空间中保留样本间的角度结构而非仅依赖实值幅度。

Result: CDM在合成与真实数据上优于线性及基于实值核的非线性方法；高噪声下保持鲁棒性，谱分离更清晰；在静息态fMRI数据中捕获更强的非局部时空动力学；无需任务调优即在EEG睡眠数据集上取得竞争性能，计算效率优于传统ML和DNN方法。

Conclusion: CDM通过复值核与角度结构保持机制有效提升了高维数据的判别能力和噪声鲁棒性，在神经影像等实际应用中展现了通用性和实用价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose Complex Diffusion Maps (CDM), a novel diffusion mapping framework that aims to reveal the dominant complex harmonics of high-dimensional data. Inspired by the local Gaussian kernel relevant to the heat equation and the nonlocal Schrödinger kernel relevant to the Schrödinger equation, we propose a unified family of $ω$-parameterized complex-valued kernels for the trade-off between local and nonlocal connections. We establish the theoretical foundation based on the operator spectrum theory, where the corresponding diffusion operator, diffusion distance, and complex harmonic maps are well-defined. An optimization-based interpretation of the maps is also developed, aiming to preserve angular structure in the complex diffusion space rather than relying solely on real-valued magnitude. We extensively evaluate CDM on both synthetic and real-world datasets. The complex-valued kernel amplifies differences among easily confusable samples, improving discriminative power over both linear and nonlinear methods based on real-valued kernels. CDM remains robust in high-noise settings, yielding a clearer eigengap that enhances spectral separation. For resting-state fMRI data, CDM captures more strongly correlated and nonlocal spatiotemporal dynamics. Without task-specific tuning, CDM achieves competitive performance on a public EEG sleep dataset, while maintaining high computational efficiency compared with both traditional machine learning and deep neural network approaches, highlighting its generality and practical value.

</details>


### [286] [Probe-Geometry Alignment: Erasing the Cross-Sequence Memorization Signature Below Chance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01699)
*Anamika Paul Rupa,Anietie Andy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型行为遗忘后内部仍残留可恢复的记忆痕迹，并证明这种残留可以通过每层秩一干预（探针几何对齐，PGA）实现手术式移除，在不损失能力的情况下将跨序列探针驱动至随机水平以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期攻击表明大语言模型的"行为遗忘"会在模型内部留下可通过对抗性探针恢复的痕迹，需要理解和移除这些残留痕迹以确保真正的隐私消除。

Method: 提出留一法跨序列探针测试记忆签名泛化性；引入探针几何对齐（PGA），在每层沿探针读出方向对齐激活以实现手术式擦除；并扩展对抗性PGA防御重新拟合攻击。

Result: 跨序列记忆签名在Pythia-70M、GPT-2 medium、Mistral-7B上分别有+0.32、+0.19、+0.30的差距，PGA将其驱动至随机以下（如Pythia-70M: 0.07，GPT-2 medium: 0.06）。记忆签名与回忆因果可分离，自然记忆与微调注入的秘密属于两个不同的表征区域。

Conclusion: 跨序列签名是预训练表征中真实的、因果可分离的、区域特定的属性，可通过每层单一秩一干预在无能力损失下将其移除至随机水平以下。

Abstract: Recent attacks show that behavioural unlearning of large language models leaves internal traces recoverable by adversarial probes. We characterise where this retention lives and show it can be surgically removed without measurable capability cost. Our central protocol is a leave-one-out cross-sequence probe that tests whether a memorisation signature generalises across held-out sequences. The signature is real and consistent across scale: memorisation-specific gaps of +0.32, +0.19, +0.30 on Pythia-70M, GPT-2 medium, and Mistral-7B; on Pythia-70M, the random-initialisation control collapses to -0.04 at the deepest layer where the pretrained signature peaks. The probe direction is causally separable from recall -- projecting it out collapses the signature locally (+0.44 -> -0.19) while behavioural recall barely changes -- and a probe trained on naturally memorised content does not classify fine-tuning-injected secrets, marking two representationally distinct regimes. We then introduce probe-geometry alignment (PGA), a surgical erasure that aligns activations along the probe's live readout direction at each depth. PGA drives the cross-sequence probe below random chance at all four scales tested (toy depth-4: 0.17; Pythia-70M: 0.07; Mistral-7B: 0.45; GPT-2 medium: 0.06 via MD-PGA k=2) and remains robust to six adversarial probe variants. Against a re-fitting attacker who trains a fresh probe on PGA-treated activations, we extend PGA adversarially, defeating the re-fit probe at every memorisation-relevant depth while preserving five zero-shot capability benchmarks within 2.8 percentage points per task (mean Δacc = +0.2pp). The cross-sequence signature is a real, causally separable, regime-specific property of pretrained representations -- removable below chance with a single rank-one intervention per depth at no measurable capability cost.

</details>


### [287] [CoAction: Cross-task Correlation-aware Pareto Set Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01712)
*Xinyue Chen,Yingxuan Liang,Yiqin Huang,Chikai Shang,Hai-Lin Liu,Fangqing Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoAction框架，利用任务感知Transformer同时处理多个多目标优化问题，通过任务特定嵌入和自注意力机制实现跨任务知识共享，在基准测试和实际应用中取得了竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Pareto集学习（PSL）方法每次只能解决单个多目标优化问题，在多任务场景下计算成本高且无法利用跨任务相关性。

Method: 提出CoAction框架，使用Transformer编码器作为骨干架构，为每个任务分配任务特定的嵌入向量，利用自注意力机制捕获复杂的任务依赖关系，实现多任务同时处理与知识共享。

Result: 在涵盖基准问题和实际应用的多任务测试套件上，在超体积（Hypervolume）、范围（Range）和稀疏性（Sparsity）指标上表现出有效性和竞争性性能。

Conclusion: CoAction框架能够有效利用跨任务相关性，同时处理多个多目标优化问题，降低计算成本并提升性能。

Abstract: Pareto set learning (PSL) is an emerging paradigm in multi-objective optimization that trains neural networks to map preference vectors to Pareto optimal solutions. However, existing PSL methods primarily focus on solving a single multi-objective optimization problem at a time. This limitation not only increases computational costs in multi-objective multitask optimization scenarios by requiring a separate model for each task, but also fails to exploit the inter-task correlations across tasks. To address this, we propose a Cross-tAsk correlation-aware Pareto Set Learning (CoAction) framework, which leverages task-aware transformer to handle multiple tasks simultaneously. Specifically, by assigning task-specific embedding vectors to individual tasks, the model effectively distinguishes between tasks while facilitating knowledge sharing among them. We utilize a Transformer encoder as the backbone architecture to leverage its self-attention mechanism for capturing complex task dependencies. The proposed approach is evaluated on comprehensive multitask test suites covering both benchmark problems and real-world applications, demonstrating effectiveness and competitive performance in Hypervolume, Range, and Sparsity.

</details>


### [288] [Stable GFlowNets with Probabilistic Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01729)
*Zengxiang Lei,Ananth Shreekumar,Jonathan Rosenthal,Ruoyu Song,Alvaro A. Cardenas,Daniel J. Fremont,Dongyan Xu,Satish Ukkusuri,Z. Berkay Celik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Generative Flow Networks (GFlowNets)训练不稳定问题，首先分析了现有目标函数对TV距离不敏感导致损失激增和模式坍塌的原因，然后建立了从损失到TV距离的逆向保证，最后提出了Stable GFlowNets算法，在理论上和实验上证明了其稳定性和分布保真度的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GFlowNets在实际训练中常出现严重的损失激增和模式坍塌问题，现有目标函数即使学习分布与目标分布之间的TV距离很小，仍可能导致无界训练损失，这一不匹配问题亟需解决。

Method: 首先评估GFlowNet目标函数对TV距离的敏感性，证明小TV距离无法保证有界损失；接着推导损失到TV距离的逆向界（loss-to-TV bounds），从有界轨迹平衡损失保证全局保真度；最后提出Stable GFlowNets算法，将理论结果应用于稳定训练。

Result: 证明了GFlowNet目标函数与TV距离之间存在不匹配问题；建立了损失到TV距离的逆向保证；Stable GFlowNets算法在实验中展现出更稳定的训练行为和更优的分布保真度。

Conclusion: Stable GFlowNets通过理论指导的算法设计，有效缓解了GFlowNets的训练不稳定性，提升了分布匹配的保真度。

Abstract: Generative Flow Networks (GFlowNets) learn to sample states proportional to an unnormalized reward. Despite their theoretical promise, practical training is often unstable, exhibiting severe loss spikes and mode collapse. To tackle this, we first assess the sensitivity of GFlowNet objectives, demonstrating that a small Total Variation (TV) distance between the learned and target distributions does not preclude unbounded training loss. Motivated by this mismatch, we establish converse guarantees by deriving loss-to-TV bounds that certify global fidelity from bounded trajectory balance losses. Lastly, we propose Stable GFlowNets, an algorithm that leverages our theoretical results to stabilize training, and empirically demonstrate improved training behavior and superior distributional fidelity.

</details>


### [289] [Robust Linear Dueling Bandits with Post-serving Context under Unknown Delays and Adversarial Corruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01752)
*Youngmin Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对具有后服务上下文、延迟反馈和对抗性腐败的动态环境，提出了一种线性对偶赌博机算法term，通过预测后服务上下文和自适应加权策略，实现了延迟机制无关的遗憾上界，并建立了几乎匹配的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性对偶赌博机研究未同时考虑后服务上下文、延迟反馈和对抗性腐败这三种动态环境挑战，而现实应用（如推荐系统、在线广告）中这些因素常同时存在，亟需一种统一的解决方案。

Method: 提出term算法，集成一个从服务前信息预测后服务上下文的学习近似器，并采用自适应加权策略裁剪特征向量，以同时减轻腐败和延迟观测的影响。

Result: 在标准正则性条件和参数化后服务映射下，实现了延迟机制无关的遗憾上界$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(d(\sqrt{T} + \mathcal{C} + \mathcal{D}))$，腐败与延迟呈相加而非相乘结构；同时建立了在无后服务上下文场景下、与上界仅差$\sqrt{d}$因子的下界。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在线性对偶赌博机中统一处理了后服务上下文、延迟反馈和对抗性腐败，揭示了腐败与延迟之间可分离的代价结构，为动态环境下的在线决策提供了理论保障。

Abstract: We study linear dueling bandits in volatile environments characterized by the simultaneous presence of post-serving contexts, delayed feedback, and adversarial corruption. Feedback is subject to unknown stochastic or adversarial delays and a cumulative corruption budget $\mathcal{C}$. To address these challenges, we propose \term, which integrates a learned approximator that predicts post-serving contexts from pre-serving information. It further employs an adaptive weighting strategy that clips feature vectors to mitigate the impact of corrupted and delayed observations simultaneously. Under standard regularity conditions and a parametric post-serving mapping, we rigorously establish that our algorithm is delay-regime-agnostic, achieving a regret upper bound of $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(d(\sqrt{T} + \mathcal{C} + \mathcal{D}))$, where $d$ is the total feature dimension and $\mathcal{D}$ encapsulates the delay complexity. Crucially, our analysis reveals an additive cost structure between corruption and delay, avoiding the multiplicative degradation typical of prior works. We further establish lower bounds that nearly match our upper bounds up to a $\sqrt{d}$ factor for adversarial delays in the absence of post-serving contexts.

</details>


### [290] [Mitigating Multimodal LLMs Hallucinations via Relevance Propagation at Inference Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01766)
*Itai Allouche,Joseph Keshet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LIME框架，通过推理时增强模态利用来减少多模态大模型的幻觉问题，无需训练或修改参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在推理时存在模态利用不平衡问题，文本token主导导致模型依赖语言先验而非感知证据，产生幻觉。

Method: 基于层相关性传播(LRP)量化token贡献，定义相关性目标函数，在推理时通过更新key-value表示增强对感知输入的依赖，无需训练或修改参数。

Result: 在视觉和音频多模态基准上一致减少幻觉、增强模态对齐，同时保持生成质量；提升了模态贡献并产生更局部化和语义对齐的相关性模式。

Conclusion: LIME通过推理时干预有效缓解多模态幻觉，是一种即插即用的无训练框架。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have revolutionized the landscape of AI, demonstrating impressive capabilities in tackling complex vision and audio-language tasks. However, a critical challenge remains: these models often suffer from hallucinations, generating outputs that diverge from the provided perceptual inputs. This tendency stems from an inherent imbalance in modality utilization during inference, where the dominance of textual tokens undermines the potential of perceptual inputs. As a result, the model frequently resorts to textual language priors at the expense of grounded evidence. To tackle this issue, we propose Learning Inference-time Modality Enhancement (LIME), a training-free framework designed to bolster multimodal grounding by explicitly enhancing modality usage during decoding. LIME leverages Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (LRP) to quantify token-level contributions and defines a relevance-based objective that promotes increased reliance on perceptual inputs. This objective is enforced through inference-time updates to the model's key-value representations, without modifying model parameters or requiring additional training data. We evaluate LIME across multiple multimodal benchmarks in both vision and audio domains, demonstrating consistent reductions in hallucinations and enhanced grounding while preserving generation quality. Further analysis shows that LIME increases modality contribution and produces more localized and semantically aligned relevance patterns.

</details>


### [291] [Adversarial Imitation Learning with General Function Approximation: Theoretical Analysis and Practical Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01778)
*Tian Xu,Zhilong Zhang,Zexuan Chen,Ruishuo Chen,Yihao Sun,Yang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了OPT-AIL框架，首次在通用函数逼近下实现了对抗模仿学习的理论保证，包含无模型和基于模型两种方法，在多个挑战性任务上优于现有深度AIL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗模仿学习的理论分析局限于表格和线性函数逼近等简化场景，且算法设计复杂难以实现，理论与实践的差距巨大。本文旨在弥合这一差距，探索通用函数逼近下在线AIL的理论基础。

Method: 提出了基于优化的对抗模仿学习框架（OPT-AIL），包含两个组件：奖励学习的在线优化和策略学习的乐观正则化优化。在该框架下发展了无模型OPT-AIL和基于模型OPT-AIL两种具体方法。

Result: 两种变体均实现了多项式级别的专家样本复杂度和交互复杂度，能够学习接近专家的策略；这是首个在通用函数逼近下具有可证明效率的AIL方法。实验表明OPT-AIL在多个挑战性任务上优于先前最先进的深度AIL方法。

Conclusion: OPT-AIL成功搭建了对抗模仿学习理论与实践的桥梁，仅需近似优化两个目标即可实现实际部署，同时保持了理论上的效率保证。

Abstract: Adversarial imitation learning (AIL), a prominent approach in imitation learning, has achieved significant practical success powered by neural network approximation. However, existing theoretical analyses of AIL are primarily confined to simplified settings, such as tabular and linear function approximation, and involve complex algorithmic designs that impede practical implementation. This creates a substantial gap between theory and practice. This paper bridges this gap by exploring the theoretical underpinnings of online AIL with general function approximation. We introduce a novel framework called optimization-based AIL (OPT-AIL), which performs online optimization for reward learning coupled with optimism-regularized optimization for policy learning. Within this framework, we develop two concrete methods: model-free OPT-AIL and model-based OPT-AIL. Our theoretical analysis demonstrates that both variants achieve polynomial expert sample complexity and interaction complexity for learning near-expert policies. To the best of our knowledge, they represent the first provably efficient AIL methods under general function approximation. From a practical standpoint, OPT-AIL requires only the approximate optimization of two objectives, thereby facilitating practical implementation. Empirical studies demonstrate that OPT-AIL outperforms previous state-of-the-art deep AIL methods across several challenging tasks.

</details>
